### Access v1 执行与兼容边界

```markdown
# 可注册范围授权 v1

`definition.schema.json` 是独立权限包；不可向已有 manifest 或远程 actor 随意添加字段。`binding.schema.json` 是主框架受控管理入口创建的实际用户授权。模块安装仅注册定义，不携带真实人员授权。

每个权限、角色模板、办理视图与待办源 ID 以 `module_id + '.'` 开头；能力映射仅绑定本模块。模块命名空间不得相互包含。scope 的所有键值同时匹配可信仓库 facts，每一条授权独立判断，不能跨授权拼接范围。facts 值可为非空字符串，或表达已验证关系的非空字符串数组；浏览器 payload 和模型输出不是可信事实。

角色 `required_scope` 至少有一个范围维度，不接受 `*` 通配授权。已授予角色保留权限快照，并与当前模板权限取交集；升级新增权限需另行授权，收紧立即生效。职责分离 guards 对最终权限统一生效，双角色不能绕过。禁用、撤权、过期、缺失事实和未知动作默认拒绝。业务状态机仍由模块实现，授权允许不等于状态转换成功。

`experiences.required_permissions` 要求全部满足，`step_ids` 为可选的可见步骤集合。无对象的目录只检查有效授权存在；执行仍需 fresh facts。`workitem_sources` 是聚合元数据，不自动运行代码。列表查询必须先落实授权范围再分页、计数和统计。

Python 模块使用 `ctx.access.check(ctx.actor, permission, trusted_facts)`；Node 或远端 Python 通过受认证的适配接口取得等价决定，不能由浏览器直接提交授权 facts。当前角色目录与 invoke actor 无需私加新角色字段，动态角色由独立注册表保存。

`AccessControl(sessions)` 的管理接口仅供已验证 `identity.manage` 的主框架管理入口调用；核心不接受客户端自称管理员。决策方法只读，不另开写审计事务。`AccessControl.from_session(session)` 借用业务事务，禁止管理写入，也不会提交调用者事务。

真实引擎为 Casbin 1.43.0，使用框架内固定的 RBAC domain 模型：每条完整授权作为一个 domain，由框架校验 scope 与 guard 后交给 Casbin。模块不能注入匹配表达式。SQLite 保存定义、角色绑定、到期与撤销、修订及管理审计；引擎可经同一门面替换。Casbin 不替代登录、列表范围过滤、正式状态机、幂等或事务检查。

`authorization_stamp(actor)` 绑定当前策略定义修订及此人的有效授权。其他人的角色分配不会打断当前人的流程；本人撤权、到期、定义变化、禁用再启用均使旧实例授权快照失效。长任务和分布式提交仍需要明确的提交点与再次校验，不能把一次允许决定当永久通行证。

接口主要方法：`register_definition`、`grant`、`revoke`、`set_enabled`、`definitions`、`bindings`、`check`、`check_command`、`has_permission`、`matching_scopes`、`capability_permission(s)`、`experiences`、`module_registered`、`authorization_stamp`。`has_permission` 只供目录；`matching_scopes` 是 OR-of-AND 的完整范围组，查询适配层不能扁平合并。

`command_mappings.command` 用于字符串子命令选择器。workflow v1 的 `input.command` 是结构化事务信封，应为每个具体能力配置无 command 的权限映射；结构化信封不会被当作子命令名称。未知字符串子命令拒绝，不能回退到普通能力权限；同一个能力不能混合无条件映射和字符串子命令映射。

开源资料：[Casbin 域内 RBAC](https://casbin.apache.org/docs/rbac-with-domains/)、[PyCasbin](https://github.com/apache/casbin-pycasbin)。
# Optional cross-module source reads

See [registered source object reads 1.0](source-reads.md) and
`source-read-request.schema.json`. A target ticket stays bound to its own call;
the platform obtains fresh source authorization through registered read-only
object adapters. This does not add long-task delegation or shared storage.
```

### AI services v1 声明、配置与调用边界

```markdown
# AI services 1.0

`registration.ai_services` is optional. Old registrations remain valid. A new
service declares `id`, `title`, `description`, concrete `capability_ids`, and
`supported_sources` containing `platform`, `module`, or both. IDs belong to the
module namespace. Services require `permissions.mode=platform_access`; the
handshake must negotiate `ai-services-1.0` when this array is nonempty. No API key,
provider URL, executable handler, or default provider belongs in registration.

## Configuration ownership

Platform administrators use the following same-origin endpoints. Every write
requires the current session and CSRF token. Keys are secret input only and must
never enter invocations, audit details, registrations, exports, or browser storage.

* `GET /api/v1/ai-services`: `{items:[...]}`. Each item contains the declaration,
  `module_id`, `remote_service_id`, `remote_revision`, `source`,
  `platform_profile_id`, `revision`, `remote_enabled`, and `binding_stale`.
* `PUT /api/v1/ai-services/{module_id}/{service_id}/binding`: the binding request
  schema. `expected_revision` is 0 initially. `source` is explicitly `disabled`,
  `platform`, or `module`. Platform source requires a specific existing, enabled
  model profile ID. Other sources require `platform_profile_id:null`.
* `GET` / `PUT /api/v1/ai-services/{module_id}/{service_id}/module-config`: read
  or save the module-owned encrypted configuration, using the configuration
  schemas. Saving this configuration and selecting its source are separate writes.
  The platform proxies the body to a fixed authenticated module control endpoint;
  it does not persist the module's API key. A blank key retains the current key;
  `clear_api_key:true` clears it. Destination changes require a fresh or cleared key.
* `POST /api/v1/ai-services/{module_id}/{service_id}/test`:
  `{expected_revision}` tests only the currently saved source. Returns route
  metadata plus `result` matching the test result schema. This sends a synthetic
  connection test to a real provider; it does not read business data.

The module implements `GET` / `PUT
/platform/v1/ai/services/{service_id}/config` and `POST
/platform/v1/ai/services/{service_id}/test`. These fixed controls use the existing
platform service Bearer authentication. The config GET returns `{revision,profile}`
with the public fields in the response schema. PUT uses `{expected_revision,profile}`;
test uses `{expected_revision}` and returns the test result directly. The module
checks configuration revisions before and after network calls. Service credentials
authorize configuration transport, never a business operation or an arbitrary user.

## Business execution

The module first authorizes its real business object, then sends the current
`X-Platform-Access` ticket with the scoped `request_id`, `operation`, `permission`,
`facts`, and declared `service_id` to `POST /api/v1/ai/resolve`. The platform verifies
current user, grant stamp, capability, module connection, availability, and binding.
The response is `{service_id,source,binding_revision,module_config_revision,
platform_config_digest}`. Module source uses an integer module config revision
and a null platform digest; platform source uses a null module revision and an
opaque 64-character digest of the current encrypted platform profile. This digest
contains no key or supplier connection configuration.

* For `platform`, the module calls `POST /api/v1/ai/chat` with the same scope,
  `binding_revision`, `module_config_revision:null`, the original
  `platform_config_digest`, and `messages`. The central
  gateway calls exactly the bound profile; its key never reaches the module.
  The response adds `result` with normalized `content`, `tool_calls`, `model`,
  optional `finish_reason`, and token `usage`. This version accepts text messages
  only and does not accept tool definitions.
* For `module`, the module checks its local config revision matches the resolved
  revision, calls only its local profile, checks its revision again, and sends the
  same scope plus both revisions and `platform_config_digest:null` to
  `POST /api/v1/ai/validate` before returning or
  committing the result. The platform re-reads the fixed module config metadata.
  No local business mutation should precede this final validation.

`/validate` returns route metadata after checking current permission, availability,
connection, binding revision, and the selected provider configuration revision or
digest. SDKs validate again after `/chat` before committing a business result. The
platform digest covers both the resolve-to-chat window and the chat-to-final-validate
window. Central chat additionally compares the platform profile before and after
the provider request. No branch silently uses a default profile or falls
back to the other source. Disablement, deletion, revocation, restart-invalidated
tickets, configuration changes, timeouts, and malformed responses fail explicitly.

Standalone modules may explicitly use their encrypted module profile with their
own business authentication. Once configured as platform-connected, missing or
expired platform scope is an error, not permission to switch to standalone mode.

## Bounds and persistence

Declarations contain at most 16 services and 64 capability IDs per service. Callback
resolve and validate bodies are at most 16 KiB; chat is at most 64 KiB, 64 text
messages, and 16,000 characters per message. Module configuration requests and
responses are at most 32 KiB. Provider deadlines are explicitly configured from 1
to 300 seconds, without automatic retry or redirects. Access tickets still have
the existing short lifetime (at most 60 seconds); a long response that outlives its
ticket is rejected, so use a suitable provider timeout and start a fresh business
request rather than extending authority silently.

Binding files contain only references and revisions, using atomic file replacement
and a process-local lock. Module profiles and platform profiles encrypt their own
keys. This supports one configuration-writing process per data directory; distributed
locking, delegated background AI jobs, streaming, supplier-specific tools, and
multi-replica credential distribution are outside this version.
```

### contracts/ai/v1/binding-request.schema.json

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "properties": {
    "expected_revision": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "source": {
      "enum": [
        "disabled",
        "platform",
        "module"
      ]
    },
    "platform_profile_id": {
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,80}$"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "expected_revision",
    "source",
    "platform_profile_id"
  ],
  "allOf": [
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "source": {
            "const": "platform"
          }
        }
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "platform_profile_id": {
            "type": "string",
            "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,80}$"
          }
        }
      },
      "else": {
        "properties": {
          "platform_profile_id": {
            "type": "null"
          }
        }
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

```json
              "disabled",
              "platform",
              "module"
            ]
          },

            "oneOf": [
              {
                "type": "string",
                "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,80}$"

                "type": "null"

          "revision": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1
          },

            "type": "string",
            "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,80}$"
          },
          "remote_revision": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1
          },

            "type": "string",
            "pattern": "^[a-f0-9]{64}$"
          }

          "service_id",
          "source",
          "platform_profile_id",
          "revision",
          "remote_service_id",
          "remote_revision",
          "contract_digest"

          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "source": {
                  "const": "platform"
                }
              }

            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "platform_profile_id": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,80}$"
                }
              }
            },
            "else": {
              "properties": {
                "platform_profile_id": {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              }
            }
          }
        ]
      }
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "version",
    "items"
  ]
}
```

### 独立技术监控v1

```markdown
# 独立技术监控协议1.0

监控中心是独立FastAPI/Uvicorn进程和SQLite，主平台只取得逐服务聚合快照。技术监控不能调用业务能力或授予用户权限。业务运行观察1.0的单次调用事件、当前授权与正式对象引用保留原通道。

| 接口（独立监控地址） | 权限与内容 |
| --- | --- |
| `GET /health` | 服务存活及collector_id，不含目标列表 |
| `PUT /api/v1/monitor/targets` | 平台控制Bearer、owner_id与CAS；同步最多256个已登记目标 |
| `GET /api/v1/monitor/snapshot` | 平台控制Bearer；只返回白名单状态、计数与最新值汇总，无原始报告、地址和令牌 |
| `POST /api/v1/monitor/targets/{id}/probe` | 平台控制Bearer；在独立进程中探测固定健康接口，不执行能力 |
| `GET /api/v1/monitor/targets/{id}/reporting` | 平台控制Bearer；取得本服务独立报告凭据，仅写模块私有配置，不交浏览器/对话 |
| `POST /api/v1/monitor/reports/{id}` | 对应服务的报告Bearer；不能管理目标、读取汇总或冒用其他服务 |

主框架管理接口`GET /api/v1/remote-services/monitoring/summary`只读进程缓存，需要当前平台管理员；普通服务列表同样携带汇总。浏览器刷新不增加底层采集频率。正常主API构造只使用独立客户端；显式注入RemoteServiceManager的单元测试可使用原本地探测驱动，部署没有隐式本地降级。

主平台每5秒拉取一份至多1MiB完整快照，刷新合并；超过20秒未取得有效汇总为过期，连接故障退避到最多30秒。读取失败不把旧快照标成新数据，也不把所有模块判离线。实际业务调用仍执行原授权、修订及HTTP错误检查。

监控中心每15秒目标周期探测固定`base_url/platform/v1/health`；单次整体5秒、4并发、连续失败3次offline，45秒未完成探测stale。目标多且普遍超时时实际周期可能超过15秒，保持有界并发并显示真实过期，不承诺未经压测的规模SLA。服务发布与健康独立；一次接入验收读取健康接口属于正式接入检查，不属于后台监控流量。

报告最多8KiB，每实例每秒1次；每服务最多16实例。实例标识、boot_id与单调序号用于去重/乱序检查；相同重发不更新新鲜时间。每实例仅存最新值，长于1小时的身份清理；退休启动批次也有上限，不是无限历史或审计日志。重启不把旧采样时间更新为现在。

可选指标：归一到主机可见核数的进程cpu_percent（0—100），rss_bytes，inflight_requests，queue_depth，requests_total，errors_total。计数含义由模块注明；默认Node模板统计HTTP请求，队列深度须运行器真实提供。缺失指标不填0。只聚合45秒内新鲜实例；CPU取均值，其他指标求和；仅所有新鲜实例均提供该项时返回数值，否则null。计数是当前新鲜进程累计，不是跨重启历史总量。

探测凭据沿用管理员配置的模块连接Bearer，独立加密保存，固定只访问健康路径。报告凭据每服务独立生成，加密保存、存摘要验证；更换目标地址/探测凭据时旋转报告令牌并清空旧报告。仅发布修订变化可保留同端点报告和健康时间，不伪造新鲜。全批配置事务提交后才更新内存；旧连接的在途响应不能覆盖新修订。

Node`sdk/node/telemetry.mjs`与Python`sdk/python/telemetry.py`直接报告独立地址，最多1在途，不积累无界队列、不立即重试，失败不影响业务。配置独立于AI、业务访问和业务观察票据。未接入报告的旧模块只获得健康，负载保持未知。

Schema由实际Pydantic模型生成，运行`scripts/build_monitor_contract.py --write`及`--check`；不读取真实配置。设计和运行步骤见`docs/独立监控服务设计.md`。本协议为本地候选，不代表部署Celery、Prometheus或OpenTelemetry，不提供多节点高可用或业务事务保证。
```

### Python handler 的仓储事实授权函数（完整函数）

```python
from app.errors import require

def authorize_record(ctx, record):
    # record is loaded by this module's repository, never accepted as input facts.
    facts = {"project_id": record["project_id"], "owner_id": record["owner_id"]}
    decision = ctx.access.check(ctx.actor, "example-access.item.read", facts)
    require(decision["allowed"], "object_scope", "对象不存在或当前范围不可见。", 404)
    return record
```

Python adapter 工厂可使用 AccessControl(context.sessions) 读取中央授权；业务事务内可用 AccessControl.from_session(session)，它不提交事务也不允许管理写入。has_permission 只供目录，正式对象按 check 与业务守卫判断。ctx.access_stamp 由平台设置，ctx.create_resource 自动记录；不要自行替换用户或快照。

服务端桥接请求头为 X-Platform-Access，默认票据有效期 45 秒；票据最多 12288 字节，请求正文最多 16384 字节。票据由主框架发送，示例 JSON 不包含票据、服务 token 或签名密钥。operation 和 request_id 必须对应正在处理的调用，facts 先从服务自有仓库读取。网络错误默认拒绝，不通过旧角色回退。

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "request_id",
    "operation",
    "permission",
    "facts"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "request_id": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,159}$"
    },
    "operation": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,159}$"
    },
    "permission": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,159}$"
    },
    "facts": {
      "type": "object",
      "maxProperties": 32,
      "propertyNames": {
        "pattern": "^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,79}$"
      },
      "additionalProperties": {
        "oneOf": [
          {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 160
          },
          {
            "type": "array",
            "minItems": 1,
            "maxItems": 20,
            "uniqueItems": true,
            "items": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 160
            }
          }
        ]
      }
    }
  }
}
```

### templates/remote-access-client/README.md

````markdown
# Node.js 与其他语言的统一权限判断

主框架对已注册 access definition 的远程调用额外发送 `X-Platform-Access` 短时票据。原 manifest / invoke / actor JSON 不变；旧 actor.roles 不用于表达新模块角色。票据含最终用户授权快照、模块能力与连接修订、请求号和操作，由主框架 HMAC 签名，最长 60 秒；默认进程密钥使重启前票据失效。

1. 模块先验证已有连接 Bearer，按原协议读取 request_id 和 actor；拒绝未经认证的调用。
2. 从模块自己的数据库读取对象事实，例如 project_id、owner_id；不能把浏览器提交的 owner_id 当事实。
3. 用部署时配置的主框架 callback URL 创建 `createAccessClient`，调用其返回函数：

```js
const decide = createAccessClient({
  callbackUrl: process.env.PLATFORM_ACCESS_DECISION_URL,
});
const decision = await decide({
  ticket: request.headers['x-platform-access'],
  requestId: body.request_id,
  operation: 'invoke', // workflow 适配使用对应 objects.read 等真实 operation
  permission: 'example-module.item.read',
  facts: { project_id: row.project_id, owner_id: row.owner_id },
});
if (!decision.allowed) throw new Error('当前无权办理');
```

4. 允许后，模块仍在事务内检查业务状态、预期修订、幂等和职责分离。提交前重新判断，避免把一次允许当永久授权。

票据不传入网页、模型、日志或持久化业务数据；callback URL 仅来自服务端配置，不能采用表单或微服务请求指定的地址。示例禁止重定向，限制请求、响应体积和超时，非 2xx / 无效响应 / 连接失败均拒绝，不能降级为旧角色允许。

票据仅允许其能力已登记的权限，不可切换用户、模块、连接、请求或操作。facts 的可信性来自已受控接入的业务服务，桥接不能防止一个已被攻陷或恶意的权威模块谎报自身对象事实；这与模块直接运行授权代码处于相同信任边界。服务对自己的 facts 负责，主框架对身份、授权和票据有效性负责。

当前默认随机密钥适合单主框架进程。多 worker / 多副本部署须统一配置安全签名密钥或集中决策入口，并处理密钥轮转；否则分发到另一进程的票据会拒绝。此票据是单次调用内的短时决策通道，不是 OAuth 登录、第三方通用访问令牌或长任务委托协议。

`example-service.mjs` 是无需额外 npm 依赖的 Node 只读服务样例，提供 manifest 1.2、health 和 invoke 1.1。部署变量为 `PLATFORM_SERVICE_TOKEN`、`PLATFORM_ACCESS_DECISION_URL` 和可选 `PORT`（默认系统分配端口），仅处理两条合成记录。主框架需注册 example-access 权限包并单独授予项目/本人范围后才能读取。`ACCESS_EXAMPLE_SELF_TEST=1` 仅在自动验收中开启，增加受服务凭据保护的内存诊断；默认关闭。实际集成测试见 `tests/test_remote_access_node_e2e.py`，测试生成临时凭据与随机空闲端口，不占用现有演示服务。
````

### templates/remote-access-client/example-service.mjs

```javascript
/** Minimal read-only remote module showing repository facts + central authorization.
 * Synthetic records only. No credentials or tickets are logged or persisted.
 */
import http from 'node:http';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import { createAccessClient } from './node.mjs';

const token = process.env.PLATFORM_SERVICE_TOKEN;
if (!token || token.length < 24) throw new Error('Configure a private service credential of at least 24 characters');
const decide = createAccessClient({ callbackUrl: process.env.PLATFORM_ACCESS_DECISION_URL });
const moduleId = 'example-access';
const capabilityId = `${moduleId}.item.read`;
const permission = capabilityId;
const ownerId = process.env.ACCESS_EXAMPLE_OWNER_ID || 'synthetic-owner';
const selfTest = process.env.ACCESS_EXAMPLE_SELF_TEST === '1';
const records = new Map([
  ['alpha', { id: 'alpha', project_id: 'P', owner_id: ownerId, title: '合成项目 P 的记录' }],
  ['beta', { id: 'beta', project_id: 'Q', owner_id: ownerId, title: '合成项目 Q 的记录' }],
]);
const manifest = {
  protocol_version: '1.2', module: { id: moduleId, version: '1.0.0', name: 'Node 统一权限示例', description: '从服务自有仓库读取事实，调用主框架作范围授权判断。' },
  capabilities: [{ id: capabilityId, version: '1.0.0', title: '读取合成记录', description: '只有当前项目授权覆盖的记录才返回内容。',
    kind: 'query', roles: ['member'], execution: { mode: 'background', confirmation: false },
    input_schema: { type: 'object', properties: { object_id: { type: 'string', enum: ['alpha', 'beta'] } }, required: ['object_id'], additionalProperties: false },
    output_schema: { type: 'object', properties: { allowed: { type: 'boolean' }, reason: { type: 'string' }, record: { type: ['object', 'null'] }, diagnostic_checks: { type: 'object' } }, required: ['allowed', 'reason', 'record', 'diagnostic_checks'], additionalProperties: false },
    presentation: { page: { renderer: 'json' }, conversation: { renderer: 'json' } } }],
  surfaces: { page_tools: [{ id: 'read', title: '合成记录', description: '范围授权读取。', kind: 'form', capability_ids: [capabilityId] }], conversation: { capability_ids: [capabilityId], starter_prompts: ['读取当前项目的合成记录'] } },
};
const stats = { invokes: 0, callbacks: 0, wrong_operation_denied: 0, denied_reads: 0, rechecks: 0 };
let previousDecision;
function auth(req) {
  const actual = Buffer.from(req.headers.authorization || '');
  const expected = Buffer.from(`Bearer ${token}`);
  return actual.length === expected.length && crypto.timingSafeEqual(actual, expected);
}
function send(res, status, data) { res.writeHead(status, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' }); res.end(JSON.stringify(data)); }
async function checked(args) { stats.callbacks++; return decide(args); }
const server = http.createServer(async (req, res) => {
  try {
    if (!auth(req)) return send(res, 401, { error: { code: 'service_auth', message: '服务认证失败' } });
    if (req.method === 'GET' && req.url === '/platform/v1/manifest') return send(res, 200, manifest);
    if (req.method === 'GET' && req.url === '/platform/v1/health') return send(res, 200, { status: 'ok' });
    if (selfTest && req.method === 'GET' && req.url === '/platform/v1/test-stats') return send(res, 200, stats);
    if (selfTest && req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/platform/v1/test-recheck') {
      stats.rechecks++;
      return send(res, 200, previousDecision ? await checked(previousDecision) : { allowed: false, reason: 'no_previous_call', policy_revision: 0 });
    }
    if (req.method !== 'POST' || req.url !== '/platform/v1/invoke') return send(res, 404, { error: { code: 'not_found', message: '接口不存在' } });
    const chunks = []; let length = 0;
    for await (const chunk of req) { length += chunk.length; if (length > 16384) return send(res, 413, { error: { code: 'request_size', message: '请求过大' } }); chunks.push(chunk); }
    const body = JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8'));
    if (body.protocol_version !== '1.1' || body.capability_id !== capabilityId || body.capability_version !== '1.0.0' ||
        !/^[a-f0-9]{32}$/.test(body.request_id || '') || !body.context?.actor?.id ||
        !body.input || Object.keys(body.input).join() !== 'object_id' || !records.has(body.input.object_id)) {
      return send(res, 422, { error: { code: 'input_contract', message: '请求不符合契约' } });
    }
    stats.invokes++;
    // This repository lookup, never the supplied actor or input.project_id, owns facts.
    const record = records.get(body.input.object_id);
    const args = { ticket: req.headers['x-platform-access'], requestId: body.request_id, operation: 'invoke', permission,
      facts: { project_id: record.project_id, owner_id: record.owner_id } };
    const decision = await checked(args);
    const checks = {};
    if (selfTest) {
      checks.wrong_operation_denied = !(await checked({ ...args, operation: 'objects.read' })).allowed;
      if (checks.wrong_operation_denied) stats.wrong_operation_denied++;
      previousDecision = args; // Synthetic diagnostic only, memory for this short test.
    }
    if (!decision.allowed) stats.denied_reads++;
    send(res, 200, { protocol_version: '1.1', request_id: body.request_id, capability_id: capabilityId, capability_version: '1.0.0',
      summary: decision.allowed ? '已读取授权项目内的合成记录' : '当前授权范围不允许读取此记录',
      data: { allowed: decision.allowed, reason: decision.reason, record: decision.allowed ? record : null, diagnostic_checks: checks } });
  } catch { send(res, 500, { error: { code: 'handler_failed', message: '示例服务处理失败' } }); }
});
server.listen(Number(process.env.PORT || 0), '127.0.0.1', () => console.log(JSON.stringify({ ready: true, port: server.address().port })));
for (const signal of ['SIGTERM', 'SIGINT']) process.on(signal, () => server.close(() => process.exit(0)));
```

### templates/remote-access-client/node.mjs

```javascript
/** Trusted server-side client. Never import this into a browser bundle. */
export function createAccessClient({ callbackUrl, timeoutMs = 4000, fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch }) {
  const endpoint = new URL(callbackUrl);
  if (!['http:', 'https:'].includes(endpoint.protocol) || endpoint.username || endpoint.password ||
      endpoint.hash || endpoint.search || endpoint.pathname !== '/api/v1/access/remote-decisions') {
    throw new Error('Invalid configured access-decision endpoint');
  }
  if (!Number.isInteger(timeoutMs) || timeoutMs < 1 || timeoutMs > 10000) {
    throw new Error('Access decision timeout must be 1-10000 milliseconds');
  }
  // callbackUrl comes exclusively from deployment configuration. No URL, actor,
  // role, token destination or fetch options are accepted from invocation input.
  return async function decideAccess({ ticket, requestId, operation, permission, facts }) {
    const deny = reason => ({ allowed: false, reason, policy_revision: 0 });
    if (typeof ticket !== 'string' || ticket.length === 0 || ticket.length > 12288) return deny('access_ticket_invalid');
    let body;
    try { body = JSON.stringify({ request_id: requestId, operation, permission, facts }); }
    catch { return deny('access_decision_contract'); }
    if (Buffer.byteLength(body, 'utf8') > 16384) return deny('access_decision_size');
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
    try {
      const response = await fetchImpl(endpoint, {
        method: 'POST', redirect: 'error', signal: controller.signal,
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-Platform-Access': ticket }, body,
      });
      if (!response.ok) return deny('access_decision_unavailable');
      // Read bounded bytes rather than response.json() on an unbounded body.
      const reader = response.body.getReader();
      const chunks = []; let size = 0;
      while (true) {
        const { value, done } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        size += value.byteLength;
        if (size > 4096) { await reader.cancel(); return deny('access_decision_response_size'); }
        chunks.push(Buffer.from(value));
      }
      const result = JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8'));
      if (typeof result.allowed !== 'boolean' || typeof result.reason !== 'string' ||
          !Number.isInteger(result.policy_revision) || result.policy_revision < 0 ||
          (result.allowed && (result.reason !== 'allowed' || result.policy_revision < 1))) return deny('access_decision_invalid');
      return { allowed: result.allowed, reason: result.reason, policy_revision: result.policy_revision };
    } catch {
      return deny('access_decision_unavailable');
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  };
}
```

## 简化模块模板与 SDK（自动导出）

优先使用模板生成器与同批模板包；本附录保留完整公开源码，供离线核对。生成、静态检查、安装、授权和业务验收是不同步骤。已有服务按手册适配，不将独立服务改写成参考示例数据库。

### templates/module-kit/README.md

```markdown
# 通用模块模板 v1

两侧完整说明：[模块框架说明](../../docs/模块框架说明.md)、[主框架说明](../../docs/主框架说明.md)。远程模块新增 registration 1.0，提供公开内容声明和双向握手；平台七项检查全部通过后才能发布。新 Node SDK 使用真实 handlers / workflowHandlers 表，已有服务升级时须同步适配。

先阅读 [模块模板使用手册](../../docs/模块模板使用手册.md)。本目录是模板和可携带服务端 SDK；它不会自行安装、启动、授权或修改现有业务。

| 路径 | 用途 |
| --- | --- |
| `blueprints/tool-python` | Python 原生简单工具，纯业务函数与页面/对话共用组件 |
| `blueprints/workflow-node` | Node 24 远程流程参考，项目记录、职责、状态、修订及幂等 |
| `sdk/python/module_kit.py` | 原生工具的授权与输入输出边界；生成时另复制到 backend |
| `sdk/python/remote_access.py` | 已有 Python/FastAPI 独立服务的中央授权客户端 |
| `sdk/node` | Node 固定传输、中央授权与单进程 SQLite 回执参考 |

在平台根目录运行 `python scripts/module_kit.py scaffold --help` 或 `python scripts/module_kit.py check <包目录>`，使用平台 Python 环境。共享前端静态检查还使用宿主 Node 与已安装 TypeScript，只解析 AST，不执行供应商源码。

`workflow-python-remote` 是已有 Python 服务的可检查适配布局，不是第三个新建生成器。原业务库和专业算法可以保留。SDK 是受控、随包发布的小型封装，不能替代服务器仓储事实、业务状态机或正式授权；不动态下载代码，不成为另一套 RBAC。

模板本身是包含占位符的源码，请先生成新包。修改业务文件后，执行生成包自测、当前平台 check，再在隔离平台安装联调。真实平台注册、生产浏览器操作、模型专业效果和外部生产存储分别验收。
```

### templates/module-kit/blueprints/tool-python/README.md

````markdown
# __TITLE__

这是一个 Python 原生简单工具。修改 `backend/business.py` 中的纯函数即可替换文本处理逻辑；同时维护输入输出 Schema 和共享组件。它使用当前平台登录及机构范围授权，不含另一套登录、聊天或权限引擎。

## 先独立运行

生成包已经包含便携 SDK `backend/_module_kit.py`，只依赖 Python 标准库。执行：

```powershell
python tests/self_test.py
```

14 项离线检查覆盖文本边界、机构事实来源、拒绝授权、权限服务故障和结果契约。这里的合成权限服务只验证调用边界；真实 grant、撤权、宿主页面/对话、恢复与分支应在安装后联调，不能从离线检查推断通过。无需启动服务、配置账号或连接外部模型。

## 需要改哪些文件

| 文件 | 开发者工作 |
| --- | --- |
| `backend/business.py` | 纯业务函数；接收字典，返回 `summary` 和 `data`，不读取浏览器身份 |
| `backend/manifest.json` | 原生 Python 能力、Schema、页面和对话表单入口 |
| `backend/access-definition.json` | 模块权限 `.use`、职责 `.user`、必填机构范围 |
| `backend/handlers.py` | 薄平台边界，调用 SDK 并转换为 `CapabilityOutput` |
| `backend/_module_kit.py` | 随包供应的 SDK 1.0.0；升级时整体替换并重跑检查 |
| `frontend/Component.tsx` | 页面与对话共用的业务界面，只调用宿主 SDK |
| `frontend/interaction.json` | 共享组件定义及动作参数绑定 |

文本示例把 CRLF/CR 统一为换行，压缩每行空白并去掉全文首尾空白；字符数按 Unicode 码点计，行数包含保留的空行，片段数按空白切分，不代表中文分词或专业语义分析。最多输入 20000 个字符。

## 安装到平台

1. 将整个 `backend/` 复制到 `extensions/__PACK_NAME__/`，包含 `_module_kit.py` 和 `access-definition.json`。在 `extensions/installed.json` 的 `modules` 数组追加 `__PACK_NAME__`，保留现有条目。
2. 将整个 `frontend/` 复制到 `components/packs/__PACK_NAME__/`，在 `components/installed.json` 的 `packs` 数组追加 `__PACK_NAME__`。
3. 按平台维护流程执行类型检查、前端生产构建、提示词同步检查，再重启受控平台进程。包代码以 `__file__` 解析本地 SDK/业务文件，不依赖启动目录或全局 `sys.path`。不允许普通用户上传任意 Python 后执行。
4. 由具有 `identity.manage` 的管理者，为实际用户授予 `__MODULE_ID__.user`，填写该用户真实 `organization_id`。安装只注册权限定义，不发放授权；`member` 是旧清单兼容声明，不能替代真实 grant。仅有 `system_admin` 也不自动获得工具使用权。
5. 登录获授权的用户，进入 `/#components`，打开 `__MODULE_ID__.tool`；在对话中输入 `/component __MODULE_ID__.tool`。两端使用同一能力 `__MODULE_ID__.analyze`。固定表单能力入口也经过同一后台授权。

检查安装复制是否完整：`backend/` 的四个业务文件及 `_module_kit.py` 必须在同一扩展目录；组件目录必须保留文件名 `Component.tsx`、`interaction.json`。注册后先核对清单中能力、模块 ID、组件动作版本和 access 映射一致，再做真实账号验证。具体 CLI 与完整命令见平台的《模块模板使用手册》。

## 权限、数据与恢复边界

SDK 始终以平台注入的 `ctx.actor.organization_id` 作为本工具的可信范围事实，机构缺失、访问服务缺失、检查失败或拒绝均停止执行。输入不能传入 `organization_id`、角色或权限来替换身份。以后增加持久业务对象时，必须从业务仓库读取对象机构等事实并逐次授权，不能继续用操作者机构替代对象归属。

| 环节 | 条件 |
| --- | --- |
| 发现 / 打开 | 登录、模块和能力可用，平台目录判断存在 `.use` 授权 |
| 读取既有结果 / 恢复 | 本人实例及当前平台授权快照仍有效 |
| 执行动作 | 以上条件，加本人机构与完整 grant 范围匹配；输入 Schema 与业务边界都通过 |
| 分支 | 平台复制本人已保存的组件状态；不会自动再次执行文本处理 |

没有正式业务对象、状态流转或待办目录，因而 access 的 `experiences` 与 `workitem_sources` 为空；简单工具不冒充“我的业务”流程。CSRF、修订、幂等、启停和私人实例由平台宿主处理。游客、通知、SSO、委派、长任务与外部副作用均未实现。

本地 manifest 使用平台原生结构；manifest 1.2 / invoke 1.1 是远程 HTTP 接入边界，不能把两者混填。需要远程 Node 流程时选择对应模板。
````

### templates/module-kit/blueprints/tool-python/backend/business.py

```python
"""Replace this pure business function; it has no platform or web dependency."""

MAX_TEXT_LENGTH = 20_000


def analyze(payload: dict) -> dict:
    if not isinstance(payload, dict) or set(payload) != {"text"}:
        raise ValueError("expected exactly the text field")
    text = payload["text"]
    if not isinstance(text, str) or not 1 <= len(text) <= MAX_TEXT_LENGTH or not text.strip():
        raise ValueError("text outside supported bounds")
    # Preserve line boundaries, remove surrounding whitespace and collapse
    # whitespace within a line. Counts are Unicode code points, not graphemes.
    text = text.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\r", "\n")
    normalized = "\n".join(" ".join(line.split()) for line in text.split("\n")).strip()
    data = {
        "normalized_text": normalized,
        "character_count": len(normalized),
        "line_count": len(normalized.split("\n")),
        "segment_count": len(normalized.split()),
    }
    return {
        "summary": f"已整理文本：{data['character_count']} 个字符，{data['line_count']} 行。",
        "data": data,
    }
```

### templates/module-kit/blueprints/tool-python/backend/handlers.py

```python
"""Native platform boundary. Both local dependencies travel inside this package."""

from pathlib import Path
from runpy import run_path

from app.contracts import CapabilityOutput
from app.errors import PlatformError

_base = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
_sdk = run_path(str(_base / "_module_kit.py"))
_business = run_path(str(_base / "business.py"))


def analyze(ctx, payload):
    try:
        result = _sdk["run_tool"](
            ctx, permission="__MODULE_ID__.use", payload=payload, handler=_business["analyze"]
        )
    except _sdk["KitError"] as error:
        raise PlatformError(error.code, error.message, error.status) from None
    return CapabilityOutput(summary=result["summary"], data=result["data"])
```

### templates/module-kit/blueprints/tool-python/frontend/Component.tsx

```tsx
import type { InteractionProps } from "@platform/interaction-sdk";

/** Shared page/conversation view; the host owns authentication and persistence. */
export default function TextTool({
  state,
  result,
  busy,
  actions,
  updateState,
  invokeAction,
}: InteractionProps) {
  const text = String(state.text ?? "");
  const canRun = actions.some(
    (action) => action.id === "analyze" && action.available,
  );
  const output = result?.data;
  return (
    <section aria-label="文本整理工具">
      <label className="field">
        待整理文本
        <textarea
          aria-label="待整理文本"
          rows={7}
          maxLength={20000}
          value={text}
          disabled={busy}
          onChange={(event) =>
            updateState({ ...state, text: event.target.value })
          }
        />
      </label>
      <p className="subtle">
        最多 20000 个字符。统一换行、整理空白，并保留段落。
      </p>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="button primary"
        disabled={busy || !canRun || !text.trim()}
        onClick={() => void invokeAction("analyze")}
      >
        {busy ? "正在处理…" : "整理与统计"}
      </button>
      {!canRun && <p role="status">当前没有工具使用权限，或模块暂不可用。</p>}
      {output && (
        <div aria-label="文本整理结果" aria-live="polite">
          <p>
            {Number(output.character_count)} 个字符 ·{" "}
            {Number(output.line_count)} 行 · {Number(output.segment_count)}{" "}
            个空白分隔片段
          </p>
          <pre style={{ whiteSpace: "pre-wrap", overflowWrap: "anywhere" }}>
            {String(output.normalized_text ?? "")}
          </pre>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```

### templates/module-kit/blueprints/tool-python/tests/self_test.py

```python
"""Offline business/SDK checks; no account, database, server or dependency needed.

Run from any working directory: python <generated-package>/tests/self_test.py
The decision fake checks the SDK boundary, not the platform's policy engine.
The framework integration suite separately verifies real grants and revocation.
"""
from pathlib import Path
from runpy import run_path
from types import SimpleNamespace
import json
import unittest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
SDK = run_path(str(ROOT / "backend" / "_module_kit.py"))
BUSINESS = run_path(str(ROOT / "backend" / "business.py"))
MANIFEST = json.loads((ROOT / "backend" / "manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
PERMISSION = MANIFEST["id"] + ".use"


class DecisionFake:
    def __init__(self, allowed=True, organization_id="org-a"):
        self.allowed, self.organization_id, self.calls = allowed, organization_id, []

    def check(self, actor, permission, facts):
        self.calls.append((actor.copy(), permission, facts.copy()))
        return {"allowed": self.allowed if facts == {"organization_id": self.organization_id} else False}


def context(access=None, **actor_fields):
    return SimpleNamespace(actor={"id": "synthetic-user", "organization_id": "org-a", "roles": ["member"], **actor_fields},
                           access=DecisionFake() if access is None else access)


def invoke(ctx, payload=None, handler=None):
    return SDK["run_tool"](ctx, permission=PERMISSION, payload={"text": "example"} if payload is None else payload,
                           handler=BUSINESS["analyze"] if handler is None else handler)


class ToolTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def assert_error(self, code, thunk):
        with self.assertRaises(SDK["KitError"]) as caught:
            thunk()
        self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, code)
        return caught.exception

    def test_normalizes_line_endings_and_reports_exact_counts(self):
        result = invoke(context(), {"text": " 甲\t乙 \r\n\r\n hello   world \r final "})
        self.assertEqual(result["data"], {"normalized_text": "甲 乙\n\nhello world\nfinal", "character_count": 22,
                                          "line_count": 4, "segment_count": 5})
        self.assertIn("22", result["summary"])

    def test_multilingual_count_is_codepoints_not_bytes(self):
        data = invoke(context(), {"text": "中文 🙂"})["data"]
        self.assertEqual(data["character_count"], 4)
        self.assertEqual(data["segment_count"], 2)

    def test_business_is_pure_and_input_is_unchanged(self):
        payload = {"text": " a  b "}
        result = BUSINESS["analyze"](payload)
        self.assertEqual(payload, {"text": " a  b "})
        self.assertEqual(result, BUSINESS["analyze"](payload))

    def test_maximum_input_and_output_fit_declared_schema(self):
        data = invoke(context(), {"text": "字" * 20000})["data"]
        self.assertEqual(data["character_count"], 20000)
        schema = MANIFEST["capabilities"][0]["output_schema"]
        self.assertEqual(set(data), set(schema["required"]))
        for name, value in data.items():
            field = schema["properties"][name]
            self.assertTrue(type(value) is int if field["type"] == "integer" else isinstance(value, str))
            self.assertLessEqual(value if type(value) is int else len(value), field.get("maximum", field.get("maxLength")))

    def test_invalid_inputs_do_not_pass_business_boundary(self):
        for payload in ({}, {"text": ""}, {"text": " \n "}, {"text": None}, {"text": 42}, {"text": "x" * 20001}, {"text": "x", "organization_id": "org-b"}):
            with self.subTest(payload_type=str(type(payload.get("text")))):
                self.assert_error("tool_input_invalid", lambda: invoke(context(), payload))

    def test_scope_facts_come_only_from_authenticated_actor(self):
        ctx = context()
        invoke(ctx)
        self.assertEqual(ctx.access.calls[0][1:], (PERMISSION, {"organization_id": "org-a"}))

    def test_other_organization_is_denied_before_business_runs(self):
        calls = []
        self.assert_error("tool_forbidden", lambda: invoke(context(DecisionFake(organization_id="org-b")), handler=lambda value: calls.append(value)))
        self.assertEqual(calls, [])

    def test_no_global_admin_or_legacy_role_fallback(self):
        self.assert_error("tool_forbidden", lambda: invoke(context(DecisionFake(False), roles=["system_admin", "admin", "member"])))

    def test_missing_identity_or_organization_fails_closed(self):
        for fields in ({"id": ""}, {"enabled": False}, {"organization_id": None}, {"organization_id": " "}, {"organization_id": "*"}):
            ctx = context(**fields)
            with self.subTest(fields=fields):
                with self.assertRaises(SDK["KitError"]):
                    invoke(ctx)
                self.assertEqual(ctx.access.calls, [])

    def test_missing_or_failed_access_service_fails_closed(self):
        self.assert_error("tool_access_unavailable", lambda: invoke(SimpleNamespace(actor=context().actor, access=None)))

        class Unavailable:
            def check(self, *args):
                raise RuntimeError("private service detail")

        error = self.assert_error("tool_access_unavailable", lambda: invoke(context(Unavailable())))
        self.assertNotIn("private", error.message)

    def test_malformed_decision_cannot_be_truthy_allow(self):
        for value in (1, "true", None, [], {}):
            self.assert_error("tool_forbidden", lambda: invoke(context(DecisionFake(value))))

    def test_json_input_and_size_guards(self):
        for payload in ([], {"text": float("nan")}, {"text": "\ud800"}):
            self.assert_error("tool_input_invalid", lambda: invoke(context(), payload))
        self.assert_error("tool_input_too_large", lambda: invoke(context(), {"text": "a" * 100001}))

    def test_result_contract_and_json_guards(self):
        for result in (None, {}, {"summary": "", "data": {}}, {"summary": "ok", "data": []},
                       {"summary": "ok", "data": {}, "token": "not permitted"},
                       {"summary": "ok", "data": {"number": float("inf")}}):
            self.assert_error("tool_result_invalid", lambda: invoke(context(), handler=lambda payload: result))
        self.assert_error("tool_result_too_large", lambda: invoke(context(), handler=lambda payload: {"summary": "ok", "data": {"text": "x" * 256001}}))

    def test_grant_contract_matches_installed_capability(self):
        definition = json.loads((ROOT / "backend" / "access-definition.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(definition["module_id"], MANIFEST["id"])
        self.assertEqual(definition["command_mappings"], [{"capability_id": MANIFEST["id"] + ".analyze", "permission": PERMISSION}])
        self.assertEqual(definition["role_templates"][0]["required_scope"], ["organization_id"])
        self.assertEqual(definition["experiences"], [])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)
```

### templates/module-kit/blueprints/workflow-node/README.md

````markdown
# __TITLE__

这个参考包演示一条可持久化的项目业务：创建草稿 → 作者编辑、提交 → 另一位审核人审核通过。同一组 React 步骤同时用于平台页面和对话。安装后由平台提供登录、授权、个人草稿、继续办理和聊天分支；服务端只持有正式记录。

## 开发者主要改哪里

| 文件 | 修改内容 |
| --- | --- |
| `backend/business.mjs` | 项目仓储、对象字段、正式状态和业务动作 |
| `backend/manifest.json` | 每项能力的输入、输出和说明 |
| `backend/access-definition.json` | 权限、职责模板、范围和办理入口 |
| `frontend/flow.json` | 共享步骤、动作、页面组合及正式状态机 |
| `frontend/steps.tsx` | 页面与对话复用的业务组件 |
| `sdk/node/` | 可移植的服务协议和事务封装，常规业务开发无需复制修改 |

模块 ID 是 `__MODULE_ID__`，前端安装包名是 `__PACK_NAME__`。正式项目应使用自己的唯一标识，不与其他模块命名空间包含或重叠。

## 可选 AI 服务

`backend/registration.json` 的 `ai_services: []` 是模型服务正式声明位置；本模板默认不调用模型。实际需要 AI 时增加服务条目（能力绑定和支持来源），用随包 `sdk/node/ai-service.mjs` 创建 `AIServiceManager`，在 `backend/server.mjs` 显式传 `aiManager` 给 `createModuleServer` 并从真实业务 handler 调用。服务端私有配置放在 `SERVICE_DATA_DIR/ai`，不能写入声明、共享界面或业务回执。接入平台后由管理员交互选择平台 API 或模块 API，每次双向验证当前票据、来源绑定及配置修订，不作自动回退。字段与接线见生成包 `examples/ai-services/README.md`。未适配和验收之前，不把这个可选声明位置称为模块已具备 AI 业务。

## 职责和范围

| 职责 | 允许动作 | 范围与条件 | 页面和对话 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 提交者 `__MODULE_ID__.author` | 读取、创建、编辑、提交 | 每份 grant 同时包含 project_id 和 organization_id；只能编辑、提交本人草稿 | 选择、填写、审核结果 |
| 审核人 `__MODULE_ID__.reviewer` | 读取、审核 | 同样的完整项目/机构范围；只审核别人提交的记录 | 选择、只读内容、审核 |

本参考明确允许同一授权项目和机构内协作读取记录内容。目录先逐对象中央授权，再分页；不会返回范围外记录数量。提交者拥有其他项目的审核职责时，仍按本次对象事实核验，不能把不同授权的范围拼接。`allowed_actions` 只是状态与作者关系形成的候选，宿主再过滤能力权限；实际执行始终重新中央授权、检查正式状态和 expected_revision。

`frontend/flow.json` 中的 `state_machine` 声明草稿、待审核、已通过及动作之间的对应关系。`backend/business.mjs` 读取这同一声明执行正式状态转移，固定页面和对话也读取同一状态名称；提交后不再允许编辑。框架调试器绘制的是正式状态，用户切换 `pick/edit/review` 导航步骤不会改变业务状态。状态机声明不执行任意表达式，`guard_description` 仅说明条件，实际权限、作者关系、修订和意见校验仍由业务后端执行。增加新动作时同时修改能力、共享步骤、状态机及后端规则并测试，不能只在图上画一条线。

项目与机构关系从受控 `MODULE_PROJECTS_FILE` 加载；浏览器只选择 project_id，不能指定可信 organization_id。已有系统应把此查询改为原项目仓储。修改项目配置后旧对象机构不一致会拒绝读取，不能靠重命名配置把旧数据移交其他机构。

## 独立验证与启动

要求 Node.js 24；无需 npm install。先确认使用正确版本：

```powershell
node --version
node --test tests/workflow.test.mjs
```

测试使用临时数据库、随机端口和合成中央决策 HTTP 服务，结束会清理自己的临时目录。它实际验证 HTTP、SQLite、幂等、重启、并发、权限失败和分页，但不代表已经在真实平台注册或通过生产浏览器验证。

部署时在受保护位置准备项目 JSON，可参考 `backend/projects.example.json` 的结构；合成项目编号不能冒充真实项目。由管理员设置服务进程环境：

```powershell
$env:SERVICE_DATA_DIR = 'D:\private-module-data\__PACK_NAME__'
$env:MODULE_PROJECTS_FILE = 'D:\private-module-config\__PACK_NAME__-projects.json'
$env:PLATFORM_ACCESS_DECISION_URL = 'http://127.0.0.1:8030/api/v1/access/remote-decisions'
# PLATFORM_SERVICE_TOKEN 通过私有部署配置注入，至少 24 字符；不要写进源码或共享脚本。
$env:PORT = '8040'
node backend/server.mjs
```

示例地址和路径须改为实际部署环境。服务只监听回环地址；跨机器部署通过受控网关、TLS 与网络访问策略另行配置。启动只输出就绪端口，不输出凭据。停止使用 Ctrl+C；数据保存在 `records.sqlite`，再次使用同一目录启动会恢复对象和回执。参考包最多 1000 条记录、100 个已配置项目；大规模服务应实现仓储范围查询，不直接增加扫描量。

所有 `/platform/v1/health`、`manifest`、`invoke` 和 `workflow/*` 接口要求私有服务 Bearer。业务还要求平台本次调用的短时 `X-Platform-Access` 票据；单有服务 Bearer 或伪造 actor 不构成业务授权。中央连接失败、过期票据和撤权都拒绝，禁止退回固定角色放行。

## 安装到平台

1. 服务进程启动后，由平台管理员录入受控地址与私有凭据，发现 manifest，保持禁用。
2. 把 `frontend/` 的文件复制到平台 `flows/packs/__PACK_NAME__/`，在 `flows/installed.json` 的 `packs` 追加 `__PACK_NAME__`，保留其他条目。使用宿主 React 和 `@platform/workflow-sdk`，不在此目录另装 React。
3. 按平台规程复核提示词、重建快照、执行一致性检查，类型检查和生产构建，再重启受控平台载入 flow。仅复制文件不会热加载流程；权限附件注册前应确认该流程已被宿主载入。不要用开发服务器成功替代生产验收。
4. 管理员审核并注册 `backend/access-definition.json`。安装定义不自动授予任何用户业务权限。
5. 核验 flow 和能力绑定后执行平台七项接入检查，包含服务身份、接口、模块主动确认及已安装宿主。全部通过后在验收有效期内发布；失败时按报告修复并重新验收。具有 `identity.manage` 的管理者给合成测试用户分别授予提交者、审核人，scope 中同时填入项目和机构的准确值，无通配符。
6. 从“我的业务”或 `/flow __MODULE_ID__.workflow` 分别在页面与对话办理。验证 A 创建、保存和提交，B 用自己的流程审核，A 刷新看到结果；再验证撤权、重新登录、同一实例继续、分支不重放正式写入。

服务被禁用或中央授权变化时由现有平台拒绝旧实例；服务不自建个人会话。恢复同一个私人草稿须使用原 instance_id，跨人交接只共享正式对象，不复制私有对话。

## 边界与渐进适配

已实现：有限项目记录、原子 SQLite 状态/修订/幂等回执、中央范围决策、对象读写、授权后目录分页、共享前端组件。SDK 的事务与回执不覆盖外部写操作；跨服务器事务需要另行设计。

未实现：文件、长任务、明细分页、模型调用、通知、游客、SSO、长期委派和通用跨角色事件调度。未实现的 workflow 方法返回 501。流程导航、聊天分支与“我的业务”由现有主框架提供，不能在此包中伪造其状态。

已有独立服务可先保留原 API 和数据库：将既有对象映射为 projection，将动作包装为 invoke，将项目/机构/作者事实从原仓储读取，再接中央授权。保留原事务，把请求编号和正式写入原子保存。只把业务组件迁入 `frontend/`，不重写登录、聊天、原计算逻辑或全部页面。

交付前记录真实平台版本、提示词指纹和验收证据；独立测试通过与真实宿主联调通过应分开记录。
# 注册标准与握手

backend/registration.json 声明本服务公开的对象、业务步骤、权限及共享流程。SDK 在启动时检查实际函数表；收到平台握手后主动比较完整内容和所需协议，再确认。平台发现与离线 check 均不能代替接入验收，七项通过后才能发布；用户授权另行分配。修改能力、权限或流程时同步 registration.json 和独立测试。
````

### templates/module-kit/blueprints/workflow-node/backend/business.mjs

```javascript
/** Business changes belong here. SDK owns transport/transactions; the platform owns identity and grants. */
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { Store } from "../sdk/node/store.mjs";
import { compile, requireThat, fail } from "../sdk/node/runtime.mjs";

export const MODULE_ID = "__MODULE_ID__";
export const OBJECT_TYPE = `${MODULE_ID}.record`;
// The same installed declaration drives visualization and authoritative state
// changes. It is static trusted module code, never a URL or a user expression.
export const stateMachine = JSON.parse(
  readFileSync(new URL("../frontend/flow.json", import.meta.url), "utf8"),
).flows[0].state_machine;
function transitionTarget(state, action) {
  const choices = stateMachine.transitions.filter(
    (item) => item.from === state && item.action_id === action,
  );
  requireThat(
    choices.length === 1,
    "state_conflict",
    "当前状态不允许此操作。",
    409,
  );
  return choices[0].to;
}
const identity = {
  type: "string",
  pattern: "^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,99}$",
};
const projectCheck = compile({
  type: "array",
  minItems: 1,
  maxItems: 100,
  items: {
    type: "object",
    required: ["project_id", "organization_id", "title"],
    properties: {
      project_id: identity,
      organization_id: identity,
      title: { type: "string", minLength: 1, maxLength: 160 },
    },
    additionalProperties: false,
  },
});
const listCheck = compile({
  type: "object",
  additionalProperties: false,
  properties: {
    object_type: { type: "string", const: OBJECT_TYPE },
    cursor: { type: "string", minLength: 1, maxLength: 2048 },
    limit: { type: "integer", minimum: 1, maximum: 50 },
  },
});
const readCheck = compile({
  type: "object",
  additionalProperties: false,
  required: ["object_ref"],
  properties: {
    object_ref: {
      type: "object",
      additionalProperties: false,
      required: ["object_type", "object_id"],
      properties: {
        object_type: { type: "string", const: OBJECT_TYPE },
        object_id: {
          type: "string",
          pattern: "^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,99}$",
        },
      },
    },
  },
});
const ordinaryDenials = new Set([
  "grant_scope_mismatch",
  "actor_relation_mismatch",
  "separation_of_duties",
  "fact_condition_failed",
]);
export function createBusiness({ filename, projects, decide, ai = null }) {
  // Optional injected AIServiceManager. The neutral workflow below has no AI
  // action; a real module explicitly declares and calls its own AI capability.
  requireThat(
    projectCheck(projects) &&
      new Set(projects.map((item) => item.project_id)).size === projects.length,
    "project_config",
    "项目仓库配置无效或包含重复编号。",
    500,
  );
  // This trusted deployment configuration is the project repository for the reference package.
  // An existing service replaces this lookup with its own repository, never input.organization_id.
  const repository = new Map(
    projects.map((item) => [item.project_id, Object.freeze({ ...item })]),
  );
  const store = new Store(filename);
  store.db.exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS records(
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, project_id TEXT NOT NULL, organization_id TEXT NOT NULL, author_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    title TEXT NOT NULL, body TEXT NOT NULL, state TEXT NOT NULL, revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
    reviewer_id TEXT, review_note TEXT, updated_at TEXT NOT NULL);`);
  function facts(record) {
    return {
      project_id: record.project_id,
      organization_id: record.organization_id,
      author_id: record.author_id,
    };
  }
  function load(id) {
    const record = store.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM records WHERE id=?").get(id);
    requireThat(
      record &&
        repository.get(record.project_id)?.organization_id ===
          record.organization_id,
      "object_scope",
      "记录不存在或项目配置已变化。",
      404,
    );
    return record;
  }
  async function decision(
    body,
    ticket,
    operation,
    permission,
    trustedFacts,
    optional = false,
  ) {
    const result = await decide({
      ticket,
      requestId: body.request_id,
      operation,
      permission: `${MODULE_ID}.${permission}`,
      facts: trustedFacts,
    });
    requireThat(
      result &&
        typeof result.allowed === "boolean" &&
        Number.isSafeInteger(result.policy_revision),
      "access_denied",
      "中央授权不可用。",
      403,
    );
    if (result.allowed) return result;
    if (
      optional &&
      ordinaryDenials.has(result.reason) &&
      result.policy_revision > 0
    )
      return result;
    throw fail("access_denied", "当前授权不允许此操作，请重新核验授权。", 403);
  }
  function projection(record, actor, summary = false) {
    const own = record.author_id === actor.id;
    const actions = stateMachine.transitions
      .filter(
        (item) =>
          item.from === record.state &&
          (item.action_id === "approve" ? !own : own),
      )
      .map((item) => item.action_id);
    return {
      object_ref: { object_type: OBJECT_TYPE, object_id: record.id },
      revision: record.revision,
      state: record.state,
      data: summary
        ? {
            title: record.title,
            project_id: record.project_id,
            organization_id: record.organization_id,
          }
        : {
            title: record.title,
            body: record.body,
            project_id: record.project_id,
            organization_id: record.organization_id,
            author_id: record.author_id,
            reviewer_id: record.reviewer_id,
            review_note: record.review_note,
          },
      allowed_actions: actions,
      jobs: [],
      updated_at: record.updated_at,
    };
  }
  async function list(body, ticket, operation, payload) {
    requireThat(listCheck(payload), "input_contract", "目录参数无效。");
    let policyRevision;
    const accepted = [];
    // Even an empty database checks a live ticket against configured trusted project facts.
    for (const project of repository.values()) {
      const result = await decision(
        body,
        ticket,
        operation,
        "read",
        {
          project_id: project.project_id,
          organization_id: project.organization_id,
        },
        true,
      );
      requireThat(
        policyRevision === undefined ||
          policyRevision === result.policy_revision,
        "access_denied",
        "读取期间权限定义已变化。",
        403,
      );
      policyRevision = result.policy_revision;
    }
    // Small-reference policy: at most 1000 records. Replace this with repository scope queries at scale.
    // Crucially, authorization happens before pagination and no unauthorized totals are returned.
    for (const record of store.db
      .prepare("SELECT * FROM records ORDER BY id")
      .all()) {
      if (
        repository.get(record.project_id)?.organization_id !==
        record.organization_id
      )
        continue;
      const result = await decision(
        body,
        ticket,
        operation,
        "read",
        facts(record),
        true,
      );
      requireThat(
        policyRevision === result.policy_revision,
        "access_denied",
        "读取期间权限定义已变化。",
        403,
      );
      if (result.allowed) accepted.push(record);
    }
    const binding = {
      module: MODULE_ID,
      actor: body.context.actor,
      policyRevision,
      snapshot: store.fingerprint(
        accepted.map((record) => [record.id, record.revision]),
      ),
    };
    const offset = store.offset(payload.cursor, binding),
      limit = payload.limit || 20;
    requireThat(
      offset <= accepted.length,
      "cursor_invalid",
      "分页游标超出目录范围。",
    );
    return {
      objects: accepted
        .slice(offset, offset + limit)
        .map((record) => projection(record, body.context.actor, true)),
      next_cursor:
        offset + limit < accepted.length
          ? store.cursor(binding, offset + limit)
          : null,
    };
  }
  async function invoke({ body, ticket }) {
    return store.transaction(async () => {
      const action = body.capability_id.slice(MODULE_ID.length + 1);
      requireThat(
        body.capability_id === `${MODULE_ID}.${action}` &&
          ["create", "edit", "submit", "approve", "read", "list"].includes(
            action,
          ),
        "capability_unknown",
        "未声明的能力。",
      );
      if (action === "list")
        return {
          summary: "已读取授权范围内的项目记录。",
          data: await list(body, ticket, "invoke", body.input),
        };
      if (action === "read") {
        const record = load(body.input.object_id);
        await decision(body, ticket, "invoke", "read", facts(record));
        return {
          summary: "已读取当前记录。",
          data: { projection: projection(record, body.context.actor) },
        };
      }
      const cached = store.receipt(body);
      const record = cached
        ? load(cached.objectId)
        : action === "create"
          ? null
          : load(body.input.object_id);
      const project =
        action === "create"
          ? repository.get(body.input.command.project_id)
          : null;
      requireThat(
        action !== "create" || project,
        "project_unknown",
        "所选项目不在服务端项目仓库。",
      );
      const trustedFacts = record
        ? facts(record)
        : { ...project, author_id: body.context.actor.id };
      delete trustedFacts.title;
      await decision(body, ticket, "invoke", action, trustedFacts);
      if (cached) return cached.response; // Reauthorize every retry, including after restart; never replay a write.
      const stamp = new Date().toISOString();
      let id = record?.id;
      if (action === "create") {
        requireThat(
          store.db.prepare("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM records").get().n < 1000,
          "capacity_limit",
          "参考包最多保存 1000 条记录，请扩展仓储后继续。",
          413,
        );
        requireThat(
          body.input.command.title.trim().length > 0,
          "title_required",
          "请输入标题。",
        );
        id = randomUUID();
        store.db
          .prepare("INSERT INTO records VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)")
          .run(
            id,
            project.project_id,
            project.organization_id,
            body.context.actor.id,
            body.input.command.title,
            body.input.command.body,
            stateMachine.initial,
            1,
            null,
            null,
            stamp,
          );
      } else {
        requireThat(
          record.revision === body.input.expected_revision,
          "revision_conflict",
          "记录已更新，请重新读取。",
          409,
        );
        const own = record.author_id === body.context.actor.id;
        requireThat(
          action === "approve" ? !own : own,
          "separation_of_duties",
          "当前办理人与记录作者的关系不符合要求。",
          403,
        );
        const nextState = transitionTarget(record.state, action);
        if (action === "edit")
          requireThat(
            body.input.command.title.trim().length > 0,
            "title_required",
            "请输入标题。",
          );
        if (action === "approve")
          requireThat(
            body.input.command.note.trim().length > 0,
            "note_required",
            "请输入审核意见。",
          );
        const changed = store.db
          .prepare(
            "UPDATE records SET title=?,body=?,state=?,revision=revision+1,reviewer_id=?,review_note=?,updated_at=? WHERE id=? AND revision=? AND state=?",
          )
          .run(
            action === "edit" ? body.input.command.title : record.title,
            action === "edit" ? body.input.command.body : record.body,
            nextState,
            action === "approve" ? body.context.actor.id : null,
            action === "approve" ? body.input.command.note : null,
            stamp,
            id,
            body.input.expected_revision,
            record.state,
          );
        requireThat(
          changed.changes === 1,
          "revision_conflict",
          "另一请求已修改记录。",
          409,
        );
      }
      // A final fresh central check precedes the atomic commit. Denial rolls back both object and receipt.
      await decision(body, ticket, "invoke", action, trustedFacts);
      const response = {
        summary: {
          create: "已创建草稿。",
          edit: "已保存记录。",
          submit: "已提交，等待其他审核人办理。",
          approve: "已审核通过。",
        }[action],
        data: { projection: projection(load(id), body.context.actor) },
      };
      store.saveReceipt(body, id, response);
      return response;
    });
  }
  async function workflow({ body, operation, ticket }) {
    return store.transaction(async () => {
      if (operation === "objects.list")
        return list(body, ticket, operation, body.input);
      requireThat(
        operation === "objects.read",
        "workflow_unsupported",
        "此参考包未实现明细分页、文件或长任务适配。",
        501,
      );
      requireThat(
        readCheck(body.input),
        "input_contract",
        "对象引用不符合契约。",
      );
      const record = load(body.input.object_ref.object_id);
      await decision(body, ticket, operation, "read", facts(record));
      return { projection: projection(record, body.context.actor) };
    });
  }
  // The transport dispatches through these exact maps and validates complete
  // manifest coverage before registration; the handshake never calls them.
  const handlers = Object.fromEntries(
    ["create", "edit", "submit", "approve", "read", "list"].map((action) => [
      `${MODULE_ID}.${action}`,
      invoke,
    ]),
  );
  const workflowHandlers = {
    "objects.list": workflow,
    "objects.read": workflow,
  };
  return {
    invoke,
    workflow,
    handlers,
    workflowHandlers,
    close: () => store.close(),
  };
}
```

### templates/module-kit/blueprints/workflow-node/backend/server.mjs

```javascript
import { readFileSync, mkdirSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from "node:url";
import { createModuleServer, requireThat } from "../sdk/node/runtime.mjs";
import { createAccessClient } from "../sdk/node/access-client.mjs";
import { createObservationClient } from "../sdk/node/observation.mjs";
import { AIServiceManager } from "../sdk/node/ai-service.mjs";
import { createTelemetryReporter } from "../sdk/node/telemetry.mjs";
import { createBusiness } from "./business.mjs";

const root = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
export const manifest = JSON.parse(
  readFileSync(path.join(root, "manifest.json"), "utf8"),
);
export const registration = JSON.parse(
  readFileSync(path.join(root, "registration.json"), "utf8"),
);
export const moduleGraph = JSON.parse(
  readFileSync(path.join(root, "module-graph.json"), "utf8"),
);
export function createService({
  token,
  callbackUrl,
  filename,
  projects,
  fetchImpl,
  observationUrl,
  aiManager = null,
  telemetryConfig = null,
}) {
  const business = createBusiness({
    filename,
    projects,
    decide: createAccessClient({ callbackUrl, fetchImpl }),
    ai: aiManager,
  });
  try {
    const observer = createObservationClient({
      callbackUrl:
        observationUrl ||
        new URL("/api/v1/runtime-observations/events", callbackUrl).href,
      graph: moduleGraph,
      fetchImpl,
    });
    // Observe only the projection returned after the business transaction. The
    // module names its own formal machine; the transport does not infer one.
    const handlers = Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(business.handlers).map(([id, handler]) => [
        id,
        async (context) => {
          const result = await handler(context);
          if (result.data.projection)
            context.observe?.state(
              "__MODULE_ID__.record-lifecycle",
              result.data.projection,
            );
          return result;
        },
      ]),
    );
    const server = createModuleServer({
      manifest,
      registration,
      token,
      handlers,
      workflowHandlers: business.workflowHandlers,
      graph: moduleGraph,
      observationClient: observer,
      aiManager,
    });
    const counters = {inflight_requests:0, requests_total:0, errors_total:0};
    const telemetry = telemetryConfig ? createTelemetryReporter({
      ...telemetryConfig, metrics:()=>({...counters}),
    }) : null;
    if (telemetry) {
      server.on('request', (_request, response)=>{
        counters.requests_total++; counters.inflight_requests++;
        let counted=false;
        const finish=()=>{ if(counted)return; counted=true; counters.inflight_requests--;
          if(response.statusCode>=500 || !response.writableFinished)counters.errors_total++; };
        response.once('finish',finish); response.once('close',finish);
      });
      server.once('listening',()=>telemetry.start());
      server.once('close',()=>void telemetry.stop());
    }
    return {
      server,
      close: async () => {
        if (server.listening)
          await new Promise((resolve) => server.close(resolve));
        await telemetry?.stop();
        await business.close();
        await observer?.flush();
      },
    };
  } catch (error) {
    void business.close();
    throw error;
  }
}
if (
  process.argv[1] &&
  import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(path.resolve(process.argv[1])).href
) {
  requireThat(
    process.env.MODULE_PROJECTS_FILE && process.env.SERVICE_DATA_DIR,
    "service_config",
    "请配置 MODULE_PROJECTS_FILE 和 SERVICE_DATA_DIR。",
    500,
  );
  const directory = path.resolve(process.env.SERVICE_DATA_DIR);
  mkdirSync(directory, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
  const projects = JSON.parse(
    readFileSync(path.resolve(process.env.MODULE_PROJECTS_FILE), "utf8"),
  );
  const service = createService({
    token: process.env.PLATFORM_SERVICE_TOKEN,
    callbackUrl: process.env.PLATFORM_ACCESS_DECISION_URL,
    observationUrl: process.env.PLATFORM_OBSERVATION_URL,
    filename: path.join(directory, "records.sqlite"),
    projects,
    telemetryConfig: process.env.DUOFLOW_MONITOR_CONFIG_FILE ? (()=>{
      const configuration=JSON.parse(readFileSync(process.env.DUOFLOW_MONITOR_CONFIG_FILE,'utf8'));
      return {url:configuration.url, token:configuration.token, intervalSeconds:configuration.interval_seconds ?? 15,
        ...(configuration.instance_id ? {instanceId:configuration.instance_id} : {})};
    })() : null,
    aiManager: registration.ai_services?.length ? new AIServiceManager({
      services: registration.ai_services,
      dataDir: path.join(directory, "ai"),
      platformBaseUrl: process.env.PLATFORM_AI_BASE_URL ?? null,
    }) : null,
  });
  const port = Number(process.env.PORT || 0);
  requireThat(
    Number.isInteger(port) && port >= 0 && port <= 65535,
    "service_config",
    "PORT 无效。",
    500,
  );
  service.server.listen(port, "127.0.0.1", () =>
    console.log(
      JSON.stringify({ ready: true, port: service.server.address().port }),
    ),
  );
  for (const signal of ["SIGTERM", "SIGINT"])
    process.on(signal, () => void service.close().then(() => process.exit(0)));
}
```

```json
          "id": "review",
          "title": "审核与结果",
          "description": "审核人与作者必须不同。导航步骤不会自动执行审批。",
          "component": "Review",
          "actions": [
            {
              "id": "approve",
              "title": "审核通过",
              "capability_id": "__MODULE_ID__.approve",
              "capability_version": "1.0.0",
              "command_schema": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                  "note": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "minLength": 1,
                    "maxLength": 1000
                  }
                },
                "required": [
                  "note"
                ],
                "additionalProperties": false
              }
            }
          ]
        }
      ],
      "state_machine": {
        "id": "__MODULE_ID__.record-lifecycle",
        "version": "1.0.0",
        "initial": "draft",
        "states": [
          {
            "id": "draft",
            "title": "草稿"
          },
          {
            "id": "submitted",
            "title": "待审核"
          },
          {
            "id": "approved",
            "title": "已通过",
            "terminal": true
          }
        ],
        "transitions": [
          {
            "id": "save-draft",
            "from": "draft",
            "to": "draft",
            "step_id": "edit",
            "action_id": "edit",
            "title": "保存草稿",
            "guard_description": "作者本人、当前范围编辑权限与修订均通过。"
          },
          {
            "id": "submit-draft",
            "from": "draft",
            "to": "submitted",
            "step_id": "edit",
            "action_id": "submit",
            "title": "提交审核",
            "guard_description": "作者本人、当前范围提交权限与修订均通过。"
          },
          {
            "id": "approve-submitted",
            "from": "submitted",
            "to": "approved",
            "step_id": "review",
            "action_id": "approve",
            "title": "审核通过",
            "guard_description": "非作者的授权审核人，填写意见且修订一致。"
          }
        ]
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

### templates/module-kit/blueprints/workflow-node/frontend/steps.tsx

```tsx
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import type {
  WorkflowStepComponents,
  WorkflowStepProps,
} from "@platform/workflow-sdk";
import definition from "./flow.json";

const objectType = definition.flows[0].provider.object_type;
const stateLabels: Record<string, string> = Object.fromEntries(
  definition.flows[0].state_machine.states.map((state) => [
    state.id,
    state.title,
  ]),
);
function useAction() {
  const [error, setError] = useState(""),
    [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const running = useRef(false);
  async function run(work: () => Promise<unknown>) {
    if (running.current) return;
    running.current = true;
    setPending(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      await work();
    } catch (reason) {
      setError(
        reason instanceof Error ? reason.message : "操作未完成，请重试。",
      );
    } finally {
      running.current = false;
      setPending(false);
    }
  }
  return { error, pending, run };
}
function Picker(props: WorkflowStepProps) {
  const action = useAction(),
    locked = props.busy || action.pending;
  const available = props.actions.some(
    (item) => item.id === "create" && item.available,
  );
  const project_id = String(props.draft.project_id ?? ""),
    title = String(props.draft.title ?? ""),
    body = String(props.draft.body ?? "");
  const records = props.objects.filter(
    (item) => item.object_ref.object_type === objectType,
  );
  const more = props.objectPages.some(
    (item) => item.object_type === objectType && item.next_cursor,
  );
  return (
    <section aria-label="选择或创建记录">
      <h3>当前授权项目的记录</h3>
      <p>
        同项目、同机构授权范围内的参与者可读；编辑、提交和审核另外核验职责。
      </p>
      <ul>
        {records.map((record) => (
          <li key={record.object_ref.object_id}>
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={locked}
              onClick={() =>
                void action.run(() =>
                  props.openObject(record.object_ref.object_id),
                )
              }
            >
              {String(record.data.title)} ·{" "}
              {stateLabels[record.state] ?? record.state}
            </button>{" "}
            <small>{String(record.data.project_id)}</small>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {!records.length && <p>当前没有可读记录。</p>}
      {more && (
        <button
          type="button"
          disabled={locked}
          onClick={() =>
            void action.run(() => props.loadMoreObjects(objectType))
          }
        >
          加载更多记录
        </button>
      )}
      <h3>创建草稿</h3>
      <p>项目编号请使用管理员提供的服务端已登记编号。</p>
      <label>
        项目编号
        <input
          aria-label="项目编号"
          value={project_id}
          disabled={locked || !available}
          onChange={(event) =>
            props.updateDraft({
              ...props.draft,
              project_id: event.target.value,
            })
          }
        />
      </label>
      <label>
        标题
        <input
          aria-label="新记录标题"
          value={title}
          maxLength={200}
          disabled={locked || !available}
          onChange={(event) =>
            props.updateDraft({ ...props.draft, title: event.target.value })
          }
        />
      </label>
      <label>
        内容
        <textarea
          aria-label="新记录内容"
          value={body}
          maxLength={4000}
          disabled={locked || !available}
          onChange={(event) =>
            props.updateDraft({ ...props.draft, body: event.target.value })
          }
        />
      </label>
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled={locked || !available || !title.trim() || !project_id.trim()}
        onClick={() =>
          void action.run(() =>
            props.execute("create", { project_id, title, body }),
          )
        }
      >
        创建记录
      </button>
      {!available && <p>当前没有创建记录权限。</p>}
      {action.error && <p role="alert">{action.error}</p>}
    </section>
  );
}
function Editor(props: WorkflowStepProps) {
  const action = useAction();
  if (!props.projection)
    return (
      <section>
        <h3>填写与提交</h3>
        <p>请先选择或创建一条记录。</p>
      </section>
    );
  const source = props.projection.data,
    locked = props.busy || action.pending;
  const editable = props.actions.some(
    (item) => item.id === "edit" && item.available,
  );
  const submittable = props.actions.some(
    (item) => item.id === "submit" && item.available,
  );
  const title = String(props.draft.title ?? source.title ?? ""),
    body = String(props.draft.body ?? source.body ?? "");
  const changed =
    title !== String(source.title ?? "") || body !== String(source.body ?? "");
  return (
    <section aria-label="填写与提交">
      <h3>填写与提交</h3>
      <p>
        当前修订 {props.projection.revision} ·{" "}
        {stateLabels[props.projection.state] ?? props.projection.state}
      </p>
      <label>
        标题
        <input
          aria-label="记录标题"
          value={title}
          maxLength={200}
          disabled={locked || !editable}
          onChange={(event) =>
            props.updateDraft({ ...props.draft, title: event.target.value })
          }
        />
      </label>
      <label>
        内容
        <textarea
          aria-label="记录内容"
          value={body}
          maxLength={4000}
          disabled={locked || !editable}
          onChange={(event) =>
            props.updateDraft({ ...props.draft, body: event.target.value })
          }
        />
      </label>
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled={locked || !editable || !title.trim()}
        onClick={() =>
          void action.run(() => props.execute("edit", { title, body }))
        }
      >
        保存记录
      </button>
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled={locked || !submittable || changed}
        onClick={() => void action.run(() => props.execute("submit", {}))}
      >
        提交审核
      </button>
      {changed && editable && <p>提交前请先保存正式记录。</p>}
      {!editable && <p>当前记录只读。</p>}
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled={locked}
        onClick={() => props.navigate("review")}
      >
        查看审核与结果
      </button>
      {action.error && <p role="alert">{action.error}</p>}
    </section>
  );
}
function Review(props: WorkflowStepProps) {
  const action = useAction();
  if (!props.projection)
    return (
      <section>
        <h3>审核与结果</h3>
        <p>请先选择一条记录。</p>
      </section>
    );
  const locked = props.busy || action.pending,
    available = props.actions.some(
      (item) => item.id === "approve" && item.available,
    );
  const note = String(props.draft.note ?? "");
  return (
    <section aria-label="审核与结果">
      <h3>审核与结果</h3>
      <p>
        {String(props.projection.data.title)} ·{" "}
        {stateLabels[props.projection.state] ?? props.projection.state}
      </p>
      <p>{String(props.projection.data.body ?? "")}</p>
      {props.projection.data.review_note && (
        <p>审核意见：{String(props.projection.data.review_note)}</p>
      )}
      <label>
        审核意见
        <textarea
          aria-label="审核意见"
          value={note}
          maxLength={1000}
          disabled={locked || !available}
          onChange={(event) =>
            props.updateDraft({ ...props.draft, note: event.target.value })
          }
        />
      </label>
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled={locked || !available || !note.trim()}
        onClick={() =>
          void action.run(() => props.execute("approve", { note }))
        }
      >
        审核通过
      </button>
      {!available && (
        <p>仅具有本项目范围审核权限且不是作者的办理人，可审核待审记录。</p>
      )}
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled={locked}
        onClick={() => void action.run(() => props.refresh())}
      >
        重新读取结果
      </button>
      {action.error && <p role="alert">{action.error}</p>}
    </section>
  );
}
export const stepComponents: WorkflowStepComponents = {
  Picker,
  Editor,
  Review,
};
```
