### templates/module-kit/blueprints/workflow-node/tests/workflow.test.mjs

```javascript
/** Real isolated HTTP/SQLite tests. The central decision endpoint below is a synthetic stub,
 * not a substitute for registration, real grants, host UI, or production browser acceptance. */
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import http from "node:http";
import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";
import {
  createService,
  manifest,
  registration,
  moduleGraph,
} from "../backend/server.mjs";
import { createObservationClient } from "../sdk/node/observation.mjs";
import { compile, createModuleServer } from "../sdk/node/runtime.mjs";
import { createAtomBindings } from "../sdk/node/atoms.mjs";
import {
  assertTransition,
  assertAtomResult,
} from "../sdk/node/graph-testing.mjs";

const M = manifest.module.id;
const token = "synthetic-test-service-credential-32";
const projects = [
  { project_id: "P", organization_id: "O", title: "合成 P" },
  { project_id: "Q", organization_id: "Z", title: "合成 Q" },
];
const actor = (id) => ({
  id,
  name: id,
  role: "member",
  roles: ["member"],
  organization_id: "O",
  team_ids: [],
  authorization_revision: 1,
});
const id = () => randomBytes(16).toString("hex");
async function listen(server) {
  await new Promise((resolve) => server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", resolve));
  return `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
}
async function fixture(t) {
  const directory = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), "module-kit-node-"));
  const tickets = new Map(),
    seen = [],
    revoked = new Set();
  let unavailable = false,
    denyCommit = false;
  const central = http.createServer(async (req, res) => {
    let body = "";
    for await (const chunk of req) body += chunk;
    const input = JSON.parse(body),
      claims = tickets.get(req.headers["x-platform-access"]);
    if (req.url === "/api/v1/runtime-observations/probe") {
      res.writeHead(200, { "Content-Type": "application/json" });
      res.end(JSON.stringify({ accepted: true, nonce: input.nonce }));
      return;
    }
    seen.push(input);
    let reason = "allowed";
    if (unavailable) {
      res.writeHead(503);
      res.end("{}");
      return;
    }
    if (!claims || claims.expired) reason = "access_ticket_expired";
    else if (
      claims.requestId !== input.request_id ||
      claims.operation !== input.operation ||
      claims.permission !== input.permission
    )
      reason = "access_ticket_permission_mismatch";
    else if (revoked.has(claims.actor)) reason = "access_authorization_changed";
    else if (
      input.facts.project_id !== "P" ||
      input.facts.organization_id !== "O" ||
      claims.actor === "outsider"
    )
      reason = "grant_scope_mismatch";
    else if (
      ["edit", "submit"].some(
        (suffix) => input.permission === `${M}.${suffix}`,
      ) &&
      input.facts.author_id !== claims.actor
    )
      reason = "actor_relation_mismatch";
    else if (
      input.permission === `${M}.approve` &&
      input.facts.author_id === claims.actor
    )
      reason = "separation_of_duties";
    else if (input.permission === `${M}.approve` && claims.actor !== "reviewer")
      reason = "grant_scope_mismatch";
    else if (
      input.permission !== `${M}.read` &&
      claims.actor === "reviewer" &&
      input.permission !== `${M}.approve`
    )
      reason = "grant_scope_mismatch";
    else if (
      denyCommit &&
      input.permission === `${M}.create` &&
      seen.filter((item) => item.request_id === input.request_id).length > 1
    )
      reason = "access_authorization_changed";
    res.writeHead(200, { "Content-Type": "application/json" });
    res.end(
      JSON.stringify({
        allowed: reason === "allowed",
        reason,
        policy_revision: reason.startsWith("access_") ? 0 : 1,
      }),
    );
  });
  const centralUrl = await listen(central),
    filename = path.join(directory, "records.sqlite");
  let service = createService({
      token,
      callbackUrl: centralUrl + "/api/v1/access/remote-decisions",
      filename,
      projects,
    }),
    url = await listen(service.server);
  const makeBody = (action, input, who = "author") => ({
    protocol_version: "1.1",
    request_id: id(),
    capability_id: `${M}.${action}`,
    capability_version: "1.0.0",
    input,
    context: { workspace_id: "synthetic-workspace", actor: actor(who) },
  });
  async function request(body, operation = "invoke", options = {}) {
    const ticket = "synthetic-ticket-" + id();
    const suffix = body.capability_id?.slice(M.length + 1);
    tickets.set(ticket, {
      requestId: body.request_id,
      operation,
      permission: `${M}.${operation === "invoke" && suffix !== "list" ? suffix : "read"}`,
      actor: body.context.actor.id,
      expired: options.expired,
    });
    const headers = {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
      ...(!options.noTicket ? { "X-Platform-Access": ticket } : {}),
    };
    const result = await fetch(
      url +
        "/platform/v1/" +
        (operation === "invoke" ? "invoke" : `workflow/${operation}`),
      { method: "POST", headers, body: JSON.stringify(body) },
    );
    return { status: result.status, body: await result.json(), ticket };
  }
  const invoke = (action, input, who = "author", options = {}) =>
    request(makeBody(action, input, who), "invoke", options);
  const flow = (operation, input, who = "author", options = {}) =>
    request(
      {
        protocol_version: "1.0",
        request_id: id(),
        context: { actor: actor(who) },
        input,
      },
      operation,
      options,
    );
  const create = (title = "合成记录") =>
    invoke("create", { command: { project_id: "P", title, body: "内容" } });
  t.after(async () => {
    await service.close();
    await new Promise((resolve) => central.close(resolve));
    rmSync(directory, { recursive: true, force: true });
  });
  return {
    get url() {
      return url;
    },
    filename,
    tickets,
    seen,
    revoked,
    makeBody,
    request,
    invoke,
    flow,
    create,
    setUnavailable: (value) => {
      unavailable = value;
    },
    setDenyCommit: (value) => {
      denyCommit = value;
    },
    restart: async () => {
      await service.close();
      service = createService({
        token,
        callbackUrl: centralUrl + "/api/v1/access/remote-decisions",
        filename,
        projects,
      });
      url = await listen(service.server);
    },
  };
}

test("health and manifest use real HTTP and require the private service bearer", async (t) => {
  const f = await fixture(t);
  for (const endpoint of ["health", "manifest"]) {
    assert.equal((await fetch(f.url + "/platform/v1/" + endpoint)).status, 401);
    const response = await fetch(f.url + "/platform/v1/" + endpoint, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
    });
    assert.equal(response.status, 200);
    const data = await response.json();
    assert.equal(
      endpoint === "health" ? data.status : data.protocol_version,
      endpoint === "health" ? "ok" : "1.2",
    );
  }
});

test("registration checks both complete contracts and actual bindings without business writes", async (t) => {
  const f = await fixture(t),
    headers = {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
    };
  assert.equal((await fetch(f.url + "/platform/v1/registration")).status, 401);
  assert.equal((await fetch(f.url + "/platform/v1/module-graph")).status, 401);
  assert.deepEqual(
    await (
      await fetch(f.url + "/platform/v1/module-graph", { headers })
    ).json(),
    moduleGraph,
  );
  assert.deepEqual(
    await (
      await fetch(f.url + "/platform/v1/registration", { headers })
    ).json(),
    registration,
  );
  const offer = {
    registration_version: "1.1",
    nonce: "a".repeat(32),
    contract_digest: "b".repeat(64),
    platform_features: [
      "invoke-1.1",
      "access-1.0",
      "shared-hosts-1.0",
      "registration-1.0",
      "registration-1.1",
      "module-graph-1.0",
      "flow-exports-1.0",
      "runtime-observation-1.0",
    ],
    manifest,
    registration,
    runtime_probe: { ticket: "c".repeat(64), nonce: "d".repeat(32) },
  };
  async function confirm(body) {
    return fetch(f.url + "/platform/v1/handshake", {
      method: "POST",
      headers,
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    });
  }
  const accepted = await confirm(offer);
  assert.equal(accepted.status, 200);
  const answer = await accepted.json();
  assert.equal(answer.accepted, true);
  assert.deepEqual(
    answer.capability_ids,
    manifest.capabilities.map((cap) => cap.id).sort(),
  );
  for (const field of ["manifest", "registration", "platform_features"])
    assert.equal((await confirm({ ...offer, [field]: [] })).status, 422);
  assert.equal((await confirm({})).status, 422);
  const database = new DatabaseSync(f.filename, { readOnly: true });
  assert.equal(
    database.prepare("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM records").get().n,
    0,
  );
  assert.equal(
    database.prepare("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM kit_receipts").get().n,
    0,
  );
  database.close();
  assert.equal(f.seen.length, 0);
});
test("atomic startup binding rejects missing functions and never runs business handlers", () => {
  let calls = 0;
  const handlers = Object.fromEntries(
    manifest.capabilities.map((cap) => [
      cap.id,
      () => {
        calls++;
      },
    ]),
  );
  const bindings = createAtomBindings({
    graph: moduleGraph,
    manifest,
    handlers,
  });
  assert.equal(
    bindings.forCapability(manifest.capabilities[0].id).kind,
    "backend",
  );
  const copy = bindings.document;
  copy.atoms.length = 0;
  assert.ok(bindings.document.atoms.length > 0);
  assert.throws(() =>
    createAtomBindings({ graph: moduleGraph, manifest, handlers: {} }),
  );
  assert.equal(calls, 0);
});
test("strict request validation and server-owned project facts reject injected scope", async (t) => {
  const f = await fixture(t);
  assert.equal(
    (
      await f.invoke("create", {
        command: { project_id: "UNKNOWN", title: "x", body: "" },
      })
    ).status,
    422,
  );
  assert.equal(
    (
      await f.invoke("create", {
        command: {
          project_id: "P",
          organization_id: "FORGED",
          title: "x",
          body: "",
        },
      })
    ).status,
    422,
  );
  const malformed = f.makeBody("create", {
    command: { project_id: "P", title: "x", body: "" },
  });
  malformed.context.actor.extra = "forged";
  assert.equal((await f.request(malformed)).status, 422);
  const created = await f.create();
  assert.equal(created.status, 200);
  assert.equal(created.body.data.projection.data.organization_id, "O");
  assert.ok(f.seen.every((item) => !JSON.stringify(item).includes("FORGED")));
});
test("missing, expired and unavailable central decisions fail closed even for empty lists", async (t) => {
  const f = await fixture(t);
  for (const options of [{ noTicket: true }, { expired: true }])
    assert.equal(
      (await f.flow("objects.list", {}, "author", options)).status,
      403,
    );
  f.setUnavailable(true);
  assert.equal((await f.create()).status, 403);
  assert.equal((await f.flow("objects.list", {})).status, 403);
  f.setUnavailable(false);
  assert.deepEqual((await f.flow("objects.list", {})).body.data.objects, []);
});
test("draft -> submitted -> approved requires scoped non-author reviewer; formal states persist", async (t) => {
  const f = await fixture(t),
    created = await f.create(),
    object_id = created.body.data.projection.object_ref.object_id;
  assert.equal(
    (
      await f.invoke(
        "edit",
        {
          object_id,
          expected_revision: 1,
          command: { title: "他人编辑", body: "" },
        },
        "reviewer",
      )
    ).status,
    403,
  );
  for (const name of ["constructor", "toString", "__proto__"]) {
    assert.equal(
      (
        await f.invoke("submit", {
          object_id,
          expected_revision: 1,
          command: JSON.parse(`{"${name}":3}`),
        })
      ).status,
      422,
    );
  }
  const submitted = await f.invoke("submit", {
    object_id,
    expected_revision: 1,
    command: {},
  });
  assert.equal(submitted.body.data.projection.state, "submitted");
  assertTransition(
    moduleGraph,
    `${M}.record-lifecycle`,
    created.body.data.projection,
    "submit",
    submitted.body.data.projection,
  );
  const atom = moduleGraph.atoms.find(
    (item) => item.capability?.id === `${M}.submit`,
  );
  assertAtomResult(
    moduleGraph,
    atom.id,
    { object_id, expected_revision: 1, command: {} },
    submitted.body.data,
    (schema, value) => assert.ok(compile(schema)(value)),
    manifest,
  );
  assert.equal(
    (
      await f.invoke("approve", {
        object_id,
        expected_revision: 2,
        command: { note: "自审" },
      })
    ).status,
    403,
  );
  const approved = await f.invoke(
    "approve",
    { object_id, expected_revision: 2, command: { note: "合成审核通过" } },
    "reviewer",
  );
  assert.equal(approved.status, 200);
  assert.equal(approved.body.data.projection.revision, 3);
  assert.equal(approved.body.data.projection.state, "approved");
  assertTransition(
    moduleGraph,
    `${M}.record-lifecycle`,
    submitted.body.data.projection,
    "approve",
    approved.body.data.projection,
  );
  assert.throws(() =>
    assertTransition(
      moduleGraph,
      `${M}.record-lifecycle`,
      created.body.data.projection,
      "approve",
      approved.body.data.projection,
    ),
  );
  assert.equal(
    (
      await f.invoke("edit", {
        object_id,
        expected_revision: 3,
        command: { title: "不应修改", body: "" },
      })
    ).status,
    409,
  );
  await f.restart();
  const read = await f.flow(
    "objects.read",
    { object_ref: { object_type: M + ".record", object_id } },
    "reviewer",
  );
  assert.equal(read.body.data.projection.data.review_note, "合成审核通过");
});
test("atomic idempotency survives restart, reauthorizes replay, and never persists tickets", async (t) => {
  const f = await fixture(t),
    body = f.makeBody("create", {
      command: { project_id: "P", title: "幂等", body: "" },
    });
  const first = await f.request(body);
  await f.restart();
  const replay = await f.request(body);
  assert.deepEqual(replay.body, first.body);
  const conflict = structuredClone(body);
  conflict.input.command.title = "改变";
  assert.equal((await f.request(conflict)).status, 409);
  const database = new DatabaseSync(f.filename, { readOnly: true });
  assert.equal(
    database.prepare("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM records").get().n,
    1,
  );
  const receipts = database.prepare("SELECT * FROM kit_receipts").all();
  database.close();
  assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(receipts).includes(first.ticket));
  assert.ok(!readFileSync(f.filename).includes(Buffer.from(first.ticket)));
  f.revoked.add("author");
  assert.equal((await f.request(body)).status, 403);
});
test("concurrent same-revision edits commit one change and one receipt", async (t) => {
  const f = await fixture(t),
    created = await f.create(),
    object_id = created.body.data.projection.object_ref.object_id;
  const result = await Promise.all(
    ["一", "二"].map((title) =>
      f.invoke("edit", {
        object_id,
        expected_revision: 1,
        command: { title, body: "" },
      }),
    ),
  );
  assert.deepEqual(result.map((item) => item.status).sort(), [200, 409]);
  const read = await f.invoke("read", { object_id });
  assert.equal(read.body.data.projection.revision, 2);
});
test("revocation at the commit check rolls back object and receipt together", async (t) => {
  const f = await fixture(t);
  f.setDenyCommit(true);
  assert.equal((await f.create()).status, 403);
  f.setDenyCommit(false);
  assert.deepEqual((await f.flow("objects.list", {})).body.data.objects, []);
  const database = new DatabaseSync(f.filename, { readOnly: true });
  assert.equal(
    database.prepare("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM kit_receipts").get().n,
    0,
  );
  database.close();
});
test("read and list are scoped before pagination, and cursors bind actor and revision snapshot", async (t) => {
  const f = await fixture(t);
  for (let n = 0; n < 3; n++)
    assert.equal((await f.create("记录" + n)).status, 200);
  const first = await f.flow("objects.list", { limit: 2 });
  assert.equal(first.body.data.objects.length, 2);
  assert.ok(first.body.data.next_cursor);
  const cursor = first.body.data.next_cursor,
    object = first.body.data.objects[0];
  assert.equal(
    (await f.flow("objects.list", { limit: 2, cursor })).body.data.objects
      .length,
    1,
  );
  assert.equal(
    (await f.flow("objects.list", { limit: 2, cursor }, "reviewer")).status,
    409,
  );
  assert.deepEqual(
    (await f.flow("objects.list", {}, "outsider")).body.data.objects,
    [],
  );
  assert.equal(
    (
      await f.invoke(
        "read",
        { object_id: object.object_ref.object_id },
        "outsider",
      )
    ).status,
    403,
  );
  assert.equal(
    (await f.flow("objects.list", { limit: 2, cursor: cursor + "bad" })).status,
    422,
  );
  await f.invoke("edit", {
    object_id: object.object_ref.object_id,
    expected_revision: 1,
    command: { title: "更新", body: "" },
  });
  assert.equal(
    (await f.flow("objects.list", { limit: 2, cursor })).status,
    409,
  );
  assert.ok(
    first.body.data.objects.every((item) => !Object.hasOwn(item.data, "body")),
  );
});
test("unsupported files/jobs are explicit 501, and unsupported schema keywords fail startup", async (t) => {
  const f = await fixture(t);
  for (const operation of ["objects.detail", "uploads.create", "jobs.status"])
    assert.equal((await f.flow(operation, {})).status, 501);
  assert.throws(() => compile({ type: "object", allOf: [] }), /Schema/);
  assert.throws(
    () =>
      compile({ type: "object", properties: { value: { $ref: "#/$defs/a" } } }),
    /Schema/,
  );
});
test("schema subset never interprets prototype names as validators or ignores nested extra-field schemas", () => {
  const closed = compile({
    type: "object",
    properties: {},
    additionalProperties: false,
  });
  for (const name of ["constructor", "toString", "__proto__"])
    assert.equal(closed(JSON.parse(`{"${name}":3}`)), false);
  assert.throws(
    () => compile({ type: "object", additionalProperties: { type: "string" } }),
    /additionalProperties/,
  );
  const explicit = compile(
    JSON.parse(
      '{"type":"object","properties":{"__proto__":{"type":"string"}},"additionalProperties":false}',
    ),
  );
  assert.equal(explicit(JSON.parse('{"__proto__":"data"}')), true);
  assert.equal(explicit(JSON.parse('{"__proto__":3}')), false);
  assert.equal(Object.prototype.polluted, undefined);
});

test("observation delivery retries stable identities and isolates collector failures", async () => {
  const batches = [];
  const observer = createObservationClient({
    callbackUrl: "http://127.0.0.1:9999/api/v1/runtime-observations/events",
    graph: moduleGraph,
    fetchImpl: async (url, options) => {
      batches.push(JSON.parse(options.body));
      throw new Error("offline");
    },
  });
  const session = observer.session({
    ticket: "a".repeat(64),
    requestId: "b".repeat(32),
    atomId: moduleGraph.atoms[0].id,
  });
  session.mark("started");
  const result = { value: 7 };
  session.mark("succeeded");
  await observer.flush();
  assert.deepEqual(result, { value: 7 });
  assert.deepEqual(session.status(), { sequence: 2, dropped: 2 });
  assert.equal(batches.length, 4);
  assert.ok(
    batches.every((batch) =>
      batch.events.every((event) => !Object.hasOwn(event, "input")),
    ),
  );
  const absent = observer.session({
    requestId: "c".repeat(32),
    atomId: moduleGraph.atoms[0].id,
  });
  absent.mark("started");
  await observer.flush();
  assert.equal(batches.length, 4);
});

test("stateless role-based tools execute without central tickets and still reject unauthorized actors", async (t) => {
  const cap = {
    ...manifest.capabilities[0],
    id: `${M}.echo`,
    kind: "query",
    input_schema: {
      type: "object",
      properties: { text: { type: "string" } },
      required: ["text"],
      additionalProperties: false,
    },
    output_schema: {
      type: "object",
      properties: { text: { type: "string" } },
      required: ["text"],
      additionalProperties: false,
    },
  };
  const m = { ...manifest, capabilities: [cap] };
  const r = {
    registration_version: "1.0",
    module_id: M,
    module_version: m.module.version,
    permissions: { mode: "capability_roles", definition: null },
    data_objects: [],
    business_chains: [],
    host_requirements: { workflows: [], interactions: [] },
    workflow_operations: [],
  };
  const server = createModuleServer({
    manifest: m,
    registration: r,
    token,
    handlers: {
      [cap.id]: ({ body }) => ({
        summary: "已完成",
        data: { text: body.input.text },
      }),
    },
  });
  const url = await listen(server);
  t.after(() => new Promise((resolve) => server.close(resolve)));
  const body = {
    protocol_version: "1.1",
    request_id: id(),
    capability_id: cap.id,
    capability_version: cap.version,
    input: { text: "合成文字" },
    context: { workspace_id: "test", actor: actor("member") },
  };
  async function send(value) {
    return fetch(url + "/platform/v1/invoke", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(value),
    });
  }
  const accepted = await send(body);
  assert.equal(accepted.status, 200);
  assert.equal((await accepted.json()).data.text, "合成文字");
  body.context.actor.role = "researcher";
  body.context.actor.roles = ["researcher"];
  assert.equal((await send(body)).status, 403);
});
```

### templates/module-kit/examples/ai-services/README.md

````markdown
# 模块 AI 服务：声明、独立配置与平台选路

本目录配合 `sdk/node/ai-service.mjs`、`sdk/python/ai_service.py`。这是可运行的 SDK 使用示例，不是已经安装或授权的业务模块。Node 要求 24；Python 要求 3.11+、httpx、cryptography，使用套件锁定依赖。

在实际模块 `backend/registration.json` 的顶层声明 AI 服务，例如已有能力为 `your.module.analyze`：

```json
{
  "ai_services": [{
    "id": "your.module.analysis",
    "title": "描述分析",
    "description": "对已授权的输入生成候选分析；正式采纳由模块规则决定。",
    "capability_ids": ["your.module.analyze"],
    "supported_sources": ["platform", "module"]
  }]
}
```

将该字段并入完整注册声明；不要用这个片段替代 registration。服务 ID 属于模块命名空间，能力 ID 必须绑定实际处理器。不在声明、能力输入、图、共享组件或业务回执中填写 API URL、模型密钥和供应商配置。无 AI 的模板保持 `ai_services: []` 或不写该可选字段。新 AI 模块握手额外要求 `ai-services-1.0`；旧模块不要求。

## Node 服务接线

```javascript
import { AIServiceManager } from '../sdk/node/ai-service.mjs';
const ai = new AIServiceManager({
  services: registration.ai_services,
  dataDir: path.join(process.env.SERVICE_DATA_DIR, 'ai'),
  platformBaseUrl: process.env.PLATFORM_AI_BASE_URL,
});
const server = createModuleServer({
  manifest, registration, token, handlers, workflowHandlers,
  graph: moduleGraph, observationClient: observer, aiManager: ai,
});
```

`PLATFORM_AI_BASE_URL` 为服务端固定平台 origin，例如 `http://127.0.0.1:8030`。独立运行时传 `platformBaseUrl: null`，只使用模块自有 API。连接平台后必须配置 origin，平台不可用、票据失效、禁用或未选来源均拒绝，绝不偷偷改用模块配置。带 AI 声明的 Node runtime 在正式握手时要求实际 manager 已配置平台地址，防止漏配后把平台调用当成独立调用。握手不会自动猜测或写入部署地址。workflow-node 启动器对非空声明自动构造 manager；业务仍须在实际 handler 使用传入的 `ai`，声明本身不会调用模型。

业务 handler 使用当前票据和仓储事实：

```javascript
async function analyze({ body, ticket }) {
  const record = await repository.read(body.input.object_id);
  const scope = {
    ticket, requestId: body.request_id, operation: 'invoke',
    permission: 'your.module.analyze',
    facts: { project_id: record.project_id, organization_id: record.organization_id },
  };
  // 先按既有规则验证当前授权、正式状态、输入修订和幂等；事实来自仓储。
  const { result, route } = await ai.chatWithReceipt({
    serviceId: 'your.module.analysis', scope,
    messages: [{ role: 'user', content: record.description }],
  });
  const candidate = validateDomainOutput(result.content);
  // 期间可能发生来源读取或业务校验。紧邻提交再次核验 AI 路由及配置。
  await ai.validate({ serviceId: 'your.module.analysis', scope, route });
  // 然后由原业务事务再次核对权限、来源和对象修订，以 CAS 保存候选。
  // repository/validateDomainOutput 属于模块，SDK 不代替这些领域实现。
  return { summary: '已生成待核验候选', data: { candidate } };
}
```

只需要模型结果而不提交业务时，可用 `await ai.chat(...)`，返回 `{content,tool_calls:[],model,finish_reason?,usage?}`。`chatWithReceipt` 额外返回不含凭据的 `route`，可在最终提交前 `validate`。配置变化、中央撤权和路由切换均应拒绝旧结果；不要捕获错误后提交空结果或调用备用模型。`validate` 是当前检查，不是跨服务器的原子事务或长期授权；模块仍需本地事务/CAS/副作用恢复。

## Python HTTP 适配

```python
from sdk.python.ai_service import AIServiceManager, AIServiceError

ai = AIServiceManager(services=registration['ai_services'], data_dir=private_dir / 'ai',
                      platform_base_url='http://127.0.0.1:8030')
# 对应 Registration(..., ai_manager=ai)，固定声明和真实管理器必须一致。
receipt = await ai.chat_with_receipt(service_id='your.module.analysis', messages=messages,
    scope=dict(ticket=ticket, request_id=body['request_id'], operation='invoke',
               permission='your.module.analyze', facts=repository_facts))
await ai.validate(service_id='your.module.analysis', route=receipt['route'], scope=current_scope)
```

Python 适配器先验证服务 Bearer，再把下面固定控制路由转给 `await ai.control(service_id, method, action, body)`。不要暴露公共页面或能力用于读写这些配置。`AIServiceError.code/status` 只含安全错误，不转发供应商响应正文。Python `Registration(..., ai_manager=ai)` 校验声明和真实管理器绑定、握手特征及已配置的固定平台地址；HTTP adapter 仍须实际接入控制路由。

## 自有配置与手动选路

Node runtime 已实现仅服务 Bearer 可访问的控制接口，Python adapter 调用同一控制方法：

| 路由 | 请求/结果 |
| --- | --- |
| `GET /platform/v1/ai/services/<id>/config` | `{revision,profile}`；首次为 `0/null`，公开 profile 只有 `api_key_configured`，没有密钥 |
| `PUT /platform/v1/ai/services/<id>/config` | `{expected_revision,profile:{name,base_url,model,api_key?,clear_api_key?,enabled,temperature,max_tokens,timeout_seconds}}`，以修订 CAS 保存 |
| `POST /platform/v1/ai/services/<id>/test` | `{expected_revision}`，发送固定合成连接测试提示，返回实际供应商响应摘要 |

密钥留空保留旧值，`clear_api_key: true` 明确清除，变更 base_url 时必须重新输入或明确清除密钥。URL 仅支持 HTTPS，回环地址可用 HTTP；禁止重定向。不支持 tools、流式、多模态或自定义供应商 HTTP headers。

接平台后，管理员在平台 AI 设置中分别保存平台模型配置、模块自有配置及来源绑定，再手动选择平台或模块。初始绑定为禁用；配置存在不等于已选中。平台来源经中央 broker 调用，不将平台密钥下发模块。模块来源仍每次请求 `/api/v1/ai/resolve`，使用本地密钥执行后再请求 `/validate`；同时核对 `binding_revision` 和 `module_config_revision`。平台来源也透传 `platform_config_digest`，在中央 profile 更新后拒绝旧结果；模块来源该字段为 null。所有修订和 digest 由 resolve 固定，handler 不得从浏览器或旧业务记录伪造。

私有配置目录产生 `module-ai.config.enc` 与 `module-ai.key`，整个配置使用 AES-256-GCM 加密、随机 nonce、原子替换；密钥为本地随机 32 字节。该目录不入 Git，不随可携带源码导出。文件模式为 0600、目录0700；Windows 部署另收紧目录 ACL。复制完整目录的人仍能解密，因此不能声称本地文件密钥抵御主机或整目录泄漏。单进程管理配置，多进程需外部锁/事务存储。

独立演练可运行 `standalone-node.mjs` / `standalone-python.py`。设置私有 `MODULE_AI_DATA_DIR`，执行 `status` 查看状态；`configure` 从标准输入读取配置 JSON（避免终端历史和日志记录密钥）；`test` 才会实际调用供应商并可能计费。示例默认不会联网，不发现环境中的供应商密钥。SDK 测试中使用的 transport seam 只是合成网络回归，不能当成真实 API 或业务验收。
````

### templates/module-kit/examples/ai-services/standalone-node.mjs

```javascript
/** Explicit local exercise; configure reads JSON from private stdin, never args. */
import { AIServiceManager, AIServiceError } from "../../sdk/node/ai-service.mjs";

const services = [{ id: "example.ai.analysis", title: "独立合成分析", description: "显式连接测试", capability_ids: ["example.ai.analyze"], supported_sources: ["platform", "module"] }];
try {
  if (!process.env.MODULE_AI_DATA_DIR) throw new AIServiceError("ai_private_directory_required", 500);
  const ai = new AIServiceManager({ services, dataDir: process.env.MODULE_AI_DATA_DIR });
  const action = process.argv[2] || "status", id = services[0].id;
  let output;
  if (action === "configure") {
    let raw = "";
    for await (const chunk of process.stdin) {
      raw += chunk;
      if (Buffer.byteLength(raw) > 32768) throw new AIServiceError("ai_configuration", 422);
    }
    output = ai.putConfig(id, { expected_revision: ai.getConfig(id).revision, profile: JSON.parse(raw) });
  } else if (action === "test") output = await ai.testConnection(id, ai.getConfig(id).revision);
  else if (action === "status") output = ai.getConfig(id);
  else throw new AIServiceError("ai_example_action", 422);
  process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(output) + "\n");
} catch (error) {
  process.stderr.write(JSON.stringify({ error: error instanceof AIServiceError ? error.code : "ai_example_failed" }) + "\n");
  process.exitCode = 1;
}
```

### templates/module-kit/examples/ai-services/standalone-python.py

```python
"""Explicit local exercise: configure reads private stdin; test really calls API."""
import asyncio
import importlib.util
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys

spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("ai_service", Path(__file__).parents[2] / "sdk/python/ai_service.py")
sdk = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(sdk)
services = [dict(id="example.ai.analysis", title="独立合成分析", description="显式连接测试",
                 capability_ids=["example.ai.analyze"], supported_sources=["platform", "module"])]
try:
    if not os.environ.get("MODULE_AI_DATA_DIR"):
        raise sdk.AIServiceError("ai_private_directory_required", 500)
    ai = sdk.AIServiceManager(services=services, data_dir=os.environ["MODULE_AI_DATA_DIR"])
    action, service_id = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "status", services[0]["id"]
    if action == "configure":
        raw = sys.stdin.read(32769)
        if len(raw.encode()) > 32768:
            raise sdk.AIServiceError("ai_configuration", 422)
        output = ai.put_config(service_id, expected_revision=ai.get_config(service_id)["revision"], profile=json.loads(raw))
    elif action == "test":
        output = asyncio.run(ai.test_connection(service_id, ai.get_config(service_id)["revision"]))
    elif action == "status":
        output = ai.get_config(service_id)
    else:
        raise sdk.AIServiceError("ai_example_action", 422)
    print(json.dumps(output, ensure_ascii=False))
except Exception as error:
    print(json.dumps(dict(error=error.code if isinstance(error, sdk.AIServiceError) else "ai_example_failed")), file=sys.stderr)
    sys.exit(1)
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/frontend/atoms.ts

```typescript
/** Stable UI identities shared by page, conversation and the runtime graph.
 * These definitions describe component contracts; authorization and business
 * state transitions remain server responsibilities.
 */
export type AtomSchema = Record<string, unknown>;
export type AtomicHost = {
  kind: "interaction" | "workflow_step";
  id: string;
  version: string;
  step_id?: string;
};
export type AtomicEvent = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  input_schema: AtomSchema;
  output_schema: AtomSchema;
  effect: string;
  state_effect: string;
  capability?: { id: string; version: string };
};
export type FrontendAtom = {
  id: string;
  kind: "frontend";
  title: string;
  description: string;
  host: AtomicHost;
  input_schema: AtomSchema;
  output_schema: AtomSchema;
  state_schema: AtomSchema;
  initial_state: Record<string, unknown>;
  events: AtomicEvent[];
};
export type AtomNodeMapping = {
  atom_id: string;
  node_id: string;
  role: "handles" | "shows" | "reads" | "writes";
  event_id?: string;
};

/** Bind concrete components once. No downloaded code or implicit name matching. */
export function registerAtomicComponents<T>(
  definitions: FrontendAtom[],
  components: Record<string, T>,
) {
  const table = new Map<string, { definition: FrontendAtom; component: T }>();
  for (const definition of definitions) {
    if (table.has(definition.id) || !Object.hasOwn(components, definition.id)) {
      throw new Error("组件登记重复，或没有对应的实际组件。");
    }
    table.set(definition.id, {
      definition: structuredClone(definition),
      component: components[definition.id],
    });
  }
  if (table.size !== Object.keys(components).length) {
    throw new Error("实际组件中存在未登记项。");
  }
  return {
    get(id: string) {
      const entry = table.get(id);
      if (!entry) throw new Error("组件未登记。");
      return {
        definition: structuredClone(entry.definition),
        component: entry.component,
      };
    },
    list() {
      return [...table.values()].map((entry) =>
        structuredClone(entry.definition),
      );
    },
  };
}

/** A host resolves a definition by its full binding, never by a translated title. */
export function atomForHost(atoms: FrontendAtom[], host: AtomicHost) {
  const matches = atoms.filter(
    (atom) =>
      atom.host.kind === host.kind &&
      atom.host.id === host.id &&
      atom.host.version === host.version &&
      atom.host.step_id === host.step_id,
  );
  if (matches.length > 1) throw new Error("同一宿主重复登记。");
  return matches[0] ?? null;
}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/README.md

```markdown
# Node 模块 SDK v1

可选 AI 服务使用 `ai-service.mjs` 的 `AIServiceManager`，声明于 `registration.ai_services`，由 `createModuleServer({... , aiManager})` 绑定实际私有控制接口。模块可独立配置自有 API；接入平台后每次按管理员来源绑定执行，平台来源经中央 broker，模块来源本地执行，禁止静默 fallback。`chatWithReceipt` 返回模型结果及配置/绑定修订，最终业务提交前再次 `validate`；`chat` 仅返回结果。具体 Node/Python 接线、配置字段和运行边界见 [AI 服务示例](../../examples/ai-services/README.md)。

这是可随模块交付的服务端代码，要求 Node.js 24。生成器把本目录复制到生成包的 `sdk/node/`。不依赖 npm 安装，不导入主框架内部 Python，也不包含浏览器 SDK。

稳定边界：

测试辅助文件 `graph-testing.mjs` 提供 `assertTransition(graph, machineId, before, event, after)` 与 `assertAtomResult(graph, atomId, inputs, output, validate)`。先运行真实业务函数，再用实际结果核对状态边与原子输入输出。后端原子另以第六个参数传入实际 manifest，按能力 ID 和版本读取 Schema。`validate(schema, value)` 校验失败时应抛出异常。模板的 HTTP/SQLite 测试已把真实提交、审核结果与状态图核对，权限及数据库结果仍分别断言。

| 接口 | 职责 |
| --- | --- |
| `createModuleServer({manifest, registration, token, handlers, workflowHandlers})` | 固定 HTTP 路径、恒定时间 Bearer 校验、64 KiB 输入、invoke 1.1 / workflow 1.0 包络、能力输入输出校验和安全错误；通过真实函数表分派 |
| `createRegistration(...)` | 启动时检查函数完整覆盖；握手时核对平台支持与完整声明后确认。不会调用业务函数 |
| `createAccessClient({callbackUrl, timeoutMs, fetchImpl})` | 向服务端配置的固定中央地址请求授权；票据缺失、超时、重定向、异常或无效回复均拒绝 |
| `Store.transaction(callback)` | 单连接串行任务及 SQLite 原子提交；业务对象和成功回执在同一事务中保存 |
| `Store.receipt/saveReceipt` | 请求编号绑定整个请求指纹，不包含传输头；重放前由业务重新授权 |
| `Store.cursor/offset` | HMAC 游标与调用人、授权修订、目录快照绑定；游标本身不授予权限 |

`invoke({body,ticket})` 返回 `{summary,data}`。`workflow({body,operation,ticket})` 返回适配器 `data`。`ticket` 只在当前内存调用栈使用，不返回、记录、写入仓储或传给前端。`facts` 必须来自服务端仓储；本 SDK 不会把输入自动变成可信事实。

`compile` 是明确封闭的 Schema 子集，支持本模板所需的 type、properties、required、布尔形式 additionalProperties、items、长度/数值范围、uniqueItems、pattern、enum、const 及说明字段。它不是完整 JSON Schema 引擎；Schema 形式的 additionalProperties、`$ref`、组合条件、format 等未支持内容在启动时拒绝。字段只按对象自身属性匹配，constructor、toString 和 __proto__ 不具有特殊验证能力。业务需要完整 Draft 2020-12 时，应以锁定版本的成熟验证库替换此适配点，并保留测试；不能静默忽略不认识的约束。

当前事务参考只面向一个 Node 进程和本地 SQLite。不要把持锁期间的中央回调扩展成长计算或外部写入；分布式副作用需要 outbox/inbox、领域幂等和恢复设计。本 SDK 不提供登录、角色引擎、游客、SSO、通知、长期委派、文件存储、长任务或聊天分支；这些能力不能通过添加同名配置字段声称实现。

含网络授权的对象/列表读取不应排入全局写事务队列。业务可使用独立只读连接取得已提交快照，再按当前权限和修订复检；明确限制并发、期限、候选分页与排队。参考 Store 仍是串行写事务工具，不会自动提供这种读调度。

SDK 升级随包显式发布，不从网络动态加载代码。已有独立服务可以只复用 `access-client.mjs` 和固定传输边界，保留原数据库与业务事务；不要求迁移到参考 `Store`。
# Registered source reads

`source-read-client.mjs` is a server-only optional `source-reads-1.0` callback
client. Configure the exact platform `/api/v1/access/source-reads` URL. Pass the
current target request ticket and repository facts; never forward that ticket to
a source module or retain tickets in business data. The result remains subject
to the consumer's independent category, purpose and field adapters.

`SourceReadError` exposes only a safe `code` and HTTP `status`. Preserve the
distinction between authorization (403), version conflict (409), contract (422)
and unavailability (503); do not turn every source failure into permission denial.
Private upstream messages, headers and tickets are never copied into the error.
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/access-client.mjs

```javascript
/** Trusted server-side client. Never import this into a browser bundle. */
export function createAccessClient({
  callbackUrl,
  timeoutMs = 4000,
  fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch,
}) {
  const endpoint = new URL(callbackUrl);
  if (
    !["http:", "https:"].includes(endpoint.protocol) ||
    endpoint.username ||
    endpoint.password ||
    endpoint.hash ||
    endpoint.search ||
    endpoint.pathname !== "/api/v1/access/remote-decisions"
  ) {
    throw new Error("Invalid configured access-decision endpoint");
  }
  if (!Number.isInteger(timeoutMs) || timeoutMs < 1 || timeoutMs > 10000) {
    throw new Error("Access decision timeout must be 1-10000 milliseconds");
  }
  // callbackUrl comes exclusively from deployment configuration. No URL, actor,
  // role, token destination or fetch options are accepted from invocation input.
  return async function decideAccess({
    ticket,
    requestId,
    operation,
    permission,
    facts,
  }) {
    const deny = (reason) => ({ allowed: false, reason, policy_revision: 0 });
    if (
      typeof ticket !== "string" ||
      ticket.length === 0 ||
      ticket.length > 12288
    )
      return deny("access_ticket_invalid");
    let body;
    try {
      body = JSON.stringify({
        request_id: requestId,
        operation,
        permission,
        facts,
      });
    } catch {
      return deny("access_decision_contract");
    }
    if (Buffer.byteLength(body, "utf8") > 16384)
      return deny("access_decision_size");
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
    try {
      const response = await fetchImpl(endpoint, {
        method: "POST",
        redirect: "error",
        signal: controller.signal,
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          "X-Platform-Access": ticket,
        },
        body,
      });
      if (!response.ok) return deny("access_decision_unavailable");
      // Read bounded bytes rather than response.json() on an unbounded body.
      const reader = response.body.getReader();
      const chunks = [];
      let size = 0;
      while (true) {
        const { value, done } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        size += value.byteLength;
        if (size > 4096) {
          await reader.cancel();
          return deny("access_decision_response_size");
        }
        chunks.push(Buffer.from(value));
      }
      const result = JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString("utf8"));
      if (
        typeof result.allowed !== "boolean" ||
        typeof result.reason !== "string" ||
        !Number.isInteger(result.policy_revision) ||
        result.policy_revision < 0 ||
        (result.allowed &&
          (result.reason !== "allowed" || result.policy_revision < 1))
      )
        return deny("access_decision_invalid");
      return {
        allowed: result.allowed,
        reason: result.reason,
        policy_revision: result.policy_revision,
      };
    } catch {
      return deny("access_decision_unavailable");
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  };
}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/ai-service.mjs

```javascript
/** Server-only AI services v1. No credentials enter registrations or business receipts.
 * One process owns dataDir. The local key protects a copied config, not an attacker
 * who reads the whole private directory; restrict its ACL on Windows as well.
 */
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { randomBytes, createCipheriv, createDecipheriv } from "node:crypto";
import { isIP } from "node:net";

export class AIServiceError extends Error {
  constructor(code, status = 503) {
    super(code);
    this.code = code;
    this.status = status;
  }
}
const need = (ok, code = "ai_configuration", status = 422) => {
  if (!ok) throw new AIServiceError(code, status);
};
const plain = (value) => value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value);
const identifier = (value) => typeof value === "string" && /^[a-z][a-z0-9_.-]{2,119}$/.test(value);
const scopeIdentifier = (value) => typeof value === "string" && /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,159}$/.test(value);
function factsCheck(value) {
  const factString = (entry) => typeof entry === "string" && [...entry].length > 0 && [...entry].length <= 160;
  return plain(value) && Object.keys(value).length <= 32 && Object.entries(value).every(([key, item]) =>
    /^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,79}$/.test(key) && (factString(item) || Array.isArray(item) && item.length > 0 && item.length <= 20 &&
      item.every(factString) && new Set(item).size === item.length));
}
const PUBLIC = ["id", "name", "base_url", "model", "enabled", "temperature", "max_tokens", "timeout_seconds"];
const PROFILE_FIELDS = new Set([...PUBLIC.filter((key) => key !== "id"), "api_key", "clear_api_key"]);
const FINISH = new Set(["stop", "length", "max_tokens", "tool_calls", "content_filter", "function_call"]);
const REDACTED = "【密钥已隐藏】";
function redact(value, secrets) {
  if (typeof value === "string") {
    for (const secret of [...new Set(secrets)].filter(Boolean).sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length))
      value = value.split(secret).join(REDACTED);
    return value;
  }
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((child) => redact(child, secrets));
  if (plain(value)) return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value).map(([key, child]) => [redact(key, secrets), redact(child, secrets)]));
  return value;
}
function baseURL(value, originOnly = false) {
  need(typeof value === "string" && value.length <= 2048 && !/[\s\\%?#]/u.test(value), "ai_configuration");
  let url;
  try { url = new URL(value); } catch { throw new AIServiceError("ai_configuration", 422); }
  const host = url.hostname.replace(/^\[|\]$/g, "").toLowerCase();
  const loopback = host === "localhost" || host === "::1" || (isIP(host) === 4 && host.split(".")[0] === "127");
  need(["http:", "https:"].includes(url.protocol) && !url.username && !url.password &&
    (url.protocol === "https:" || loopback) && (!originOnly || url.pathname === "/"), "ai_configuration");
  return (originOnly ? url.origin : url.href).replace(/\/$/, "");
}
function text(value, maximum) {
  need(typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0 && value.length <= maximum && !/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/u.test(value));
  return value.trim();
}
function number(value, min, max, integer = false) {
  need(typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) && value >= min && value <= max && (!integer || Number.isSafeInteger(value)));
  return value;
}
function messagesCheck(messages) {
  need(Array.isArray(messages) && messages.length > 0 && messages.length <= 64 && messages.every((entry) =>
    plain(entry) && Object.keys(entry).every((key) => ["role", "content"].includes(key)) &&
    ["system", "developer", "user", "assistant"].includes(entry.role) && typeof entry.content === "string" && [...entry.content].length <= 16000), "ai_request", 422);
  need(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(messages)) <= 60000, "ai_request_size", 413);
}
function resultCheck(value) {
  need(plain(value) && typeof value.content === "string" && typeof value.model === "string" &&
    Array.isArray(value.tool_calls) && value.tool_calls.length === 0 &&
    Object.keys(value).every((key) => ["content", "model", "tool_calls", "finish_reason", "usage"].includes(key)) &&
    (!Object.hasOwn(value, "finish_reason") || FINISH.has(value.finish_reason)) &&
    (!Object.hasOwn(value, "usage") || (plain(value.usage) && Object.entries(value.usage).every(([key, count]) =>
      ["prompt_tokens", "completion_tokens", "total_tokens"].includes(key) && Number.isSafeInteger(count) && count >= 0))), "ai_response", 502);
  return value;
}

export class AIServiceManager {
  #services; #directory; #filename; #keyfile; #platform; #fetch;
  constructor({ services, dataDir, platformBaseUrl = null, fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch }) {
    need(Array.isArray(services) && services.length > 0 && services.length <= 16, "ai_configuration", 500);
    need(services.every((service) => plain(service) && identifier(service.id) &&
      typeof service.title === "string" && service.title.length > 0 && [...service.title].length <= 200 &&
      typeof service.description === "string" && service.description.length > 0 && [...service.description].length <= 2000 &&
      Array.isArray(service.capability_ids) && service.capability_ids.length > 0 && service.capability_ids.length <= 64 &&
      service.capability_ids.every(identifier) && new Set(service.capability_ids).size === service.capability_ids.length &&
      Array.isArray(service.supported_sources) && service.supported_sources.length > 0 &&
      service.supported_sources.every((source) => ["platform", "module"].includes(source)) &&
      new Set(service.supported_sources).size === service.supported_sources.length &&
      Object.keys(service).sort().join() === "capability_ids,description,id,supported_sources,title") &&
      new Set(services.map((service) => service.id)).size === services.length, "ai_configuration", 500);
    need(typeof dataDir === "string" && dataDir.length > 0 && typeof fetchImpl === "function", "ai_configuration", 500);
    this.#services = new Map(services.map((service) => [service.id, structuredClone(service)]));
    this.#directory = path.resolve(dataDir);
    fs.mkdirSync(this.#directory, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
    this.#filename = path.join(this.#directory, "module-ai.config.enc");
    this.#keyfile = path.join(this.#directory, "module-ai.key");
    this.#platform = platformBaseUrl === null ? null : baseURL(platformBaseUrl, true);
    this.#fetch = fetchImpl;
    this.#read();
  }
  get services() { return structuredClone([...this.#services.values()]); }
  get platformConnected() { return this.#platform !== null; }
  #service(id) { need(this.#services.has(id), "ai_service_unknown", 404); return this.#services.get(id); }
  #key(create = false) {
    try {
      if (create && !fs.existsSync(this.#keyfile)) {
        const descriptor = fs.openSync(this.#keyfile, "wx", 0o600);
        try { fs.writeFileSync(descriptor, randomBytes(32)); fs.fsyncSync(descriptor); } finally { fs.closeSync(descriptor); }
      }
      const key = fs.readFileSync(this.#keyfile);
      need(key.length === 32, "ai_secret_unavailable", 503);
      return key;
    } catch { throw new AIServiceError("ai_secret_unavailable"); }
  }
  #read() {
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.#filename)) return { version: 1, services: {} };
    try {
      const buffer = fs.readFileSync(this.#filename);
      need(buffer.length >= 29 && buffer.length <= 1048576, "ai_config_corrupt", 503);
      const decipher = createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm", this.#key(), buffer.subarray(0, 12));
      decipher.setAuthTag(buffer.subarray(12, 28));
      const state = JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(buffer.subarray(28)), decipher.final()]).toString("utf8"));
      need(state.version === 1 && plain(state.services) && Object.values(state.services).every((entry) =>
        plain(entry) && Number.isSafeInteger(entry.revision) && entry.revision >= 1 && plain(entry.profile)), "ai_config_corrupt", 503);
      return state;
    } catch { throw new AIServiceError("ai_config_corrupt"); }
  }
  #write(state) {
    const temporary = path.join(this.#directory, `.module-ai-${randomBytes(12).toString("hex")}`);
    try {
      const iv = randomBytes(12), cipher = createCipheriv("aes-256-gcm", this.#key(true), iv);
      const payload = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(state), "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
      const descriptor = fs.openSync(temporary, "wx", 0o600);
      try { fs.writeFileSync(descriptor, Buffer.concat([iv, cipher.getAuthTag(), payload])); fs.fsyncSync(descriptor); } finally { fs.closeSync(descriptor); }
      fs.renameSync(temporary, this.#filename);
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof AIServiceError) throw error;
      throw new AIServiceError("ai_config_write_failed");
    } finally { if (fs.existsSync(temporary)) fs.unlinkSync(temporary); }
  }
  #secrets(state = this.#read()) { return Object.values(state.services).map((entry) => entry.profile.api_key).filter(Boolean); }
  getConfig(serviceId) {
    this.#service(serviceId);
    const entry = this.#read().services[serviceId];
    return entry ? { revision: entry.revision, profile: { ...Object.fromEntries(PUBLIC.map((key) => [key, entry.profile[key]])), api_key_configured: Boolean(entry.profile.api_key) } } : { revision: 0, profile: null };
  }
  putConfig(serviceId, { expected_revision, profile } = {}) {
    this.#service(serviceId);
    const state = this.#read(), previous = state.services[serviceId] || { revision: 0, profile: {} };
    need(Number.isSafeInteger(expected_revision) && expected_revision >= 0 && plain(profile) &&
      Object.keys(profile).every((key) => PROFILE_FIELDS.has(key)) &&
      (!Object.hasOwn(profile, "clear_api_key") || typeof profile.clear_api_key === "boolean"), "ai_configuration", 422);
    need(expected_revision === previous.revision, "ai_config_revision_changed", 409);
    const merged = { enabled: true, temperature: 0.2, max_tokens: 2048, timeout_seconds: 45, ...previous.profile, ...profile };
    need(typeof merged.enabled === "boolean");
    const stored = { id: serviceId, name: text(merged.name, 120), base_url: baseURL(merged.base_url), model: text(merged.model, 200), enabled: merged.enabled,
      temperature: number(merged.temperature, 0, 2), max_tokens: number(merged.max_tokens, 1, 131072, true), timeout_seconds: number(merged.timeout_seconds, 1, 300) };
    need(profile.api_key === undefined || (typeof profile.api_key === "string" && profile.api_key.length <= 8192));
    const supplied = (profile.api_key || "").trim();
    need(!/[^\x21-\x7e]/u.test(supplied) && !(profile.clear_api_key && supplied));
    need(!previous.profile.api_key || previous.profile.base_url === stored.base_url || supplied || profile.clear_api_key, "ai_destination_changed", 422);
    const secret = supplied || (profile.clear_api_key ? "" : previous.profile.api_key || "");
    const secrets = [...this.#secrets(state), supplied].filter(Boolean);
    need(!secrets.some((key) => Object.values(stored).some((value) => String(value).includes(key))), "ai_configuration", 422);
    need(!supplied || !Object.values(state.services).some((entry) => PUBLIC.some((key) => String(entry.profile[key]).includes(supplied))), "ai_configuration", 422);
    state.services[serviceId] = { revision: previous.revision + 1, profile: { ...stored, api_key: secret } };
    this.#write(state);
    return this.getConfig(serviceId);
  }
  async #request(url, payload, headers, timeout, provider = false) {
    const body = JSON.stringify(payload);
    need(Buffer.byteLength(body) <= (provider || url.endsWith("/chat") ? 65536 : 16384), "ai_request_size", 413);
    const controller = new AbortController(), timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeout * 1000);
    try {
      const response = await this.#fetch(url, { method: "POST", redirect: "error", signal: controller.signal,
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "Accept-Encoding": "identity", ...headers }, body });
      if (!response.ok) {
        await response.body?.cancel();
        const status = response.status;
        throw new AIServiceError(provider ? (status === 401 || status === 403 ? "ai_provider_auth" : status === 429 ? "ai_provider_limited" : "ai_provider_failed") : "ai_platform_rejected", provider ? 502 : status >= 400 && status <= 599 ? status : 502);
      }
      need(!response.headers.get("content-encoding") || response.headers.get("content-encoding") === "identity", "ai_response", 502);
      const reader = response.body.getReader();
      let size = 0; const chunks = [];
      while (true) {
        const { value, done } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        size += value.byteLength;
        if (size > 4194304) { await reader.cancel(); throw new AIServiceError("ai_response_size", 502); }
        chunks.push(Buffer.from(value));
      }
      return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString("utf8"));
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof AIServiceError) throw error;
      throw new AIServiceError(provider ? "ai_provider_unavailable" : "ai_platform_unavailable");
    } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
  }
  #scope(scope, serviceId) {
    need(plain(scope) && typeof scope.ticket === "string" && scope.ticket.length > 0 && scope.ticket.length <= 12288 &&
      scopeIdentifier(scope.requestId) && scopeIdentifier(scope.operation) && scopeIdentifier(scope.permission) && factsCheck(scope.facts), "ai_access_scope", 403);
    return { service_id: serviceId, request_id: scope.requestId, operation: scope.operation, permission: scope.permission, facts: structuredClone(scope.facts) };
  }
  #route(route, serviceId) {
    need(plain(route) && route.service_id === serviceId && this.#service(serviceId).supported_sources.includes(route.source) &&
      Number.isSafeInteger(route.binding_revision) && route.binding_revision >= 1 &&
      (route.source === "platform" ? route.module_config_revision === null &&
        typeof route.platform_config_digest === "string" && /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(route.platform_config_digest)
        : Number.isSafeInteger(route.module_config_revision) && route.module_config_revision >= 0 && route.platform_config_digest === null), "ai_route_response", 502);
    return route;
  }
  async #ownChat(serviceId, messages, revision) {
    const entry = this.#read().services[serviceId];
    need(entry && entry.revision === revision, "ai_config_revision_changed", 409);
    need(entry.profile.enabled, "ai_service_disabled", 409);
    const profile = entry.profile, secrets = this.#secrets();
    const payload = await this.#request(profile.base_url + "/chat/completions", { model: profile.model, messages: redact(messages, secrets),
      temperature: profile.temperature, max_tokens: profile.max_tokens, stream: false }, profile.api_key ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${profile.api_key}` } : {}, profile.timeout_seconds, true);
    const choice = payload?.choices?.[0], message = choice?.message;
    need(plain(message) && (message.content === null || typeof message.content === "string") &&
      (!message.tool_calls || (Array.isArray(message.tool_calls) && message.tool_calls.length === 0)), "ai_response", 502);
    const result = { content: message.content || "", tool_calls: [], model: payload.model || profile.model };
    if (FINISH.has(choice.finish_reason)) result.finish_reason = choice.finish_reason;
    if (plain(payload.usage)) result.usage = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(payload.usage).filter(([key, value]) =>
      ["prompt_tokens", "completion_tokens", "total_tokens"].includes(key) && Number.isSafeInteger(value) && value >= 0));
    need(this.getConfig(serviceId).revision === revision, "ai_config_revision_changed", 409);
    return redact(resultCheck(result), [...secrets, ...this.#secrets()]);
  }
  async chatWithReceipt({ serviceId, messages, scope = null }) {
    const service = this.#service(serviceId);
    messagesCheck(messages);
    if (!this.#platform) {
      need(service.supported_sources.includes("module"), "ai_source_unsupported", 409);
      const revision = this.getConfig(serviceId).revision;
      const result = await this.#ownChat(serviceId, structuredClone(messages), revision);
      return { result, route: { service_id: serviceId, source: "module", binding_revision: null, module_config_revision: revision, platform_config_digest: null } };
    }
    const body = this.#scope(scope, serviceId), ticket = scope.ticket, headers = { "X-Platform-Access": ticket };
    messages = redact(structuredClone(messages), [ticket]);
    const route = this.#route(await this.#request(this.#platform + "/api/v1/ai/resolve", body, headers, 15), serviceId);
    const selected = { ...body, binding_revision: route.binding_revision, module_config_revision: route.module_config_revision, platform_config_digest: route.platform_config_digest };
    let result;
    if (route.source === "platform") {
      const answer = await this.#request(this.#platform + "/api/v1/ai/chat", { ...selected, messages: redact(messages, this.#secrets()) }, headers, 310);
      this.#route(answer, serviceId);
      need(answer.source === route.source && answer.binding_revision === route.binding_revision && answer.module_config_revision === route.module_config_revision && answer.platform_config_digest === route.platform_config_digest, "ai_binding_revision_changed", 409);
      result = resultCheck(answer.result);
    } else result = await this.#ownChat(serviceId, structuredClone(messages), route.module_config_revision);
    const after = this.#route(await this.#request(this.#platform + "/api/v1/ai/validate", selected, headers, 15), serviceId);
    need(after.source === route.source && after.binding_revision === route.binding_revision && after.module_config_revision === route.module_config_revision && after.platform_config_digest === route.platform_config_digest, "ai_binding_revision_changed", 409);
    if (route.source === "module") need(this.getConfig(serviceId).revision === route.module_config_revision, "ai_config_revision_changed", 409);
    const safe = redact(result, [...this.#secrets(), ticket]);
    return { result: resultCheck(safe), route: { service_id: serviceId, source: route.source,
      binding_revision: route.binding_revision, module_config_revision: route.module_config_revision, platform_config_digest: route.platform_config_digest } };
  }
  async chat(options) { return (await this.chatWithReceipt(options)).result; }
  /** Call again immediately before a business commit; chat's checks do not cover
   * later domain/source-read work. This is not a distributed atomic transaction. */
  async validate({ serviceId, scope = null, route }) {
    this.#service(serviceId);
    if (!this.#platform) {
      need(plain(route) && route.service_id === serviceId && route.source === "module" && route.binding_revision === null && route.platform_config_digest === null &&
        Number.isSafeInteger(route.module_config_revision) && this.getConfig(serviceId).revision === route.module_config_revision,
        "ai_config_revision_changed", 409);
      return structuredClone(route);
    }
    this.#route(route, serviceId);
    const selected = { ...this.#scope(scope, serviceId), binding_revision: route.binding_revision, module_config_revision: route.module_config_revision, platform_config_digest: route.platform_config_digest };
    const after = this.#route(await this.#request(this.#platform + "/api/v1/ai/validate", selected,
      { "X-Platform-Access": scope.ticket }, 15), serviceId);
    need(after.source === route.source && after.binding_revision === route.binding_revision && after.module_config_revision === route.module_config_revision && after.platform_config_digest === route.platform_config_digest,
      "ai_binding_revision_changed", 409);
    if (route.source === "module") need(this.getConfig(serviceId).revision === route.module_config_revision, "ai_config_revision_changed", 409);
    return { service_id: serviceId, source: after.source, binding_revision: after.binding_revision, module_config_revision: after.module_config_revision, platform_config_digest: after.platform_config_digest };
  }
  async testConnection(serviceId, expectedRevision) {
    this.#service(serviceId);
    const revision = this.getConfig(serviceId).revision;
    need(Number.isSafeInteger(expectedRevision) && expectedRevision === revision, "ai_config_revision_changed", 409);
    const started = performance.now();
    const result = await this.#ownChat(serviceId, [{ role: "user", content: "This is a synthetic connection test. Reply with CONNECTION_OK only." }], revision);
    return { ok: true, message: "连接成功，已收到供应商响应。", latency_ms: Math.round(performance.now() - started), model: result.model, response_preview: result.content.slice(0, 240) };
  }
  /** Call only AFTER the HTTP adapter verifies its private service Bearer. */
  async control(serviceId, method, action, body = {}) {
    if (action === "config" && method === "GET") return this.getConfig(serviceId);
    if (action === "config" && method === "PUT") {
      need(plain(body) && Object.keys(body).sort().join() === "expected_revision,profile");
      return this.putConfig(serviceId, body);
    }
    if (action === "test" && method === "POST") {
      need(plain(body) && Object.keys(body).join() === "expected_revision");
      return this.testConnection(serviceId, body.expected_revision);
    }
    throw new AIServiceError("ai_control_not_found", 404);
  }
}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/ai-service.test.mjs

```javascript
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { AIServiceManager, AIServiceError } from "./ai-service.mjs";
import { createModuleServer } from "./runtime.mjs";
import { createRegistration } from "./registration.mjs";

// These are deterministic transport tests, never evidence of a real model call.
const services = [{ id: "sample.ai", title: "合成分析", description: "SDK 回归", capability_ids: ["sample.analyze"], supported_sources: ["platform", "module"] }];
const profile = { name: "Synthetic provider", base_url: "https://model.example/v1", model: "synthetic-model", api_key: "SYNTHETIC_PRIVATE_API_KEY_12345", enabled: true, temperature: 0.2, max_tokens: 32, timeout_seconds: 2 };
const messages = [{ role: "user", content: "合成输入" }];
const scope = { ticket: "synthetic-access-ticket-value", requestId: "a".repeat(32), operation: "invoke", permission: "sample.analyze", facts: { project_id: "P" } };
const providerResult = (content = "synthetic answer") => ({ model: "synthetic-model", choices: [{ message: { content }, finish_reason: "stop" }], usage: { total_tokens: 8 } });
function fixture(t, options = {}) {
  const parent = path.resolve(process.env.MODULE_AI_TEST_DIR || os.tmpdir());
  fs.mkdirSync(parent, { recursive: true });
  const dataDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(parent, "module-ai-test-"));
  t.after(() => {
    assert.equal(path.dirname(path.resolve(dataDir)), parent);
    assert.equal(path.basename(dataDir).startsWith("module-ai-test-"), true);
    fs.rmSync(dataDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  });
  const manager = new AIServiceManager({ services, dataDir, ...options });
  return { manager, dataDir };
}
function configured(t, options = {}) {
  const value = fixture(t, options);
  value.manager.putConfig("sample.ai", { expected_revision: 0, profile });
  return value;
}
const throwsCode = (code, status) => (error) => error instanceof AIServiceError && error.code === code && (status === undefined || error.status === status);

test("encrypted config restarts, CAS, no plaintext or key in public output", async (t) => {
  const { manager, dataDir } = configured(t);
  const publicConfig = manager.getConfig("sample.ai");
  assert.equal(publicConfig.profile.api_key_configured, true);
  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(publicConfig).includes(profile.api_key), false);
  assert.equal(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dataDir, "module-ai.config.enc")).includes(Buffer.from(profile.api_key)), false);
  const restarted = new AIServiceManager({ services, dataDir });
  assert.deepEqual(restarted.getConfig("sample.ai"), publicConfig);
  assert.throws(() => manager.putConfig("sample.ai", { expected_revision: 0, profile }), throwsCode("ai_config_revision_changed", 409));
  assert.throws(() => manager.putConfig("sample.ai", { expected_revision: 1, profile: { base_url: "https://other.example/v1" } }), throwsCode("ai_destination_changed"));
  assert.throws(() => manager.putConfig("sample.ai", { expected_revision: 1, profile: { name: profile.api_key } }), throwsCode("ai_configuration"));
  assert.equal(manager.putConfig("sample.ai", { expected_revision: 1, profile: { clear_api_key: true } }).profile.api_key_configured, false);
});

test("standalone uses its own API, redacts credential before sending and returning", async (t) => {
  let calls = 0;
  const { manager } = configured(t, { fetchImpl: async (url, options) => {
    calls++;
    assert.equal(url, profile.base_url + "/chat/completions");
    assert.equal(options.headers.Authorization, "Bearer " + profile.api_key);
    assert.equal(options.body.includes(profile.api_key), false);
    return Response.json(providerResult("answer " + profile.api_key));
  } });
  const { result, route } = await manager.chatWithReceipt({ serviceId: "sample.ai", messages: [{ role: "user", content: profile.api_key }] });
  assert.equal(calls, 1);
  assert.equal(result.content, "answer 【密钥已隐藏】");
  assert.equal(route.binding_revision, null);
  await manager.validate({ serviceId: "sample.ai", route });
  manager.putConfig("sample.ai", { expected_revision: 1, profile: { model: "changed-model" } });
  await assert.rejects(manager.validate({ serviceId: "sample.ai", route }), throwsCode("ai_config_revision_changed", 409));
});

for (const source of ["platform", "module"]) test(`connected ${source} selection executes only that source and validates receipt`, async (t) => {
  const calls = [];
  const route = { service_id: "sample.ai", source, binding_revision: 7, module_config_revision: source === "module" ? 1 : null, platform_config_digest: source === "platform" ? "f".repeat(64) : null };
  const { manager } = configured(t, { platformBaseUrl: "http://127.0.0.1:19000", fetchImpl: async (url, options) => {
    calls.push(new URL(url).pathname);
    if (new URL(url).hostname === "model.example") return Response.json(providerResult());
    assert.equal(options.headers["X-Platform-Access"], scope.ticket);
    const body = JSON.parse(options.body);
    assert.equal(body.permission, scope.permission);
    assert.deepEqual(body.facts, scope.facts);
    return Response.json(url.endsWith("/chat") ? { ...route, result: { content: "platform answer", model: "platform-model", tool_calls: [] } } : route);
  } });
  const receipt = await manager.chatWithReceipt({ serviceId: "sample.ai", messages, scope });
  assert.deepEqual(receipt.route, route);
  assert.equal(receipt.result.content, source === "module" ? "synthetic answer" : "platform answer");
  assert.deepEqual(calls, source === "platform" ? ["/api/v1/ai/resolve", "/api/v1/ai/chat", "/api/v1/ai/validate"] : ["/api/v1/ai/resolve", "/v1/chat/completions", "/api/v1/ai/validate"]);
  await manager.validate({ serviceId: "sample.ai", route, scope });
  assert.equal(calls.at(-1), "/api/v1/ai/validate");
});

test("connected has no standalone fallback when ticket or central service is missing", async (t) => {
  let calls = 0;
  const { manager } = configured(t, { platformBaseUrl: "http://localhost:19000", fetchImpl: async () => { calls++; throw new Error(profile.api_key); } });
  await assert.rejects(manager.chat({ serviceId: "sample.ai", messages }), throwsCode("ai_access_scope", 403));
  assert.equal(calls, 0);
  await assert.rejects(manager.chat({ serviceId: "sample.ai", messages, scope }), throwsCode("ai_platform_unavailable"));
  assert.equal(calls, 1);
});

test("in-flight local profile update discards the old provider answer", async (t) => {
  let manager;
  ({ manager } = configured(t, { fetchImpl: async () => {
    manager.putConfig("sample.ai", { expected_revision: 1, profile: { model: "new-model" } });
    return Response.json(providerResult());
  } }));
  await assert.rejects(manager.chat({ serviceId: "sample.ai", messages }), throwsCode("ai_config_revision_changed", 409));
});

for (const rejection of ["binding", "grant"]) test(`post-call ${rejection} change denies returned result`, async (t) => {
  const route = { service_id: "sample.ai", source: "module", binding_revision: 3, module_config_revision: 1, platform_config_digest: null };
  const { manager } = configured(t, { platformBaseUrl: "http://localhost:19000", fetchImpl: async (url) => {
    if (url.endsWith("/resolve")) return Response.json(route);
    if (url.endsWith("/validate")) return rejection === "grant" ? Response.json({ error: { message: profile.api_key } }, { status: 403 }) : Response.json({ ...route, binding_revision: 4 });
    return Response.json(providerResult());
  } });
  await assert.rejects(manager.chat({ serviceId: "sample.ai", messages, scope }), throwsCode(rejection === "grant" ? "ai_platform_rejected" : "ai_binding_revision_changed", rejection === "grant" ? 403 : 409));
});

test("module route pins config revision before provider network I/O", async (t) => {
  let calls = 0;
  const { manager } = configured(t, { platformBaseUrl: "http://localhost:19000", fetchImpl: async () => {
    calls++;
    return Response.json({ service_id: "sample.ai", source: "module", binding_revision: 1, module_config_revision: 2, platform_config_digest: null });
  } });
  await assert.rejects(manager.chat({ serviceId: "sample.ai", messages, scope }), throwsCode("ai_config_revision_changed", 409));
  assert.equal(calls, 1);
});

for (const status of [302, 401, 403, 429, 500]) test(`provider ${status} cannot leak its error body or trigger a second call`, async (t) => {
  let calls = 0;
  const { manager } = configured(t, { fetchImpl: async () => {
    calls++; return Response.json({ error: { message: profile.api_key } }, { status });
  } });
  await assert.rejects(manager.chat({ serviceId: "sample.ai", messages }), (error) => error instanceof AIServiceError && !error.message.includes(profile.api_key));
  assert.equal(calls, 1);
});

test("control test uses fixed synthetic prompt and config CAS; no unknown controls", async (t) => {
  const { manager } = configured(t, { fetchImpl: async () => Response.json(providerResult()) });
  const answer = await manager.control("sample.ai", "POST", "test", { expected_revision: 1 });
  assert.equal(answer.ok, true);
  await assert.rejects(manager.control("sample.ai", "POST", "test", {}), throwsCode("ai_configuration"));
  await assert.rejects(manager.control("sample.ai", "POST", "config", {}), throwsCode("ai_control_not_found"));
});

test("runtime requires real AI manager, service Bearer protects configuration", async (t) => {
  const { manager } = fixture(t);
  const schema = { type: "object", properties: {}, additionalProperties: false };
  const manifest = { protocol_version: "1.2", module: { id: "sample", version: "1.0.0" }, capabilities: [{ id: "sample.analyze", version: "1.0.0", input_schema: schema, output_schema: schema }] };
  const registration = { registration_version: "1.0", module_id: "sample", module_version: "1.0.0", workflow_operations: [], permissions: { mode: "platform_access" }, ai_services: services };
  const arguments_ = { manifest, registration, token: "synthetic-private-service-token", handlers: { "sample.analyze": () => ({ summary: "done", data: {} }) } };
  assert.throws(() => createModuleServer(arguments_), /AI/);
  const server = createModuleServer({ ...arguments_, aiManager: manager });
  await new Promise((resolve) => server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", resolve));
  t.after(() => new Promise((resolve) => server.close(resolve)));
  const url = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}/platform/v1/ai/services/sample.ai/config`;
  assert.equal((await fetch(url)).status, 401);
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${arguments_.token}` } });
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  assert.deepEqual(await response.json(), { revision: 0, profile: null });
  const handshake = await fetch(url.replace(/ai\/services\/sample.ai\/config$/, "handshake"), {
    method: "POST", headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${arguments_.token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: "{}",
  });
  assert.equal(handshake.status, 409);
  assert.equal((await handshake.json()).error.code, "ai_platform_not_configured");
});

for (const change of ["digest", "grant"]) test(`final commit guard rejects later platform ${change} change`, async (t) => {
  let changed = false, committed = false;
  const route = { service_id: "sample.ai", source: "platform", binding_revision: 1, module_config_revision: null, platform_config_digest: "a".repeat(64) };
  const { manager } = configured(t, { platformBaseUrl: "http://localhost:19000", fetchImpl: async (url, options) => {
    if (url.endsWith("/chat")) {
      assert.equal(JSON.parse(options.body).platform_config_digest, route.platform_config_digest);
      return Response.json({ ...route, result: { content: "answer", model: "platform-model", tool_calls: [] } });
    }
    if (changed && change === "grant") return Response.json({}, { status: 403 });
    return Response.json(changed ? { ...route, platform_config_digest: "b".repeat(64) } : route);
  } });
  const receipt = await manager.chatWithReceipt({ serviceId: "sample.ai", messages, scope });
  changed = true; // Domain/source-read work happened after chat, before commit.
  await assert.rejects((async () => {
    await manager.validate({ serviceId: "sample.ai", scope, route: receipt.route });
    committed = true;
  })(), throwsCode(change === "grant" ? "ai_platform_rejected" : "ai_binding_revision_changed"));
  assert.equal(committed, false);
});

test("text contract is checked locally before any outbound request", async (t) => {
  let calls = 0;
  const { manager } = configured(t, { fetchImpl: async () => { calls++; return Response.json(providerResult()); } });
  for (const input of [Array.from({ length: 65 }, () => messages[0]), [{ role: "user", content: "x".repeat(16001) }], [{ role: "user", content: "x", tools: [] }]])
    await assert.rejects(manager.chat({ serviceId: "sample.ai", messages: input }), throwsCode("ai_request"));
  assert.equal(calls, 0);
});

test("AI declaration automatically negotiates its feature; old modules do not", () => {
  const schema = { type: "object" };
  const manifest = { protocol_version: "1.2", module: { id: "sample", version: "1.0.0" }, capabilities: [{ id: "sample.analyze", input_schema: schema, output_schema: schema }] };
  const registration = { registration_version: "1.0", module_id: "sample", module_version: "1.0.0", workflow_operations: [], permissions: { mode: "platform_access" }, ai_services: services };
  const registrar = createRegistration({ manifest, registration, handlers: { "sample.analyze": () => {} }, compile: () => {} });
  const offer = { registration_version: "1.0", nonce: "a".repeat(32), contract_digest: "b".repeat(64), manifest, registration,
    platform_features: ["invoke-1.1", "shared-hosts-1.0", "registration-1.0", "access-1.0"] };
  assert.throws(() => registrar.confirm(offer), { code: "registration_contract" });
  offer.platform_features.push("ai-services-1.0");
  assert.equal(registrar.confirm(offer).accepted, true);
  assert.throws(() => createRegistration({ manifest, registration: { ...registration, ai_services: [{ ...services[0], capability_ids: ["sample.missing"] }] }, handlers: { "sample.analyze": () => {} }, compile: () => {} }), { code: "registration_contract" });
});
```
