### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/atoms.mjs

```javascript
/** Associate actual dispatch functions with stable atom identities.
 * Structural and host validation belongs to module_kit check/admission. This
 * startup binding check never invokes a handler or grants user permissions.
 */
export function createAtomBindings({ graph, manifest, handlers }) {
  const need = (value) => {
    if (!value)
      throw Object.assign(new Error("原子登记与实际函数绑定不一致。"), {
        code: "atomic_binding",
        status: 422,
      });
  };
  need(
    graph?.graph_version === "1.0" &&
      graph.module_id === manifest.module.id &&
      graph.module_version === manifest.module.version,
  );
  const capabilities = new Map(
    manifest.capabilities.map((cap) => [cap.id, cap]),
  );
  const backends = graph.atoms.filter((atom) => atom.kind === "backend");
  const bindings = new Map(
    backends.map((atom) => [atom.capability.id, structuredClone(atom)]),
  );
  need(
    bindings.size === backends.length &&
      bindings.size === capabilities.size &&
      Object.keys(handlers).length === capabilities.size,
  );
  for (const [id, capability] of capabilities) {
    need(
      Object.hasOwn(handlers, id) &&
        typeof handlers[id] === "function" &&
        bindings.get(id)?.capability.version === capability.version,
    );
  }
  const document = structuredClone(graph);
  return Object.freeze({
    get document() {
      return structuredClone(document);
    },
    forCapability(id) {
      need(bindings.has(id));
      return structuredClone(bindings.get(id));
    },
  });
}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/graph-testing.mjs

```javascript
/** Assertions over actual handler results; no transition is executed here. */
import assert from "node:assert/strict";

export function assertTransition(graph, machineId, before, event, after) {
  const machines = graph.state_machines.filter((item) => item.id === machineId);
  assert.equal(machines.length, 1, `Expected one state machine: ${machineId}`);
  assert.deepEqual(
    before.object_ref,
    after.object_ref,
    "The object changed between reads",
  );
  assert.ok(
    machines[0].transitions.some(
      (edge) =>
        edge.from === before.state &&
        edge.event === event &&
        edge.to === after.state,
    ),
    `Observed transition is absent from the graph: ${before.state} -> ${event} -> ${after.state}`,
  );
}

/** Supply the same Schema validator used by the application or test harness. */
export function assertAtomResult(
  graph,
  atomId,
  inputs,
  output,
  validate,
  manifest = null,
) {
  const atoms = graph.atoms.filter((item) => item.id === atomId);
  assert.equal(atoms.length, 1, `Expected one atom: ${atomId}`);
  let contract = atoms[0];
  if (contract.kind === "backend") {
    const matching = (manifest?.capabilities || []).filter(
      (capability) =>
        capability.id === contract.capability.id &&
        capability.version === contract.capability.version,
    );
    assert.equal(
      matching.length,
      1,
      "Backend atom requires its actual manifest capability",
    );
    contract = matching[0];
  }
  validate(contract.input_schema, inputs);
  validate(contract.output_schema, output);
}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/observation.mjs

```javascript
/** Server-side observation queue. Business handlers never wait for delivery. */
export function createObservationClient({
  callbackUrl,
  graph,
  fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch,
  timeoutMs = 1000,
}) {
  const endpoint = new URL(callbackUrl);
  if (
    !["http:", "https:"].includes(endpoint.protocol) ||
    endpoint.username ||
    endpoint.password ||
    endpoint.hash ||
    endpoint.search ||
    endpoint.pathname !== "/api/v1/runtime-observations/events"
  )
    throw new Error("Invalid configured observation endpoint");
  if (!Number.isInteger(timeoutMs) || timeoutMs < 1 || timeoutMs > 2000)
    throw new Error(
      "Observation timeout must be between 1 and 2000 milliseconds",
    );
  const definition = structuredClone(graph);
  const active = new Set();
  let pending = 0;

  /** Keep a bounded queue and retry the same event identity at most once. */
  function deliver(ticket, batch, lost) {
    if (pending >= 64) {
      lost();
      return;
    }
    pending += 1;
    const task = (async () => {
      for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt += 1) {
        try {
          const response = await fetchImpl(endpoint, {
            method: "POST",
            redirect: "error",
            signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs),
            headers: {
              "Content-Type": "application/json",
              "X-DuoFlow-Observation": ticket,
            },
            body: JSON.stringify(batch),
          });
          const ok = response.ok;
          await response.body?.cancel();
          if (ok) return;
          if (response.status < 500) break;
        } catch {
          /* Delivery problems never change a business result. */
        }
      }
      lost();
    })().finally(() => {
      pending -= 1;
      active.delete(task);
    });
    active.add(task);
  }

  return {
    /** Admission uses the configured destination and a one-time challenge. */
    async probe(challenge) {
      if (
        !challenge ||
        !/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(challenge.ticket) ||
        !/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(challenge.nonce)
      )
        return false;
      try {
        const url = new URL(endpoint);
        url.pathname = "/api/v1/runtime-observations/probe";
        const response = await fetchImpl(url, {
          method: "POST",
          redirect: "error",
          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs),
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "X-DuoFlow-Observation": challenge.ticket,
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            runtime_version: "1.0",
            nonce: challenge.nonce,
          }),
        });
        if (!response.ok) {
          await response.body?.cancel();
          return false;
        }
        const reader = response.body.getReader();
        let data = "",
          size = 0;
        while (true) {
          const { value, done } = await reader.read();
          if (done) break;
          size += value.byteLength;
          if (size > 1024) {
            await reader.cancel();
            return false;
          }
          data += new TextDecoder().decode(value);
        }
        const result = JSON.parse(data);
        return result.accepted === true && result.nonce === challenge.nonce;
      } catch {
        return false;
      }
    },
    /** A request owns its sequence and correlation; tickets stay in memory. */
    session({ ticket, requestId, atomId }) {
      let sequence = 0,
        dropped = 0;
      const valid =
        typeof ticket === "string" &&
        /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(ticket) &&
        /^[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(requestId);
      const emit = (phase, state) => {
        if (!valid) return;
        if (sequence >= 256) {
          dropped = Math.min(256, dropped + 1);
          return;
        }
        const event = {
          sequence: ++sequence,
          atom_id: atomId,
          call_id: requestId,
          parent_call_id: null,
          phase,
        };
        if (state) event.state = structuredClone(state);
        deliver(
          ticket,
          {
            runtime_version: "1.0",
            request_id: requestId,
            graph_version: definition.version,
            dropped,
            events: [event],
          },
          () => {
            dropped = Math.min(256, dropped + 1);
          },
        );
      };
      return {
        mark: (phase) => {
          if (["started", "succeeded", "failed"].includes(phase)) emit(phase);
        },
        /** Call only after the module has committed or read this actual projection. */
        state(machineId, projection) {
          if (
            !projection?.object_ref ||
            !Number.isSafeInteger(projection.revision)
          )
            return;
          emit("state", {
            machine_id: machineId,
            value: projection.state,
            revision: projection.revision,
            object_ref: projection.object_ref,
          });
        },
        status: () => ({ sequence, dropped }),
      };
    },
    /** Optional shutdown/test hook; ordinary requests do not await it. */
    flush: () => Promise.allSettled([...active]),
  };
}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/registration.mjs

```javascript
/** Bilateral registration v1. No business handler is executed by a handshake.
 * Transport authentication belongs to runtime.mjs; this file only compares
 * contracts and checks the same handler maps that runtime dispatches through.
 */
import { createAtomBindings } from "./atoms.mjs";

const plain = (value) =>
  value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value);
const sorted = (value) =>
  Array.isArray(value)
    ? value.map(sorted)
    : plain(value)
      ? Object.fromEntries(
          Object.keys(value)
            .sort()
            .map((key) => [key, sorted(value[key])]),
        )
      : value;
const equal = (left, right) =>
  JSON.stringify(sorted(left)) === JSON.stringify(sorted(right));
function need(ok) {
  if (!ok)
    throw Object.assign(
      new Error("注册确认未通过，请检查协议、声明与接口绑定。"),
      { code: "registration_contract", status: 422 },
    );
}

/** Fail at service startup on missing/extra handlers or unsupported schemas. */
export function createRegistration({
  manifest,
  registration,
  handlers,
  workflowHandlers = {},
  compile,
  graph = null,
  runtimeProbe = null,
  requiredPlatformFeatures = [],
}) {
  need(Array.isArray(requiredPlatformFeatures) && requiredPlatformFeatures.length <= 20 &&
    requiredPlatformFeatures.every((value) => typeof value === "string" && /^[a-z][a-z0-9.-]{0,79}$/.test(value)) &&
    new Set(requiredPlatformFeatures).size === requiredPlatformFeatures.length);
  const extraFeatures = [...requiredPlatformFeatures];
  need(
    ["1.0", "1.1"].includes(registration?.registration_version) &&
      manifest.protocol_version === "1.2",
  );
  need(
    registration.module_id === manifest.module.id &&
      registration.module_version === manifest.module.version,
  );
  const ids = manifest.capabilities.map((cap) => cap.id).sort();
  if (registration.ai_services !== undefined) {
    need(Array.isArray(registration.ai_services) && registration.ai_services.length <= 16 &&
      (!registration.ai_services.length || registration.permissions.mode === "platform_access"));
    const aiIds = new Set();
    for (const service of registration.ai_services) {
      need(plain(service) && Object.keys(service).sort().join() ===
        "capability_ids,description,id,supported_sources,title" &&
        typeof service.id === "string" && /^[a-z][a-z0-9_.-]{2,119}$/.test(service.id) &&
        service.id.startsWith(manifest.module.id + ".") && !aiIds.has(service.id) &&
        typeof service.title === "string" && service.title.length > 0 && [...service.title].length <= 200 &&
        typeof service.description === "string" && service.description.length > 0 && [...service.description].length <= 2000 &&
        Array.isArray(service.capability_ids) && service.capability_ids.length > 0 && service.capability_ids.length <= 64 &&
        service.capability_ids.every((id) => ids.includes(id)) &&
        new Set(service.capability_ids).size === service.capability_ids.length &&
        Array.isArray(service.supported_sources) && service.supported_sources.length > 0 &&
        service.supported_sources.every((source) => ["platform", "module"].includes(source)) &&
        new Set(service.supported_sources).size === service.supported_sources.length);
      aiIds.add(service.id);
    }
  }
  const modern = registration.registration_version === "1.1";
  if (modern) {
    need(
      equal(graph, registration.module_graph) &&
        typeof runtimeProbe === "function",
    );
    createAtomBindings({ graph, manifest, handlers });
  }
  need(
    equal(Object.keys(handlers).sort(), ids) &&
      Object.values(handlers).every((fn) => typeof fn === "function"),
  );
  need(
    equal(
      Object.keys(workflowHandlers).sort(),
      registration.workflow_operations,
    ) &&
      Object.values(workflowHandlers).every((fn) => typeof fn === "function"),
  );
  for (const capability of manifest.capabilities) {
    compile(capability.input_schema);
    compile(capability.output_schema);
  }
  // Freeze a private copy: later caller mutation cannot change accepted contracts.
  const localManifest = structuredClone(manifest),
    localRegistration = structuredClone(registration);
  return {
    get document() {
      return structuredClone(localRegistration);
    },
    confirm(offer) {
      need(
        plain(offer) &&
          equal(
            Object.keys(offer).sort(),
            [
              "registration_version",
              "nonce",
              "contract_digest",
              "platform_features",
              "manifest",
              "registration",
              ...(modern ? ["runtime_probe"] : []),
            ].sort(),
          ),
      );
      need(
        offer.registration_version === localRegistration.registration_version &&
          /^[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(offer.nonce) &&
          /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(offer.contract_digest),
      );
      const required = ["invoke-1.1", "shared-hosts-1.0", "registration-1.0"];
      required.push(...extraFeatures);
      if (localRegistration.ai_services?.length) required.push("ai-services-1.0");
      if (modern)
        required.push(
          "registration-1.1",
          "module-graph-1.0",
          "flow-exports-1.0",
          "runtime-observation-1.0",
        );
      if (localRegistration.permissions.mode === "platform_access")
        required.push("access-1.0");
      need(
        Array.isArray(offer.platform_features) &&
          required.every((feature) =>
            offer.platform_features.includes(feature),
          ),
      );
      need(
        equal(offer.manifest, localManifest) &&
          equal(offer.registration, localRegistration),
      );
      const answer = {
        registration_version: localRegistration.registration_version,
        nonce: offer.nonce,
        contract_digest: offer.contract_digest,
        accepted: true,
        checks: {
          platform_contract: true,
          local_manifest: true,
          local_registration: true,
          handlers: true,
          schemas: true,
        },
        capability_ids: [...ids],
        workflow_operations: [...localRegistration.workflow_operations],
      };
      if (!modern) return answer;
      return Promise.resolve(runtimeProbe(offer.runtime_probe)).then(
        (accepted) => {
          need(accepted === true);
          return {
            ...answer,
            checks: {
              ...answer.checks,
              atomic_bindings: true,
              runtime_callback: true,
            },
          };
        },
      );
    },
  };
}
```

```javascript
/** Portable module-kit transport v1. Server only; no login, RBAC or browser credentials. */
import http from "node:http";
import { timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";
import { createRegistration } from "./registration.mjs";
import { createAtomBindings } from "./atoms.mjs";

export function fail(code, message, status = 422) {
  return Object.assign(new Error(message), { code, status });
}
export function requireThat(condition, code, message, status = 422) {
  if (!condition) throw fail(code, message, status);
}
export const canonical = (value) => JSON.stringify(sort(value));
function sort(value) {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(sort);
  if (value && typeof value === "object")
    return Object.fromEntries(
      Object.keys(value)
        .sort()
        .map((key) => [key, sort(value[key])]),
    );
  return value;
}
const plain = (value) =>
  value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value);
const keywords = new Set([
  "type",
  "properties",
  "required",
  "additionalProperties",
  "items",
  "minItems",
  "maxItems",
  "uniqueItems",
  "minLength",
  "maxLength",
  "minimum",
  "maximum",
  "pattern",
  "enum",
  "const",
  "title",
  "description",
  "default",
  "$schema",
]);
/** Deliberately closed JSON Schema subset, not an implementation of arbitrary JSON Schema.
 * Unknown validation keywords fail at startup; install a full validator before expanding it. */
export function compile(schema) {
  requireThat(
    plain(schema) && Object.keys(schema).every((key) => keywords.has(key)),
    "schema_unsupported",
    "SDK 不支持此 Schema 关键字。",
    500,
  );
  requireThat(
    schema.additionalProperties === undefined ||
      typeof schema.additionalProperties === "boolean",
    "schema_unsupported",
    "SDK 的 additionalProperties 只支持布尔值。",
    500,
  );
  requireThat(
    schema.properties === undefined || plain(schema.properties),
    "schema_unsupported",
    "Schema properties 必须是对象。",
    500,
  );
  const types = Array.isArray(schema.type) ? schema.type : [schema.type];
  requireThat(
    types.every((type) =>
      [
        "object",
        "array",
        "string",
        "integer",
        "number",
        "boolean",
        "null",
      ].includes(type),
    ),
    "schema_unsupported",
    "Schema 必须显式声明受支持类型。",
    500,
  );
  const children = Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(schema.properties || {}).map(([key, value]) => [
      key,
      compile(value),
    ]),
  );
  const child = schema.items ? compile(schema.items) : null;
  const pattern = schema.pattern ? new RegExp(schema.pattern, "u") : null;
  return (value) => {
    const type =
      value === null ? "null" : Array.isArray(value) ? "array" : typeof value;
    if (
      !types.includes(type) &&
      !(types.includes("integer") && Number.isSafeInteger(value))
    )
      return false;
    if (
      typeof value === "number" &&
      (!Number.isFinite(value) ||
        (schema.minimum !== undefined && value < schema.minimum) ||
        (schema.maximum !== undefined && value > schema.maximum))
    )
      return false;
    if (
      Object.hasOwn(schema, "const") &&
      canonical(schema.const) !== canonical(value)
    )
      return false;
    if (
      schema.enum &&
      !schema.enum.some((item) => canonical(item) === canonical(value))
    )
      return false;
    if (
      typeof value === "string" &&
      ((schema.minLength !== undefined &&
        [...value].length < schema.minLength) ||
        (schema.maxLength !== undefined &&
          [...value].length > schema.maxLength) ||
        (pattern && !pattern.test(value)))
    )
      return false;
    if (
      Array.isArray(value) &&
      ((schema.minItems !== undefined && value.length < schema.minItems) ||
        (schema.maxItems !== undefined && value.length > schema.maxItems) ||
        (schema.uniqueItems &&
          new Set(value.map(canonical)).size !== value.length) ||
        (child && !value.every(child)))
    )
      return false;
    if (plain(value)) {
      if ((schema.required || []).some((key) => !Object.hasOwn(value, key)))
        return false;
      for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(value)) {
        // Only own properties are declared fields. constructor/toString must not
        // call Object.prototype functions as validators; __proto__ remains data.
        if (
          Object.hasOwn(children, key)
            ? !children[key](item)
            : schema.additionalProperties === false
        )
          return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
  };
}
export const objectSchema = (properties) => ({
  type: "object",
  properties,
  required: Object.keys(properties),
  additionalProperties: false,
});
const string = (maxLength = 100) => ({
  type: "string",
  minLength: 1,
  maxLength,
});
const actorProperties = {
  id: string(),
  role: string(40),
  roles: {
    type: "array",
    minItems: 1,
    maxItems: 20,
    uniqueItems: true,
    items: string(40),
  },
  organization_id: { type: ["string", "null"], maxLength: 100 },
  team_ids: {
    type: "array",
    maxItems: 100,
    uniqueItems: true,
    items: string(),
  },
  name: string(),
  authorization_revision: { type: "integer", minimum: 1 },
};
const actorSchema = objectSchema(actorProperties);
const requestId = { type: "string", pattern: "^[0-9a-f]{32}$" };
const workflowCheck = compile(
  objectSchema({
    protocol_version: { type: "string", const: "1.0" },
    request_id: requestId,
    context: objectSchema({ actor: actorSchema }),
    input: { type: "object" },
  }),
);
const invokeActor = {
  ...actorSchema,
  properties: {
    ...actorProperties,
    role: {
      ...string(40),
      enum: [
        "admin",
        "member",
        "researcher",
        "contributor",
        "curator",
        "reviewer",
        "system_admin",
      ],
    },
    roles: {
      ...actorProperties.roles,
      maxItems: 7,
      items: {
        ...string(40),
        enum: [
          "admin",
          "member",
          "researcher",
          "contributor",
          "curator",
          "reviewer",
          "system_admin",
        ],
      },
    },
  },
};
const invokeCheck = compile(
  objectSchema({
    protocol_version: { type: "string", const: "1.1" },
    request_id: requestId,
    capability_id: { type: "string", pattern: "^[a-z][a-z0-9_.-]{2,120}$" },
    capability_version: string(40),
    input: { type: "object" },
    context: objectSchema({ workspace_id: string(), actor: invokeActor }),
  }),
);
async function bodyOf(request, limit = 65536) {
  const chunks = [];
  let size = 0;
  for await (const chunk of request) {
    size += chunk.length;
    requireThat(size <= limit, "request_size", "请求超过接口大小限制。", 413);
    chunks.push(chunk);
  }
  try {
    return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString("utf8"));
  } catch {
    throw fail("invalid_json", "请求不是有效 JSON。");
  }
}
function send(response, status, data) {
  const body = JSON.stringify(data);
  response.writeHead(status, {
    "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
    "Cache-Control": "no-store",
    "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
  });
  response.end(body);
}
export function createModuleServer({
  manifest,
  registration,
  token,
  handlers,
  workflowHandlers = {},
  graph = null,
  observationClient = null,
  requiredPlatformFeatures = [],
  aiManager = null,
}) {
  // Snapshot the same dispatch tables that acceptance checks. Caller mutation
  // after startup must not silently remove a previously confirmed interface.
  manifest = structuredClone(manifest);
  registration = structuredClone(registration);
  handlers = Object.freeze({ ...handlers });
  workflowHandlers = Object.freeze({ ...workflowHandlers });
  const atoms = graph
    ? createAtomBindings({ graph, manifest, handlers })
    : null;
  requireThat(
    registration.ai_services?.length
      ? aiManager && canonical(aiManager.services) === canonical(registration.ai_services)
      : aiManager === null,
    "ai_registration_binding",
    "AI 服务声明必须绑定实际的服务管理器。",
    500,
  );
  requireThat(
    typeof token === "string" && token.length >= 24,
    "service_config",
    "请配置至少 24 字符的私有服务凭据。",
    500,
  );
  const expected = Buffer.from(`Bearer ${token}`);
  const capabilities = new Map(
    manifest.capabilities.map((cap) => [
      cap.id,
      {
        cap,
        input: compile(cap.input_schema),
        output: compile(cap.output_schema),
      },
    ]),
  );
  const registrar = createRegistration({
    manifest,
    registration,
    handlers,
    workflowHandlers,
    compile,
    graph: atoms?.document,
    runtimeProbe: observationClient?.probe,
    requiredPlatformFeatures,
  });
  return http.createServer(async (request, response) => {
    let observation;
    try {
      const received = Buffer.from(request.headers.authorization || "");
      requireThat(
        received.length === expected.length &&
          timingSafeEqual(received, expected),
        "service_auth",
        "服务连接认证失败。",
        401,
      );
      if (request.method === "GET" && request.url === "/platform/v1/health")
        return send(response, 200, { status: "ok" });
      if (request.method === "GET" && request.url === "/platform/v1/manifest")
        return send(response, 200, manifest);
      const aiControl = request.url?.match(/^\/platform\/v1\/ai\/services\/([a-z][a-z0-9_.-]{1,120})\/(config|test)$/);
      if (aiControl && aiManager) {
        // Service Bearer was verified above. These controls are never business
        // capabilities, and must not appear in manifests or browser bundles.
        return send(response, 200, await aiManager.control(
          aiControl[1], request.method, aiControl[2],
          request.method === "GET" ? {} : await bodyOf(request, 32768),
        ));
      }
      if (
        atoms &&
        request.method === "GET" &&
        request.url === "/platform/v1/module-graph"
      )
        return send(response, 200, atoms.document);
      if (
        registration.flow_exports &&
        request.method === "GET" &&
        request.url === "/platform/v1/flow-exports"
      )
        return send(response, 200, registrar.document.flow_exports);
      if (
        request.method === "GET" &&
        request.url === "/platform/v1/registration"
      )
        return send(response, 200, registrar.document);
      if (request.method === "POST" && request.url === "/platform/v1/handshake") {
        requireThat(!registration.ai_services?.length || aiManager.platformConnected === true,
          "ai_platform_not_configured", "接入前请配置 AI 服务的固定平台地址。", 409);
        return send(
          response,
          200,
          await registrar.confirm(await bodyOf(request, 1048576)),
        );
      }
      const operation = request.url?.startsWith("/platform/v1/workflow/")
        ? request.url.slice("/platform/v1/workflow/".length)
        : null;
      requireThat(
        request.method === "POST" &&
          (operation || request.url === "/platform/v1/invoke"),
        "not_found",
        "接口不存在。",
        404,
      );
      const body = await bodyOf(request);
      requireThat(
        (operation ? workflowCheck : invokeCheck)(body) &&
          body.context.actor.roles.includes(body.context.actor.role),
        "input_contract",
        "请求包络不符合契约。",
      );
      // Access tickets are ephemeral arguments only: never included in body fingerprints, responses or persistence.
      const ticket = request.headers["x-platform-access"];
      if (registration.permissions.mode === "platform_access") {
        requireThat(
          typeof ticket === "string" &&
            ticket.length > 0 &&
            ticket.length <= 12288,
          "access_denied",
          "缺少有效的中央授权票据。",
          403,
        );
      }
      if (operation) {
        requireThat(
          Object.hasOwn(workflowHandlers, operation),
          "workflow_unsupported",
          "此服务未注册该对象接口。",
          501,
        );
        const data = await workflowHandlers[operation]({
          body,
          operation,
          ticket,
        });
        requireThat(
          Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(data)) <=
            (operation === "objects.list" ? 131072 : 1048576),
          "response_size",
          "模块响应超出契约上限。",
          500,
        );
        return send(response, 200, {
          protocol_version: "1.0",
          request_id: body.request_id,
          data,
        });
      }
      const spec = capabilities.get(body.capability_id);
      requireThat(
        spec &&
          spec.cap.version === body.capability_version &&
          spec.input(body.input),
        "input_contract",
        "能力、版本或参数不符合契约。",
      );
      if (registration.permissions.mode === "capability_roles") {
        requireThat(
          spec.cap.roles.some((role) =>
            body.context.actor.roles.includes(role),
          ),
          "access_denied",
          "当前身份无权使用此功能。",
          403,
        );
      }
      if (atoms && observationClient) {
        observation = observationClient.session({
          ticket: request.headers["x-duoflow-observation"],
          requestId: body.request_id,
          atomId: atoms.forCapability(body.capability_id).id,
        });
        observation.mark("started");
      }
      const output = await handlers[body.capability_id]({
        body,
        ticket,
        observe: observation,
      });
      requireThat(
        typeof output.summary === "string" &&
          output.summary.length <= 4000 &&
          spec.output(output.data) &&
          Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(output)) <= 1048576,
        "output_contract",
        "模块输出不符合契约。",
        500,
      );
      observation?.mark("succeeded");
      return send(response, 200, {
        protocol_version: "1.1",
        request_id: body.request_id,
        capability_id: body.capability_id,
        capability_version: body.capability_version,
        summary: output.summary,
        data: output.data,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      observation?.mark("failed");
      const safe =
        Number.isInteger(error.status) &&
        error.status >= 400 &&
        error.status < 500;
      send(response, Number.isInteger(error.status) ? error.status : 500, {
        error: {
          code: error.code || "handler_failed",
          message: safe ? error.message : "服务暂时无法完成此操作。",
        },
      });
    }
  });
}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/source-read-client.mjs

```javascript
/** Server-only registered source reads. Tickets are never returned or stored. */
export class SourceReadError extends Error {
  constructor(code, status = 503) {
    super(code); this.code = code;
    this.status = Number.isInteger(status) && status >= 400 && status <= 599 ? status : 502;
  }
}

export function createSourceReadClient({ callbackUrl, timeoutMs = 8000, fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch }) {
  const endpoint = new URL(callbackUrl);
  if (!["http:", "https:"].includes(endpoint.protocol) || endpoint.username || endpoint.password ||
      endpoint.search || endpoint.hash || endpoint.pathname !== "/api/v1/access/source-reads")
    throw new SourceReadError("source_read_configuration", 500);
  if (!Number.isInteger(timeoutMs) || timeoutMs < 1 || timeoutMs > 30000)
    throw new SourceReadError("source_read_configuration", 500);
  return async function readSource({ ticket, requestId, operation, permission, facts, source, readInput }) {
    if (typeof ticket !== "string" || !ticket || ticket.length > 12288)
      throw new SourceReadError("access_ticket_invalid", 401);
    const body = JSON.stringify({ source_read_version: "1.0", request_id: requestId,
      operation, permission, facts, source, ...(readInput === undefined ? {} : { read_input: readInput }) });
    if (Buffer.byteLength(body, "utf8") > 16384) throw new SourceReadError("source_read_size", 413);
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
    try {
      const response = await fetchImpl(endpoint, {
        method: "POST", redirect: "error", signal: controller.signal,
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "X-Platform-Access": ticket }, body,
      });
      const reader = response.body.getReader();
      const chunks = []; let size = 0;
      while (true) {
        const { value, done } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        size += value.byteLength;
        if (size > (response.ok ? 1100000 : 4096)) {
          await reader.cancel(); throw new SourceReadError("source_read_response_size", 502);
        }
        chunks.push(Buffer.from(value));
      }
      const raw = Buffer.concat(chunks).toString("utf8");
      if (raw.includes(ticket)) throw new SourceReadError("source_read_credential", 502);
      const result = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (!response.ok) {
        const code = result.error?.code;
        throw new SourceReadError(typeof code === "string" && /^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,79}$/.test(code)
          ? code : "source_read_denied", response.status);
      }
      const ref = source?.object_ref, actual = result.source, projection = result.projection;
      if (result.source_read_version !== "1.0" || Object.keys(result).sort().join() !== "projection,source,source_read_version" ||
          actual?.module_id !== source.module_id || actual?.revision !== source.revision ||
          actual?.object_ref?.object_id !== ref?.object_id || actual?.object_ref?.object_type !== ref?.object_type ||
          projection?.revision !== source.revision || projection?.object_ref?.object_id !== ref?.object_id ||
          projection?.object_ref?.object_type !== ref?.object_type || typeof projection?.state !== "string" ||
          !projection?.data || typeof projection.data !== "object" || Array.isArray(projection.data) ||
          !Array.isArray(projection.allowed_actions)) throw new SourceReadError("source_read_response", 502);
      return result;
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof SourceReadError) throw error;
      throw new SourceReadError("source_read_unavailable");
    } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
  };
}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/source-read-client.test.mjs

```javascript
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { test } from "node:test";
import { createSourceReadClient } from "./source-read-client.mjs";
import { createRegistration } from "./registration.mjs";

const callbackUrl = "http://127.0.0.1:8031/api/v1/access/source-reads";
const source = { module_id: "example.source", object_ref: { object_type: "example.source.snapshot", object_id: "S1" }, revision: 7 };
const input = { ticket: "synthetic-ticket", requestId: "r1", operation: "invoke", permission: "consumer.use", facts: { project_id: "P" }, source };
const result = { source_read_version: "1.0", source, projection: { object_ref: source.object_ref, revision: 7, state: "published", data: { selected: "actual" }, allowed_actions: [] } };

test("callback has a fixed URL, original target header and no persisted ticket", async () => {
  let seen;
  const read = createSourceReadClient({ callbackUrl, fetchImpl: async (url, options) => {
    seen = { url: String(url), options }; return Response.json(result);
  } });
  assert.deepEqual(await read(input), result);
  assert.equal(seen.url, callbackUrl);
  assert.equal(seen.options.redirect, "error");
  assert.equal(seen.options.headers["X-Platform-Access"], input.ticket);
  assert.equal(Object.hasOwn(JSON.parse(seen.options.body), "ticket"), false);
});

test("callback override, version mismatch and credential echo fail", async () => {
  for (const url of ["https://host/wrong", "https://user:pass@host/api/v1/access/source-reads", callbackUrl + "?url=other"])
    assert.throws(() => createSourceReadClient({ callbackUrl: url }));
  const mismatch = createSourceReadClient({ callbackUrl, fetchImpl: async () => Response.json({ ...result, source: { ...source, revision: 8 } }) });
  await assert.rejects(mismatch(input), { code: "source_read_response" });
  const echo = createSourceReadClient({ callbackUrl, fetchImpl: async () => Response.json({ ...result, projection: { ...result.projection, data: { secret: input.ticket } } }) });
  await assert.rejects(echo(input), { code: "source_read_credential" });
});

test("failed source and bounded response preserve a stable failure", async () => {
  const denied = createSourceReadClient({ callbackUrl, fetchImpl: async () => Response.json({ error: { code: "source_revision_changed" } }, { status: 409 }) });
  await assert.rejects(denied(input), { code: "source_revision_changed" });
  const large = createSourceReadClient({ callbackUrl, fetchImpl: async () => new Response(" ".repeat(1100001)) });
  await assert.rejects(large(input), { code: "source_read_response_size" });
  const offline = createSourceReadClient({ callbackUrl, fetchImpl: async () => { throw new Error("private transport detail"); } });
  await assert.rejects(offline(input), { code: "source_read_unavailable" });
});

test("consumer can require the optional feature during registration confirmation", () => {
  const manifest = { protocol_version: "1.2", module: { id: "example.consumer", version: "1.0.0" }, capabilities: [] };
  const registration = { registration_version: "1.0", module_id: "example.consumer", module_version: "1.0.0", workflow_operations: [], permissions: { mode: "capability_roles" } };
  const features = ["source-reads-1.0"];
  const registrar = createRegistration({ manifest, registration, handlers: {}, compile: () => {}, requiredPlatformFeatures: features });
  features.length = 0;
  const offer = { registration_version: "1.0", nonce: "a".repeat(32), contract_digest: "b".repeat(64), manifest, registration,
    platform_features: ["invoke-1.1", "shared-hosts-1.0", "registration-1.0"] };
  assert.throws(() => registrar.confirm(offer), { code: "registration_contract" });
  offer.platform_features.push("source-reads-1.0");
  assert.equal(registrar.confirm(offer).accepted, true);
});

test("source HTTP failures preserve safe status without exposing response details", async () => {
  for (const status of [403, 409, 422, 503]) {
    const read = createSourceReadClient({ callbackUrl, fetchImpl: async () =>
      Response.json({ error: { code: "source_fixture_error", message: "private fixture detail" } }, { status }) });
    await assert.rejects(read(input), error => error.code === "source_fixture_error" &&
      error.status === status && error.message === "source_fixture_error");
  }
});
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/store.mjs

```javascript
/** SQLite unit of work: one connection, serial async operations, atomic business + receipt commit. */
import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";
import {
  createHash,
  randomBytes,
  createHmac,
  timingSafeEqual,
} from "node:crypto";
import { canonical, fail, requireThat } from "./runtime.mjs";

export class Store {
  constructor(filename) {
    this.db = new DatabaseSync(filename);
    this.db.exec(`PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL; PRAGMA busy_timeout=4000;
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS kit_receipts(request_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, fingerprint TEXT NOT NULL, object_id TEXT NOT NULL, response TEXT NOT NULL);
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS kit_metadata(key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT NOT NULL);`);
    this.db
      .prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO kit_metadata(key,value) VALUES (?,?)")
      .run("cursor-key", randomBytes(32).toString("hex"));
    this.cursorKey = this.db
      .prepare("SELECT value FROM kit_metadata WHERE key=?")
      .get("cursor-key").value;
    this.queue = Promise.resolve();
  }
  transaction(callback) {
    const work = this.queue.then(async () => {
      this.db.exec("BEGIN IMMEDIATE");
      try {
        const result = await callback(this.db);
        this.db.exec("COMMIT");
        return result;
      } catch (error) {
        this.db.exec("ROLLBACK");
        throw error;
      }
    });
    this.queue = work.catch(() => {});
    return work;
  }
  fingerprint(request) {
    return createHash("sha256").update(canonical(request)).digest("hex");
  }
  receipt(request) {
    const saved = this.db
      .prepare("SELECT * FROM kit_receipts WHERE request_id=?")
      .get(request.request_id);
    if (saved)
      requireThat(
        saved.fingerprint === this.fingerprint(request),
        "idempotency_conflict",
        "同一请求编号的内容已经变化。",
        409,
      );
    return saved
      ? { objectId: saved.object_id, response: JSON.parse(saved.response) }
      : null;
  }
  saveReceipt(request, objectId, response) {
    this.db
      .prepare("INSERT INTO kit_receipts VALUES (?,?,?,?)")
      .run(
        request.request_id,
        this.fingerprint(request),
        objectId,
        JSON.stringify(response),
      );
  }
  cursor(binding, offset) {
    const payload = Buffer.from(canonical({ binding, offset })).toString(
      "base64url",
    );
    return `${payload}.${createHmac("sha256", this.cursorKey).update(payload).digest("base64url")}`;
  }
  offset(cursor, binding) {
    if (!cursor) return 0;
    try {
      const [payload, signature, extra] = cursor.split(".");
      const expected = Buffer.from(
        createHmac("sha256", this.cursorKey)
          .update(payload)
          .digest("base64url"),
      );
      const actual = Buffer.from(signature || "");
      if (
        extra ||
        expected.length !== actual.length ||
        !timingSafeEqual(actual, expected)
      )
        throw new Error();
      const decoded = JSON.parse(
        Buffer.from(payload, "base64url").toString("utf8"),
      );
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(decoded.offset) || decoded.offset < 0)
        throw new Error();
      requireThat(
        canonical(decoded.binding) === canonical(binding),
        "revision_conflict",
        "目录或授权已变化，请从第一页重新读取。",
        409,
      );
      return decoded.offset;
    } catch (error) {
      if (error.code === "revision_conflict") throw error;
      throw fail("cursor_invalid", "分页游标无效。");
    }
  }
  async close() {
    await this.queue;
    this.db.close();
  }
}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/telemetry.mjs

```javascript
/** Optional process telemetry sent directly to the independent collector. */
import os from 'node:os';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';

export function createTelemetryReporter({url, token, instanceId = os.hostname(),
  intervalSeconds = 15, metrics = () => ({}), fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch}) {
  let endpoint;
  try { endpoint = new URL(url); } catch { throw new Error('Invalid telemetry configuration'); }
  if (endpoint.username || endpoint.password || endpoint.search || endpoint.hash ||
      !/^\/api\/v1\/monitor\/reports\/[a-z][a-z0-9_.-]{1,79}$/.test(endpoint.pathname) ||
      !['http:', 'https:'].includes(endpoint.protocol) ||
      (endpoint.protocol === 'http:' && !['127.0.0.1', 'localhost', '[::1]'].includes(endpoint.hostname)) ||
      typeof token !== 'string' || token.length < 24 || token.length > 4096 ||
      typeof instanceId !== 'string' || !/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,63}$/.test(instanceId) ||
      !Number.isFinite(intervalSeconds) || intervalSeconds < 1 || intervalSeconds > 300)
    throw new Error('Invalid telemetry configuration');
  const bootId = randomUUID().replaceAll('-', '');
  let sequence = 0, timer, active = null, stopped = false;
  let cpu = process.cpuUsage(), sampled = performance.now();
  const started = performance.now();
  const allowed = new Set(['inflight_requests', 'queue_depth', 'requests_total', 'errors_total']);
  let sent = 0, failed = 0, skipped = 0;
  async function report() {
    if (stopped || active) { skipped++; return; }
    active = (async () => {
      try {
        const now = performance.now(), used = process.cpuUsage(cpu);
        cpu = process.cpuUsage();
        const cpuPercent = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (used.user + used.system) / Math.max(1, now-sampled) / 10 / os.availableParallelism()));
        sampled = now;
        const supplied = metrics();
        if (!supplied || Array.isArray(supplied) || Object.keys(supplied).some(key => !allowed.has(key)))
          throw new Error('Invalid telemetry counters');
        for (const value of Object.values(supplied))
          if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < 0) throw new Error('Invalid telemetry counters');
        const body = JSON.stringify({monitor_version:'1.0', instance_id:instanceId, boot_id:bootId,
          sequence:++sequence, status:'ready', uptime_seconds:(now-started)/1000,
          metrics:{cpu_percent:cpuPercent, rss_bytes:process.memoryUsage().rss, ...supplied}});
        const response = await fetchImpl(endpoint, {method:'POST', redirect:'error',
          signal:AbortSignal.timeout(2000), headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json', Authorization:'Bearer '+token}, body});
        await response.body?.cancel();
        if (response.ok) sent++; else failed++;
      } catch { failed++; } // Telemetry never changes a business result or logs secrets.
    })();
    try { await active; } finally { active = null; }
  }
  return {
    report,
    start() { if (!stopped && !timer) { timer=setInterval(()=>void report(), intervalSeconds*1000); timer.unref?.(); void report(); } },
    async stop() { stopped=true; clearInterval(timer); timer=undefined; if(active) await active; },
    stats() { return {sent, failed, skipped}; },
  };
}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/node/telemetry.test.mjs

```javascript
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {createTelemetryReporter} from './telemetry.mjs';
const config={url:'http://127.0.0.1:8032/api/v1/monitor/reports/demo-service',token:'synthetic-telemetry-token-123456789',instanceId:'one'};
test('real process sample is bounded and sends directly to collector with no business data',async()=>{
  let sent;
  const reporter=createTelemetryReporter({...config, metrics:()=>({inflight_requests:0,requests_total:2}), fetchImpl:async(url,options)=>{
    sent={url:String(url),options,body:JSON.parse(options.body)};return new Response('{}');}});
  await reporter.report();await reporter.stop();
  assert.equal(sent.url,config.url);assert.equal(sent.options.redirect,'error');
  assert.equal(sent.options.headers.Authorization,'Bearer '+config.token);
  assert(sent.body.metrics.rss_bytes>0);assert(sent.body.metrics.cpu_percent>=0&&sent.body.metrics.cpu_percent<=100);
  assert.equal(sent.body.metrics.inflight_requests,0);assert(!('queue_depth' in sent.body.metrics));
  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(sent.body).includes(config.token),false);assert.equal(reporter.stats().sent,1);
});
test('unreachable collector and invalid counters are isolated and never retried immediately',async()=>{
  let calls=0;
  const reporter=createTelemetryReporter({...config,fetchImpl:async()=>{calls++;throw new Error(config.token);}});
  await reporter.report();assert.equal(calls,1);assert.equal(reporter.stats().failed,1);await reporter.stop();
  const invalid=createTelemetryReporter({...config,metrics:()=>({business_body:'SECRET'}),fetchImpl:async()=>{calls++;}});
  await invalid.report();assert.equal(calls,1);assert.equal(invalid.stats().failed,1);await invalid.stop();
});
test('only one report may be inflight',async()=>{
  let release;
  const reporter=createTelemetryReporter({...config,fetchImpl:()=>new Promise(resolve=>{release=resolve;})});
  const first=reporter.report();await reporter.report();assert.equal(reporter.stats().skipped,1);
  release(new Response('{}'));await first;await reporter.stop();
});
test('URL and identity settings reject external plain HTTP, query secrets and malformed values',()=>{
  for(const override of [{url:'http://example.org/api/v1/monitor/reports/demo-service'},
    {url:config.url+'?token=private'},{url:'https://user:secret@example.org/api/v1/monitor/reports/demo-service'},
    {instanceId:null},{intervalSeconds:0},{token:'short'}])
    assert.throws(()=>createTelemetryReporter({...config,...override}),/Invalid telemetry configuration/);
});
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/README.md

````markdown
# Python 工具 SDK 1.0.0

可选 `ai_service.py` 独立提供 `AIServiceManager`（httpx + cryptography），不依赖平台 app 包。注册声明使用 `ai_services`，既有 `module_kit.py` 纯工具入口不自动启用模型。独立运行使用自有 API，接平台后每次按中央绑定选路，禁止静默 fallback；`chat_with_receipt` / `validate` 支持在最终业务提交前重新核对配置、来源和授权。适配器须先验证服务 Bearer，再接 manager 的 `control` 方法，实际 manager 声明须与注册声明一致。详见 [AI 服务示例](../../examples/ai-services/README.md)。

`graph_testing.py` 提供 `assert_transition(graph, machine_id, before, event, after)` 与 `assert_atom_result(graph, atom_id, inputs, output, validate)`。测试先调用真实业务代码，再传入实际读到的对象或结果。后端原子另传 `manifest=实际清单`，函数按能力 ID 和版本读取 Schema。`validate(schema, value)` 由项目提供，校验失败时抛出异常。前者核对同一对象的状态变化是否有声明，后者核对原子输入输出；权限、修订和实际数据效果仍需独立断言。

已有 Python HTTP 服务另使用同目录 `registration.py` 主动核对注册握手，以及 `remote_access.py` 请求中央授权。注册确认器不替代 HTTP 认证或实际调用适配器，使用方法见同批 `docs/模块框架说明.md`。原生工具不进行远程握手。

`module_kit.py` 为标准库单文件，脚手架复制为生成包的 `backend/_module_kit.py`。安装时整个 backend 目录一起复制。没有 PyPI 安装、全局路径修改或对本仓库工作目录的依赖。

稳定入口：

```python
run_tool(ctx, permission="your.module.use", payload=payload, handler=business_function)
require_organization_access(ctx, "your.module.use")
```

`ctx` 必须来自平台，提供认证后的 `actor` 与真实 `access.check(actor, permission, facts)`。本 SDK 只读取认证身份中的 `organization_id`，采用 `allowed is True` 默认拒绝，不包含 Casbin、角色回退或本地授权缓存。业务函数接收 JSON 对象，返回 `{"summary": "说明", "data": {...}}`；输入上限 100000 UTF-8 字节，结果上限 256000 字节。`ValueError` 映射为通用输入错误，其余业务异常交给平台安全错误处理。`KitError` 提供 code、message、status，由原生处理器转换成平台错误。

该入口仅用于没有持久对象的机构内纯函数工具。正式对象、仓储事实、事务、职责状态、修订、幂等和副作用不由它实现；不要用操作者机构替代已有对象的机构。平台仍验证能力输入输出 Schema、访问快照和宿主请求安全。复制后 SDK 是模块发布的一部分；框架升级不会自动改写已交付副本，升级要对照契约、替换 SDK 并测试。
# Registered source reads

`source_reads.SourceReadClient` supports the optional `source-reads-1.0`
callback. It validates a fixed deployment callback, bounds IO and returns no
credentials. Use the current consumer ticket and repository facts. Sources are
resolved through registered read-only object adapters; domain validation and
future authorization remain the business module's responsibility.

`SourceReadError.code` and `.status` preserve safe error classification, including
403 authorization, 409 version conflict, 422 contract and 503 unavailability.
The exception text contains only the safe code, never private response messages.
````

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/ai_service.py

```python
"""Portable server-only AI service manager v1 (httpx + cryptography).

One process owns data_dir. AES-GCM and a local 0600 key protect a copied config,
not access to the entire directory. Set a private Windows directory ACL as well.
HTTP adapters MUST verify their private service Bearer before calling control().
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from copy import deepcopy
import ipaddress
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import tempfile
import threading
import time
from urllib.parse import urlsplit, urlunsplit

import httpx
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.ciphers.aead import AESGCM


class AIServiceError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, code, status=503):
        super().__init__(code)
        self.code, self.status = code, status


def _need(ok, code="ai_configuration", status=422):
    if not ok:
        raise AIServiceError(code, status)


def _identifier(value):
    return isinstance(value, str) and re.fullmatch(r"[a-z][a-z0-9_.-]{2,119}", value)


def _scope_identifier(value):
    return isinstance(value, str) and re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,159}", value)


def _facts(value):
    def fact_string(entry):
        return isinstance(entry, str) and 0 < len(entry) <= 160
    return isinstance(value, dict) and len(value) <= 32 and all(
        isinstance(key, str) and re.fullmatch(r"[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,79}", key)
        and (fact_string(item) or isinstance(item, list) and 0 < len(item) <= 20
             and all(map(fact_string, item)) and len(set(item)) == len(item)) for key, item in value.items())


_PUBLIC = ("id", "name", "base_url", "model", "enabled", "temperature", "max_tokens", "timeout_seconds")
_PROFILE_FIELDS = (set(_PUBLIC) - {"id"}) | {"api_key", "clear_api_key"}
_FINISH = {"stop", "length", "max_tokens", "tool_calls", "content_filter", "function_call"}


def _redact(value, secrets):
    if isinstance(value, str):
        for secret in sorted(set(filter(None, secrets)), key=len, reverse=True):
            value = value.replace(secret, "【密钥已隐藏】")
        return value
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [_redact(child, secrets) for child in value]
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {_redact(key, secrets): _redact(child, secrets) for key, child in value.items()}
    return value


def _base_url(value, *, origin_only=False):
    _need(isinstance(value, str) and 0 < len(value) <= 2048
          and not re.search(r"[\s\\%?#]", value))
    try:
        parsed = urlsplit(value)
        host, port = parsed.hostname, parsed.port
        loopback = host == "localhost"
        try:
            loopback = loopback or ipaddress.ip_address(host).is_loopback
        except ValueError:
            pass
        _need(parsed.scheme in {"http", "https"} and host and parsed.username is None
              and parsed.password is None and (port is None or 0 < port <= 65535)
              and (parsed.scheme == "https" or loopback)
              and (not origin_only or parsed.path in {"", "/"}))
        return urlunsplit((parsed.scheme, parsed.netloc, "" if origin_only else parsed.path.rstrip("/"), "", ""))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        raise AIServiceError("ai_configuration", 422) from None


def _text(value, maximum):
    _need(isinstance(value, str) and value.strip() and len(value) <= maximum
          and not any(ord(char) < 32 or ord(char) == 127 for char in value))
    return value.strip()


def _number(value, minimum, maximum, *, integer=False):
    _need(type(value) in (int, float) and math.isfinite(value) and minimum <= value <= maximum
          and (not integer or type(value) is int))
    return value


def _messages(messages):
    _need(isinstance(messages, list) and 0 < len(messages) <= 64 and all(
        isinstance(entry, dict) and set(entry) == {"role", "content"}
        and entry["role"] in {"system", "developer", "user", "assistant"}
        and isinstance(entry["content"], str) and len(entry["content"]) <= 16000 for entry in messages), "ai_request", 422)
    _need(len(json.dumps(messages, ensure_ascii=False).encode()) <= 60000, "ai_request_size", 413)


def _result(value):
    _need(isinstance(value, dict) and {"content", "model", "tool_calls"} <= set(value)
          and not set(value) - {"content", "model", "tool_calls", "finish_reason", "usage"}
          and isinstance(value["content"], str) and isinstance(value["model"], str)
          and value["tool_calls"] == []
          and ("finish_reason" not in value or value["finish_reason"] in _FINISH)
          and ("usage" not in value or isinstance(value["usage"], dict) and all(
              key in {"prompt_tokens", "completion_tokens", "total_tokens"}
              and type(count) is int and count >= 0 for key, count in value["usage"].items())),
          "ai_response", 502)
    return value


class AIServiceManager:
    def __init__(self, *, services, data_dir, platform_base_url=None, transport=None):
        _need(isinstance(services, list) and 0 < len(services) <= 16 and all(
            isinstance(service, dict) and set(service) == {"id", "title", "description", "capability_ids", "supported_sources"}
            and _identifier(service["id"]) and isinstance(service["title"], str) and 0 < len(service["title"]) <= 200
            and isinstance(service["description"], str) and 0 < len(service["description"]) <= 2000 and isinstance(service["capability_ids"], list)
            and 0 < len(service["capability_ids"]) <= 64 and all(map(_identifier, service["capability_ids"]))
            and len(set(service["capability_ids"])) == len(service["capability_ids"])
            and isinstance(service["supported_sources"], list) and service["supported_sources"]
            and all(source in {"platform", "module"} for source in service["supported_sources"])
            and len(set(service["supported_sources"])) == len(service["supported_sources"])
            for service in services) and len({service["id"] for service in services}) == len(services),
            "ai_configuration", 500)
        self._services = {service["id"]: deepcopy(service) for service in services}
        self._directory = Path(data_dir).absolute()
        self._directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
        self._filename = self._directory / "module-ai.config.enc"
        self._keyfile = self._directory / "module-ai.key"
        self._platform = None if platform_base_url is None else _base_url(platform_base_url, origin_only=True)
        self._transport = transport
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._read()

    @property
    def services(self):
        return deepcopy(list(self._services.values()))

    @property
    def platform_connected(self):
        return self._platform is not None

    def _service(self, service_id):
        _need(service_id in self._services, "ai_service_unknown", 404)
        return self._services[service_id]

    def _key(self, *, create=False):
        try:
            if create and not self._keyfile.exists():
                descriptor = os.open(self._keyfile, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL, 0o600)
                with os.fdopen(descriptor, "wb") as output:
                    output.write(os.urandom(32))
                    output.flush()
                    os.fsync(output.fileno())
            key = self._keyfile.read_bytes()
            _need(len(key) == 32, "ai_secret_unavailable", 503)
            return key
        except OSError:
            raise AIServiceError("ai_secret_unavailable") from None

    def _read(self):
        if not self._filename.exists():
            return {"version": 1, "services": {}}
        try:
            content = self._filename.read_bytes()
            _need(29 <= len(content) <= 1048576, "ai_config_corrupt", 503)
            # Same wire layout as Node: nonce(12), tag(16), ciphertext.
            raw = AESGCM(self._key()).decrypt(content[:12], content[28:] + content[12:28], None)
            state = json.loads(raw)
            _need(state["version"] == 1 and isinstance(state["services"], dict) and all(
                isinstance(entry, dict) and type(entry["revision"]) is int and entry["revision"] >= 1
                and isinstance(entry["profile"], dict) for entry in state["services"].values()),
                "ai_config_corrupt", 503)
            return state
        except Exception:
            raise AIServiceError("ai_config_corrupt") from None

    def _write(self, state):
        temporary = None
        try:
            nonce = os.urandom(12)
            encrypted = AESGCM(self._key(create=True)).encrypt(nonce, json.dumps(state, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False).encode(), None)
            descriptor, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".module-ai-", dir=self._directory)
            with os.fdopen(descriptor, "wb") as output:
                output.write(nonce + encrypted[-16:] + encrypted[:-16])
                output.flush()
                os.fsync(output.fileno())
            os.chmod(temporary, 0o600)
            os.replace(temporary, self._filename)
        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
            raise AIServiceError("ai_config_write_failed") from None
        finally:
            if temporary and os.path.exists(temporary):
                os.unlink(temporary)

    def _secrets(self, state=None):
        with self._lock:
            return [entry["profile"].get("api_key", "") for entry in (state or self._read())["services"].values()]

    def get_config(self, service_id):
        self._service(service_id)
        with self._lock:
            entry = self._read()["services"].get(service_id)
            if entry is None:
                return {"revision": 0, "profile": None}
            return {"revision": entry["revision"], "profile": {
                **{key: entry["profile"][key] for key in _PUBLIC},
                "api_key_configured": bool(entry["profile"].get("api_key"))}}

    def put_config(self, service_id, *, expected_revision, profile):
        self._service(service_id)
        with self._lock:
            state = self._read()
            previous = state["services"].get(service_id, {"revision": 0, "profile": {}})
            _need(type(expected_revision) is int and expected_revision >= 0
                  and isinstance(profile, dict) and not set(profile) - _PROFILE_FIELDS
                  and ("clear_api_key" not in profile or type(profile["clear_api_key"]) is bool))
            _need(expected_revision == previous["revision"], "ai_config_revision_changed", 409)
            merged = {"enabled": True, "temperature": 0.2, "max_tokens": 2048, "timeout_seconds": 45,
                      **previous["profile"], **profile}
            _need(type(merged["enabled"]) is bool)
            stored = dict(id=service_id, name=_text(merged.get("name"), 120), base_url=_base_url(merged.get("base_url")),
                          model=_text(merged.get("model"), 200), enabled=merged["enabled"],
                          temperature=_number(merged["temperature"], 0, 2),
                          max_tokens=_number(merged["max_tokens"], 1, 131072, integer=True),
                          timeout_seconds=_number(merged["timeout_seconds"], 1, 300))
            _need("api_key" not in profile or isinstance(profile["api_key"], str) and len(profile["api_key"]) <= 8192)
            supplied = profile.get("api_key", "").strip()
            _need(all(33 <= ord(char) <= 126 for char in supplied) and not (profile.get("clear_api_key") and supplied))
            _need(not previous["profile"].get("api_key") or stored["base_url"] == previous["profile"]["base_url"]
                  or supplied or profile.get("clear_api_key"), "ai_destination_changed", 422)
            secret = supplied or ("" if profile.get("clear_api_key") else previous["profile"].get("api_key", ""))
            secrets = self._secrets(state) + [supplied]
            _need(not any(key and any(key in str(value) for value in stored.values()) for key in secrets))
            _need(not supplied or not any(supplied in str(entry["profile"][key])
                  for entry in state["services"].values() for key in _PUBLIC))
            state["services"][service_id] = {"revision": previous["revision"] + 1, "profile": {**stored, "api_key": secret}}
            self._write(state)
            return self.get_config(service_id)

    async def _request(self, url, payload, headers, timeout, *, provider=False):
        body = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False).encode()
        _need(len(body) <= (65536 if provider or url.endswith("/chat") else 16384), "ai_request_size", 413)
        try:
            async with asyncio.timeout(timeout):
                async with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=self._transport, trust_env=False, follow_redirects=False, timeout=timeout) as client:
                    async with client.stream("POST", url, content=body,
                            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "Accept-Encoding": "identity", **headers}) as response:
                        if not response.is_success:
                            status = response.status_code
                            code = ("ai_provider_auth" if status in {401, 403} else "ai_provider_limited" if status == 429
                                    else "ai_provider_failed") if provider else "ai_platform_rejected"
                            raise AIServiceError(code, 502 if provider else status if 400 <= status <= 599 else 502)
                        _need(response.headers.get("content-encoding", "identity") == "identity", "ai_response", 502)
                        raw = bytearray()
                        async for chunk in response.aiter_bytes():
                            _need(len(raw) + len(chunk) <= 4194304, "ai_response_size", 502)
                            raw.extend(chunk)
                        return json.loads(raw)
        except AIServiceError:
            raise
        except (httpx.HTTPError, OSError, ValueError, TypeError, TimeoutError):
            raise AIServiceError("ai_provider_unavailable" if provider else "ai_platform_unavailable") from None

    def _scope(self, scope, service_id):
        _need(isinstance(scope, dict) and isinstance(scope.get("ticket"), str) and 0 < len(scope["ticket"]) <= 12288
              and _scope_identifier(scope.get("request_id")) and _scope_identifier(scope.get("operation"))
              and _scope_identifier(scope.get("permission")) and _facts(scope.get("facts")), "ai_access_scope", 403)
        return dict(service_id=service_id, request_id=scope["request_id"], operation=scope["operation"],
                    permission=scope["permission"], facts=deepcopy(scope["facts"]))

    def _route(self, route, service_id):
        _need(isinstance(route, dict) and {"service_id", "source", "binding_revision", "module_config_revision", "platform_config_digest"} <= set(route)
              and route.get("service_id") == service_id
              and route.get("source") in self._service(service_id)["supported_sources"]
              and type(route.get("binding_revision")) is int and route["binding_revision"] >= 1
              and (route.get("module_config_revision") is None and isinstance(route.get("platform_config_digest"), str)
                   and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", route["platform_config_digest"]) if route.get("source") == "platform" else
                   type(route.get("module_config_revision")) is int and route["module_config_revision"] >= 0
                   and route.get("platform_config_digest") is None),
              "ai_route_response", 502)
        return route

    async def _own_chat(self, service_id, messages, revision):
        with self._lock:
            entry = self._read()["services"].get(service_id)
            _need(entry and entry["revision"] == revision, "ai_config_revision_changed", 409)
            profile, secrets = entry["profile"], self._secrets()
            _need(profile["enabled"], "ai_service_disabled", 409)
        payload = await self._request(profile["base_url"] + "/chat/completions", dict(
            model=profile["model"], messages=_redact(messages, secrets), temperature=profile["temperature"],
            max_tokens=profile["max_tokens"], stream=False),
            {"Authorization": "Bearer " + profile["api_key"]} if profile.get("api_key") else {}, profile["timeout_seconds"], provider=True)
        try:
            choice, message = payload["choices"][0], payload["choices"][0]["message"]
            _need(isinstance(message, dict) and (message.get("content") is None or isinstance(message["content"], str))
                  and not message.get("tool_calls"), "ai_response", 502)
            result = dict(content=message.get("content") or "", tool_calls=[], model=payload.get("model") or profile["model"])
            if choice.get("finish_reason") in _FINISH:
                result["finish_reason"] = choice["finish_reason"]
            if isinstance(payload.get("usage"), dict):
                result["usage"] = {key: value for key, value in payload["usage"].items()
                                   if key in {"prompt_tokens", "completion_tokens", "total_tokens"} and type(value) is int and value >= 0}
            _need(self.get_config(service_id)["revision"] == revision, "ai_config_revision_changed", 409)
            return _redact(_result(result), secrets + self._secrets())
        except (KeyError, IndexError, TypeError, AttributeError):
            raise AIServiceError("ai_response", 502) from None

    async def chat_with_receipt(self, *, service_id, messages, scope=None):
        service = self._service(service_id)
        _messages(messages)
        if self._platform is None:
            _need("module" in service["supported_sources"], "ai_source_unsupported", 409)
            revision = self.get_config(service_id)["revision"]
            result = await self._own_chat(service_id, deepcopy(messages), revision)
            return {"result": result, "route": dict(service_id=service_id, source="module", binding_revision=None, module_config_revision=revision, platform_config_digest=None)}
        body = self._scope(scope, service_id)
        ticket = scope["ticket"]
        headers = {"X-Platform-Access": ticket}
        messages = _redact(deepcopy(messages), [ticket])
        route = self._route(await self._request(self._platform + "/api/v1/ai/resolve", body, headers, 15), service_id)
        selected = {**body, "binding_revision": route["binding_revision"], "module_config_revision": route["module_config_revision"], "platform_config_digest": route["platform_config_digest"]}
        if route["source"] == "platform":
            answer = await self._request(self._platform + "/api/v1/ai/chat", {**selected, "messages": _redact(messages, self._secrets())}, headers, 310)
            self._route(answer, service_id)
            _need(all(answer[key] == route[key] for key in ("source", "binding_revision", "module_config_revision", "platform_config_digest")), "ai_binding_revision_changed", 409)
            result = _result(answer.get("result"))
        else:
            result = await self._own_chat(service_id, deepcopy(messages), route["module_config_revision"])
        after = self._route(await self._request(self._platform + "/api/v1/ai/validate", selected, headers, 15), service_id)
        _need(all(after[key] == route[key] for key in ("source", "binding_revision", "module_config_revision", "platform_config_digest")), "ai_binding_revision_changed", 409)
        if route["source"] == "module":
            _need(self.get_config(service_id)["revision"] == route["module_config_revision"], "ai_config_revision_changed", 409)
        return {"result": _result(_redact(result, self._secrets() + [ticket])),
                "route": dict(service_id=service_id, source=route["source"], binding_revision=route["binding_revision"],
                              module_config_revision=route["module_config_revision"], platform_config_digest=route["platform_config_digest"])}

    async def chat(self, **options):
        return (await self.chat_with_receipt(**options))["result"]

    async def validate(self, *, service_id, route, scope=None):
        """Recheck immediately before a domain commit; no distributed atomicity."""
        self._service(service_id)
        if self._platform is None:
            _need(isinstance(route, dict) and route.get("service_id") == service_id and route.get("source") == "module"
                  and route.get("binding_revision") is None and route.get("platform_config_digest") is None and type(route.get("module_config_revision")) is int
                  and self.get_config(service_id)["revision"] == route["module_config_revision"], "ai_config_revision_changed", 409)
            return deepcopy(route)
        self._route(route, service_id)
        selected = {**self._scope(scope, service_id), "binding_revision": route["binding_revision"],
                    "module_config_revision": route["module_config_revision"], "platform_config_digest": route["platform_config_digest"]}
        after = self._route(await self._request(self._platform + "/api/v1/ai/validate", selected,
                                               {"X-Platform-Access": scope["ticket"]}, 15), service_id)
        _need(all(after[key] == route[key] for key in ("source", "binding_revision", "module_config_revision", "platform_config_digest")), "ai_binding_revision_changed", 409)
        if route["source"] == "module":
            _need(self.get_config(service_id)["revision"] == route["module_config_revision"], "ai_config_revision_changed", 409)
        return {key: after[key] for key in ("service_id", "source", "binding_revision", "module_config_revision", "platform_config_digest")}

    async def test_connection(self, service_id, expected_revision):
        self._service(service_id)
        revision = self.get_config(service_id)["revision"]
        _need(type(expected_revision) is int and expected_revision == revision, "ai_config_revision_changed", 409)
        started = time.monotonic()
        result = await self._own_chat(service_id, [{"role": "user", "content": "This is a synthetic connection test. Reply with CONNECTION_OK only."}], revision)
        return dict(ok=True, message="连接成功，已收到供应商响应。", latency_ms=round((time.monotonic() - started) * 1000),
                    model=result["model"], response_preview=result["content"][:240])

    async def control(self, service_id, method, action, body=None):
        """Only invoke after authenticating the service Bearer; never a public API."""
        if action == "config" and method == "GET":
            return self.get_config(service_id)
        if action == "config" and method == "PUT":
            _need(isinstance(body, dict) and set(body) == {"expected_revision", "profile"})
            return self.put_config(service_id, **body)
        if action == "test" and method == "POST":
            _need(isinstance(body, dict) and set(body) == {"expected_revision"})
            return await self.test_connection(service_id, body["expected_revision"])
        raise AIServiceError("ai_control_not_found", 404)
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/atoms.py

```python
"""Bind graph atoms to the real dispatch table without calling business code.

Run the kit's structural/host checks before deployment. This startup check has a
different purpose: a handshake may acknowledge only callable local bindings.
"""

from copy import deepcopy
from types import MappingProxyType


class AtomBindings:
    def __init__(self, graph, manifest, handlers):
        """Pin one definition per actual capability and reject missing callables."""
        module = manifest.get("module", manifest)
        if graph.get("graph_version") != "1.0" or (graph["module_id"], graph["module_version"]) != (
            module["id"],
            module["version"],
        ):
            raise ValueError("Atom graph identity differs from the running module")
        capabilities = {item["id"]: item for item in manifest["capabilities"]}
        backends = [atom for atom in graph["atoms"] if atom["kind"] == "backend"]
        bound = {atom["capability"]["id"]: atom for atom in backends}
        if (
            len(bound) != len(backends)
            or set(bound) != set(capabilities)
            or set(handlers) != set(capabilities)
        ):
            raise ValueError("Every capability requires one actual atomic binding")
        for identifier, atom in bound.items():
            if (
                not callable(handlers[identifier])
                or atom["capability"]["version"] != capabilities[identifier]["version"]
            ):
                raise ValueError("Atom handler or version is invalid")
        self._graph = deepcopy(graph)
        self._by_capability = deepcopy(bound)
        self.handlers = MappingProxyType(dict(handlers))

    @property
    def document(self):
        """Return a copy so callers cannot mutate accepted declarations."""
        return deepcopy(self._graph)

    def for_capability(self, identifier):
        """Resolve observation metadata; this lookup grants no execution rights."""
        return deepcopy(self._by_capability[identifier])
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/graph_testing.py

```python
"""Assertions over observed results. These helpers never execute business transitions."""


def assert_transition(graph, machine_id, before, event, after):
    """Compare independent before/after projections with a declared state edge.

    Call the real module handler between the two reads. Authorization, data
    effects and revision rules still require separate business assertions.
    """
    machines = [item for item in graph["state_machines"] if item["id"] == machine_id]
    if len(machines) != 1:
        raise AssertionError("Expected one declared state machine: " + machine_id)
    if before.get("object_ref") != after.get("object_ref"):
        raise AssertionError("Before and after refer to different business objects")
    edge = (before.get("state"), event, after.get("state"))
    if not any(
        (item["from"], item["event"], item["to"]) == edge for item in machines[0]["transitions"]
    ):
        raise AssertionError(f"Observed transition is absent from the graph: {edge!r}")


def assert_atom_result(graph, atom_id, inputs, output, validate, *, manifest=None):
    """Use the project's Schema validator on results returned by the real handler."""
    atoms = [item for item in graph["atoms"] if item["id"] == atom_id]
    if len(atoms) != 1:
        raise AssertionError("Expected one declared atom: " + atom_id)
    contract = atoms[0]
    if contract["kind"] == "backend":
        capabilities = (manifest or {}).get("capabilities", [])
        matching = [
            item
            for item in capabilities
            if item["id"] == contract["capability"]["id"]
            and item["version"] == contract["capability"]["version"]
        ]
        if len(matching) != 1:
            raise AssertionError("Backend atom requires its actual manifest capability")
        contract = matching[0]
    validate(contract["input_schema"], inputs)
    validate(contract["output_schema"], output)
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/module_kit.py

```python
"""Portable module-kit v1 helpers for native Python tools (standard library only).

This file is vendored as backend/_module_kit.py. It is not an authorization
engine: the injected platform access service makes every permission decision.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json


SDK_VERSION = "1.0.0"
MAX_INPUT_BYTES = 100_000
MAX_RESULT_BYTES = 256_000


class KitError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, status: int = 400):
        self.code, self.message, self.status = code, message, status
        super().__init__(message)


def _json_size(value, limit: int, *, output: bool = False):
    try:
        encoded = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False).encode("utf-8")
    except (TypeError, ValueError, UnicodeError, RecursionError):
        raise KitError(
            "tool_result_invalid" if output else "tool_input_invalid",
            "工具结果不是有效 JSON。" if output else "输入不是有效 JSON。",
            500 if output else 422,
        ) from None
    if len(encoded) > limit:
        raise KitError(
            "tool_result_too_large" if output else "tool_input_too_large",
            "工具结果超过大小限制。" if output else "输入超过大小限制。",
            500 if output else 413,
        )


def require_organization_access(ctx, permission: str) -> dict:
    """Authorize the caller's organization, never a scope supplied in payload.

    Only appropriate for an actor-scoped tool with no stored business object.
    Object tools must instead derive facts from their own trusted repository.
    """
    actor = getattr(ctx, "actor", None)
    if (
        not isinstance(actor, dict)
        or not isinstance(actor.get("id"), str)
        or not actor["id"].strip()
        or actor.get("enabled") is False
    ):
        raise KitError("tool_identity_required", "需要有效的平台身份。", 403)
    organization_id = actor.get("organization_id")
    if (
        not isinstance(organization_id, str)
        or not organization_id.strip()
        or organization_id == "*"
    ):
        raise KitError("tool_organization_required", "请先由平台管理员分配所属机构。", 403)
    access = getattr(ctx, "access", None)
    if not callable(getattr(access, "check", None)):
        raise KitError("tool_access_unavailable", "平台权限服务不可用。", 503)
    facts = {"organization_id": organization_id}
    try:
        decision = access.check(actor, permission, facts)
    except Exception:
        # Do not expose provider errors or attempt a legacy-role fallback.
        raise KitError("tool_access_unavailable", "平台权限检查失败，请稍后重试。", 503) from None
    if not isinstance(decision, dict) or decision.get("allowed") is not True:
        raise KitError("tool_forbidden", "未获得本机构的工具使用授权。", 403)
    return facts


def run_tool(ctx, *, permission: str, payload: dict, handler) -> dict:
    """Check current scope and call a pure function returning summary + data.

    Platform input/output Schema validation, idempotency, CSRF, lifecycle and
    invocation history remain platform responsibilities. This wrapper neither
    persists records nor commits the injected transaction.
    """
    require_organization_access(ctx, permission)
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise KitError("tool_input_invalid", "输入必须是 JSON 对象。", 422)
```
