```python
    _json_size(payload, MAX_INPUT_BYTES)
    try:
        result = handler(payload)
    except ValueError:
        raise KitError(
            "tool_input_invalid", "输入不符合工具要求，请检查字段与内容。", 422
        ) from None
    if (
        not isinstance(result, dict)
        or set(result) != {"summary", "data"}
        or not isinstance(result["summary"], str)
        or not 1 <= len(result["summary"]) <= 4000
        or not isinstance(result["data"], dict)
    ):
        raise KitError("tool_result_invalid", "工具结果不符合 summary/data 契约。", 500)
    _json_size(result, MAX_RESULT_BYTES, output=True)
    return result
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/observation.py

```python
"""Server-only observation queue; delivery cannot change business outcomes."""

from copy import deepcopy
import json
from queue import Queue, Full
import re
from threading import Thread
from urllib.parse import urlsplit
from urllib.request import Request, build_opener, HTTPRedirectHandler


class _NoRedirect(HTTPRedirectHandler):
    def redirect_request(self, *args, **kwargs):
        return None


class ObservationClient:
    def __init__(self, callback_url, graph, *, timeout=1, send=None):
        """Use administrator configuration for the destination, never request input."""
        url = urlsplit(callback_url)
        if (
            url.scheme not in {"http", "https"}
            or not url.netloc
            or url.username
            or url.password
            or url.query
            or url.fragment
            or url.path != "/api/v1/runtime-observations/events"
        ):
            raise ValueError("Invalid configured observation endpoint")
        if not 0 < timeout <= 2:
            raise ValueError("Observation timeout must be at most two seconds")
        self.url, self.graph, self.timeout = callback_url, deepcopy(graph), timeout
        self.send = send or self._send
        self.queue = Queue(maxsize=64)
        self.worker = Thread(target=self._deliver, daemon=True, name="duoflow-observations")
        self.worker.start()

    def _send(self, ticket, batch):
        request = Request(
            self.url,
            data=json.dumps(batch).encode(),
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "X-DuoFlow-Observation": ticket},
            method="POST",
        )
        with build_opener(_NoRedirect).open(request, timeout=self.timeout) as response:
            return response.status == 200

    def _deliver(self):
        """Bound retries; do not serialize credentials or report transport errors as business errors."""
        while True:
            ticket, batch, lost = self.queue.get()
            try:
                for _ in range(2):
                    try:
                        if self.send(ticket, batch):
                            break
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                else:
                    lost()
            finally:
                self.queue.task_done()

    def session(self, *, ticket, request_id, atom_id):
        """Create a call-local reporter; absent tickets produce no network traffic."""
        return ObservationSession(self, ticket, request_id, atom_id)

    def probe(self, challenge):
        """Actively confirm the platform callback using its one-time challenge."""
        if (
            not isinstance(challenge, dict)
            or set(challenge) != {"ticket", "nonce"}
            or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", str(challenge["ticket"]))
            or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{32}", str(challenge["nonce"]))
        ):
            return False
        url = self.url.rsplit("/", 1)[0] + "/probe"
        body = {"runtime_version": "1.0", "nonce": challenge["nonce"]}
        try:
            request = Request(
                url,
                data=json.dumps(body).encode(),
                headers={
                    "Content-Type": "application/json",
                    "X-DuoFlow-Observation": challenge["ticket"],
                },
                method="POST",
            )
            with build_opener(_NoRedirect).open(request, timeout=self.timeout) as response:
                raw = response.read(1025)
                if len(raw) > 1024:
                    return False
                result = json.loads(raw)
                return response.status == 200 and result == {
                    "accepted": True,
                    "nonce": challenge["nonce"],
                }
        except Exception:
            return False

    def flush(self):
        """Wait for the bounded queue during tests or controlled shutdown."""
        self.queue.join()


class ObservationSession:
    def __init__(self, client, ticket, request_id, atom_id):
        self.client, self.ticket, self.request_id, self.atom_id = (
            client,
            ticket,
            request_id,
            atom_id,
        )
        self.sequence, self.dropped = 0, 0

    def _lost(self):
        self.dropped = min(256, self.dropped + 1)

    def _emit(self, phase, state=None):
        if (
            not isinstance(self.ticket, str)
            or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", self.ticket)
            or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{32}", self.request_id)
        ):
            return
        if self.sequence >= 256:
            self._lost()
            return
        self.sequence += 1
        event = {
            "sequence": self.sequence,
            "atom_id": self.atom_id,
            "call_id": self.request_id,
            "parent_call_id": None,
            "phase": phase,
        }
        if state is not None:
            event["state"] = deepcopy(state)
        batch = {
            "runtime_version": "1.0",
            "request_id": self.request_id,
            "graph_version": self.client.graph["version"],
            "dropped": self.dropped,
            "events": [event],
        }
        try:
            self.client.queue.put_nowait((self.ticket, batch, self._lost))
        except Full:
            self._lost()

    def mark(self, phase):
        """Report the actual invocation boundary, without affecting dispatch."""
        if phase in {"started", "succeeded", "failed"}:
            self._emit(phase)

    def state(self, machine_id, projection):
        """Report a committed or freshly read projection, omitting business data."""
        if (
            not isinstance(projection, dict)
            or not projection.get("object_ref")
            or type(projection.get("revision")) is not int
            or not isinstance(projection.get("state"), str)
        ):
            return
        self._emit(
            "state",
            {
                "machine_id": machine_id,
                "value": projection["state"],
                "revision": projection["revision"],
                "object_ref": projection["object_ref"],
            },
        )
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/registration.py

```python
"""Portable bilateral registration v1 for an existing Python HTTP adapter.

The adapter authenticates the connection before calling confirm. Handlers must
be the actual dispatch map, not a second list of claimed function names. This
module performs no business calls, database access or user authorization grants.
"""

from copy import deepcopy
import re
from types import MappingProxyType

from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator


class RegistrationError(ValueError):
    """Map to HTTP 422 with fixed wording, never echo a received contract."""


class Registration:
    def __init__(
        self,
        manifest,
        document,
        handlers,
        workflow_handlers=None,
        *,
        graph=None,
        runtime_probe=None,
        required_platform_features=(),
        ai_manager=None,
    ):
        """Validate startup bindings and pin complete local declarations."""
        workflow_handlers = workflow_handlers or {}
        self._need(
            isinstance(required_platform_features, (list, tuple))
            and len(required_platform_features) <= 20
            and all(isinstance(item, str) and re.fullmatch(r"[a-z][a-z0-9.-]{0,79}", item)
                    for item in required_platform_features)
            and len(set(required_platform_features)) == len(required_platform_features)
        )
        self.required_platform_features = tuple(required_platform_features)
        self._need(
            document.get("registration_version") in {"1.0", "1.1"}
            and manifest["protocol_version"] in ("1.0", "1.1", "1.2")
        )
        self._need(
            (document["module_id"], document["module_version"])
            == (manifest["module"]["id"], manifest["module"]["version"])
        )
        self.ids = sorted(cap["id"] for cap in manifest["capabilities"])
        if "ai_services" in document:
            services = document["ai_services"]
            self._need(isinstance(services, list) and len(services) <= 16
                       and (not services or document["permissions"]["mode"] == "platform_access"))
            ai_ids = set()
            for service in services:
                self._need(
                    isinstance(service, dict)
                    and set(service) == {"id", "title", "description", "capability_ids", "supported_sources"}
                    and isinstance(service["id"], str)
                    and re.fullmatch(r"[a-z][a-z0-9_.-]{2,119}", service["id"])
                    and service["id"].startswith(manifest["module"]["id"] + ".")
                    and service["id"] not in ai_ids
                    and isinstance(service["title"], str) and 0 < len(service["title"]) <= 200
                    and isinstance(service["description"], str) and 0 < len(service["description"]) <= 2000
                    and isinstance(service["capability_ids"], list) and 0 < len(service["capability_ids"]) <= 64
                    and all(item in self.ids for item in service["capability_ids"])
                    and len(set(service["capability_ids"])) == len(service["capability_ids"])
                    and isinstance(service["supported_sources"], list) and service["supported_sources"]
                    and all(item in {"platform", "module"} for item in service["supported_sources"])
                    and len(set(service["supported_sources"])) == len(service["supported_sources"])
                )
                ai_ids.add(service["id"])
        self.ai_manager = ai_manager
        self._need(not document.get("ai_services") or ai_manager is not None
                   and ai_manager.services == document["ai_services"])
        self.modern = document["registration_version"] == "1.1"
        self.runtime_probe = runtime_probe
        if self.modern:
            from .atoms import AtomBindings

            self._need(graph == document["module_graph"] and callable(runtime_probe))
            AtomBindings(graph, manifest, handlers)
        self._need(sorted(handlers) == self.ids and all(callable(fn) for fn in handlers.values()))
        self._need(
            sorted(workflow_handlers) == document["workflow_operations"]
            and all(callable(fn) for fn in workflow_handlers.values())
        )
        for cap in manifest["capabilities"]:
            for field in ("input_schema", "output_schema"):
                Draft202012Validator.check_schema(cap[field])
        self._manifest, self._document = deepcopy(manifest), deepcopy(document)
        self.handlers = MappingProxyType(dict(handlers))
        self.workflow_handlers = MappingProxyType(dict(workflow_handlers))

    @property
    def manifest(self):
        """Expose a copy, keeping the contract checked at startup immutable."""
        return deepcopy(self._manifest)

    @property
    def document(self):
        """Return the local public declaration without exposing mutable storage."""
        return deepcopy(self._document)

    @staticmethod
    def _need(condition):
        if not condition:
            raise RegistrationError("注册确认未通过，请检查协议、声明与接口绑定。")

    def confirm(self, offer):
        """Actively compare the platform offer, then acknowledge its unique nonce."""
        self._need(not self.document.get("ai_services")
                   or self.ai_manager.platform_connected is True)
        self._need(
            isinstance(offer, dict)
            and set(offer)
            == (
                {
                    "registration_version",
                    "nonce",
                    "contract_digest",
                    "platform_features",
                    "manifest",
                    "registration",
                }
                | ({"runtime_probe"} if self.modern else set())
            )
        )
        self._need(offer["registration_version"] == self.document["registration_version"])
        for key, length in (("nonce", 32), ("contract_digest", 64)):
            self._need(
                isinstance(offer[key], str) and re.fullmatch("[0-9a-f]{%d}" % length, offer[key])
            )
        required = {
            "invoke-1.1" if self.manifest["protocol_version"] == "1.2" else "invoke-1.0",
            "shared-hosts-1.0",
            "registration-1.0",
        }
        required.update(self.required_platform_features)
        if self.document.get("ai_services"):
            required.add("ai-services-1.0")
        if self.document["permissions"]["mode"] == "platform_access":
            required.add("access-1.0")
        if self.modern:
            required.update(
                {
                    "registration-1.1",
                    "module-graph-1.0",
                    "flow-exports-1.0",
                    "runtime-observation-1.0",
                }
            )
        self._need(
            isinstance(offer["platform_features"], list)
            and all(isinstance(value, str) for value in offer["platform_features"])
        )
        self._need(required.issubset(offer["platform_features"]))
        self._need(offer["manifest"] == self.manifest and offer["registration"] == self.document)
        if self.modern:
            self._need(self.runtime_probe(offer["runtime_probe"]) is True)
        return {
            "registration_version": self.document["registration_version"],
            "nonce": offer["nonce"],
            "contract_digest": offer["contract_digest"],
            "accepted": True,
            "checks": {
                "platform_contract": True,
                "local_manifest": True,
                "local_registration": True,
                "handlers": True,
                "schemas": True,
                **({"atomic_bindings": True, "runtime_callback": True} if self.modern else {}),
            },
            "capability_ids": self.ids,
            "workflow_operations": self.document["workflow_operations"],
        }
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/remote_access.py

```python
"""Portable access v1 client for trusted Python remote services (Python 3.11+).

Validate the incoming service Bearer first, read facts from your repository,
then call ``decide`` with the original request ID, operation and access ticket.
This client neither authenticates browser input nor replaces a state machine,
business revision checks or idempotency. Tickets must not be saved or logged.
Only httpx and the standard library are required; no platform imports occur.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import hmac
import json
import math
import re
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

import httpx


_ENDPOINT = "/api/v1/access/remote-decisions"
_MAX_REQUEST = 16 * 1024
_MAX_TICKET = 12 * 1024
_MAX_RESPONSE = 4096
_IDENTIFIER = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,159}")
_FACT_KEY = re.compile(r"[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,79}")
# Return public reason codes only, never arbitrary text supplied by a server.
# New central deny reasons remain denied as access_decision_invalid until this
# portable client's allowlist is updated; they cannot expand authorization.
_DENIAL_REASONS = frozenset(
    {
        "permission_unknown",
        "identity_inactive",
        "module_disabled",
        "policy_revision_changed",
        "required_fact_missing",
        "separation_of_duties",
        "actor_relation_mismatch",
        "fact_condition_failed",
        "grant_scope_mismatch",
        "command_unmapped",
        "access_ticket_invalid",
        "access_ticket_expired",
        "access_ticket_request_mismatch",
        "access_ticket_operation_mismatch",
        "access_decision_contract",
        "access_authorization_changed",
        "access_bridge_binding",
        "access_bridge_unavailable",
        "access_ticket_permission_mismatch",
        "actor_disabled",
        "authorization_changed",
        "capability_missing",
        "forbidden",
    }
)


def _deny(reason):
    return {"allowed": False, "reason": reason, "policy_revision": 0}


def service_bearer_matches(authorization: str | None, expected_token: str) -> bool:
    """Compare the configured service secret in constant time; never default open."""
    if not isinstance(authorization, str) or not isinstance(expected_token, str):
        return False
    if not expected_token or any(ord(c) < 33 or ord(c) > 126 for c in expected_token):
        return False
    scheme, separator, token = authorization.partition(" ")
    if scheme.lower() != "bearer" or separator != " " or not token:
        return False
    if any(ord(c) < 33 or ord(c) > 126 for c in token):
        return False
    return hmac.compare_digest(token.encode("ascii"), expected_token.encode("ascii"))


def _valid_request(request_id, operation, permission, facts):
    if not all(
        isinstance(v, str) and _IDENTIFIER.fullmatch(v) for v in (request_id, operation, permission)
    ):
        return False
    if not isinstance(facts, dict) or len(facts) > 32:
        return False
    for key, value in facts.items():
        if not isinstance(key, str) or not _FACT_KEY.fullmatch(key):
            return False
        if isinstance(value, str):
            if not 1 <= len(value) <= 160:
                return False
        elif isinstance(value, list):
            if not 1 <= len(value) <= 20 or not all(
                isinstance(v, str) and 1 <= len(v) <= 160 for v in value
            ):
                return False
            if len(set(value)) != len(value):
                return False
        else:
            return False
    return True


def _unique_object(pairs):
    result = {}
    for key, value in pairs:
        if key in result:
            raise ValueError("Duplicate JSON field")
        result[key] = value
    return result


def _invalid_constant(_value):
    raise ValueError("Non-finite JSON number")


class RemoteAccessClient:
    """Fixed deployment callback, bounded I/O and fail-closed central decisions.

    ``transport`` is the standard httpx test/transport seam. Each call closes its
    own HTTP client and never stores request facts or tickets on this instance.
    Caller cancellation propagates; operational failures return a safe denial.
    """

    def __init__(self, callback_url: str, *, timeout_seconds: float = 4.0, transport=None):
        try:
            if not isinstance(callback_url, str) or any(
                ord(c) < 33 or ord(c) == 127 for c in callback_url
            ):
                raise ValueError
            endpoint = urlsplit(callback_url)
            if (
                endpoint.scheme not in {"http", "https"}
                or not endpoint.hostname
                or endpoint.username is not None
                or endpoint.password is not None
                or endpoint.path != _ENDPOINT
                or "?" in callback_url
                or "#" in callback_url
                or (endpoint.port is not None and not 1 <= endpoint.port <= 65535)
            ):
                raise ValueError
            # Validate the actual HTTP library's URL parser as well.
            parsed = httpx.URL(callback_url)
            if parsed.raw_path != _ENDPOINT.encode("ascii") or not parsed.host:
                raise ValueError
        except (TypeError, ValueError, httpx.InvalidURL):
            raise ValueError("Invalid configured access-decision endpoint") from None
        if (
            type(timeout_seconds) not in (int, float)
            or not math.isfinite(timeout_seconds)
            or not 0 < timeout_seconds <= 10
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Access decision timeout must be greater than zero and at most 10 seconds"
            )
        self._callback_url = parsed
        self._timeout_seconds = float(timeout_seconds)
        self._transport = transport

    async def decide(self, *, ticket, request_id, operation, permission, facts) -> dict:
        if (
            not isinstance(ticket, str)
            or not 0 < len(ticket) <= _MAX_TICKET
            or any(ord(c) < 33 or ord(c) > 126 for c in ticket)
        ):
            return _deny("access_ticket_invalid")
        if not _valid_request(request_id, operation, permission, facts):
            return _deny("access_decision_contract")
        try:
            body = json.dumps(
                {
                    "request_id": request_id,
                    "operation": operation,
                    "permission": permission,
                    "facts": facts,
                },
                ensure_ascii=False,
                allow_nan=False,
                separators=(",", ":"),
            ).encode("utf-8")
        except (TypeError, ValueError, UnicodeError, RecursionError):
            return _deny("access_decision_contract")
        if len(body) > _MAX_REQUEST:
            return _deny("access_decision_size")
        try:
            # A total deadline also bounds custom transports and slow trickles.
            async with asyncio.timeout(self._timeout_seconds):
                async with httpx.AsyncClient(
                    transport=self._transport,
                    trust_env=False,
                    follow_redirects=False,
                    timeout=self._timeout_seconds,
                ) as client:
                    async with client.stream(
                        "POST",
                        self._callback_url,
                        content=body,
                        headers={
                            "Content-Type": "application/json",
                            "Accept-Encoding": "identity",
                            "X-Platform-Access": ticket,
                        },
                    ) as response:
                        if response.status_code != 200:
                            return _deny("access_decision_unavailable")
                        # Reject compressed bodies to avoid decompression expansion.
                        if (
                            response.headers.get("content-encoding", "identity").lower()
                            != "identity"
                        ):
                            return _deny("access_decision_invalid")
                        length = response.headers.get("content-length")
                        if length is not None and (
                            not length.isdigit() or int(length) > _MAX_RESPONSE
                        ):
                            return _deny("access_decision_response_size")
                        received = bytearray()
                        async for chunk in response.aiter_bytes():
                            if len(received) + len(chunk) > _MAX_RESPONSE:
                                return _deny("access_decision_response_size")
                            received.extend(chunk)
            result = json.loads(
                received.decode("utf-8"),
                object_pairs_hook=_unique_object,
                parse_constant=_invalid_constant,
            )
        except (ValueError, UnicodeError, RecursionError):
            return _deny("access_decision_invalid")
        except (httpx.HTTPError, OSError, TimeoutError):
            return _deny("access_decision_unavailable")
        if (
            not isinstance(result, dict)
            or set(result) != {"allowed", "reason", "policy_revision"}
            or type(result["allowed"]) is not bool
            or not isinstance(result["reason"], str)
            or type(result["policy_revision"]) is not int
            or result["policy_revision"] < 0
        ):
            return _deny("access_decision_invalid")
        if result["allowed"]:
            if result["reason"] != "allowed" or result["policy_revision"] < 1:
                return _deny("access_decision_invalid")
        elif result["reason"] not in _DENIAL_REASONS:
            return _deny("access_decision_invalid")
        return {key: result[key] for key in ("allowed", "reason", "policy_revision")}
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/source_reads.py

```python
"""Portable server-only source read callback; no platform imports or credentials returned."""

import asyncio
import json
import re
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

import httpx


class SourceReadError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, code, status=503):
        super().__init__(code)
        self.code = code
        self.status = status if type(status) is int and 400 <= status <= 599 else 502


class SourceReadClient:
    def __init__(self, callback_url, *, timeout_seconds=8, transport=None):
        parsed = urlsplit(callback_url)
        if (parsed.scheme not in {"http", "https"} or not parsed.hostname
                or parsed.username or parsed.password or parsed.query or parsed.fragment
                or parsed.path != "/api/v1/access/source-reads"
                or type(timeout_seconds) not in (int, float) or not 0 < timeout_seconds <= 30):
            raise SourceReadError("source_read_configuration", 500)
        self.callback_url, self.timeout, self.transport = callback_url, timeout_seconds, transport

    async def read(self, *, ticket, request_id, operation, permission, facts, source, read_input=None):
        if not isinstance(ticket, str) or not 0 < len(ticket) <= 12288:
            raise SourceReadError("access_ticket_invalid", 401)
        payload = dict(source_read_version="1.0", request_id=request_id, operation=operation,
                       permission=permission, facts=facts, source=source)
        if read_input is not None:
            payload["read_input"] = read_input
        body = json.dumps(payload, allow_nan=False, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8")
        if len(body) > 16384:
            raise SourceReadError("source_read_size", 413)
        try:
            async with asyncio.timeout(self.timeout):
                async with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=self.transport, trust_env=False,
                                             follow_redirects=False, timeout=self.timeout) as client:
                    async with client.stream("POST", self.callback_url, content=body,
                            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "Accept-Encoding": "identity",
                                     "X-Platform-Access": ticket}) as response:
                        if response.headers.get("content-encoding", "identity").lower() != "identity":
                            raise SourceReadError("source_read_response", 502)
                        content = bytearray()
                        async for chunk in response.aiter_bytes():
                            if len(content) + len(chunk) > (1100000 if response.is_success else 4096):
                                raise SourceReadError("source_read_response_size", 502)
                            content.extend(chunk)
                        raw = content.decode("utf-8")
                        if ticket in raw:
                            raise SourceReadError("source_read_credential", 502)
                        result = json.loads(raw)
                        if not response.is_success:
                            code = result.get("error", {}).get("code") if isinstance(result, dict) else None
                            raise SourceReadError(code if isinstance(code, str) and
                                re.fullmatch(r"[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,79}", code) else "source_read_denied", response.status_code)
            projection = result.get("projection", {})
            if (set(result) != {"source_read_version", "source", "projection"}
                    or result["source_read_version"] != "1.0" or result["source"] != source
                    or projection.get("object_ref") != source["object_ref"]
                    or projection.get("revision") != source["revision"]
                    or not isinstance(projection.get("state"), str)
                    or not isinstance(projection.get("data"), dict)
                    or not isinstance(projection.get("allowed_actions"), list)):
                raise SourceReadError("source_read_response", 502)
            return result
        except SourceReadError:
            raise
        except (httpx.HTTPError, OSError, TimeoutError, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, AttributeError):
            raise SourceReadError("source_read_unavailable") from None
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/telemetry.py

```python
"""Optional bounded process reporter; direct to the independent monitor."""
import os
import math
import re
import socket
import threading
import time
from urllib.parse import urlsplit
import uuid

import httpx
import psutil


class TelemetryReporter:
    def __init__(self, *, url, token, instance_id=None, interval_seconds=15, metrics=None, transport=None):
        parsed = urlsplit(url)
        self.instance_id = instance_id or socket.gethostname()
        if (parsed.scheme not in {"http", "https"} or not parsed.hostname or parsed.username or parsed.password or parsed.query or parsed.fragment
                or not re.fullmatch(r"/api/v1/monitor/reports/[a-z][a-z0-9_.-]{1,79}", parsed.path)
                or (parsed.scheme == "http" and parsed.hostname not in {"127.0.0.1", "localhost", "::1"})
                or not isinstance(token, str) or not 24 <= len(token) <= 4096
                or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,63}", self.instance_id)
                or type(interval_seconds) not in {int,float} or not math.isfinite(interval_seconds)
                or not 1 <= interval_seconds <= 300):
            raise ValueError("Invalid telemetry configuration")
        self.url, self.token, self.interval = url, token, interval_seconds
        self.metrics, self.transport = metrics or (lambda: {}), transport
        self.boot_id, self.sequence = uuid.uuid4().hex, 0
        self.process = psutil.Process()
        self.process.cpu_percent()
        self.started = time.monotonic()
        self.stopped, self.lock = threading.Event(), threading.Lock()
        self.thread = None
        self.sent = self.failed = self.skipped = 0

    def report(self):
        if self.stopped.is_set() or not self.lock.acquire(blocking=False):
            self.skipped += 1
            return
        try:
            values = self.metrics()
            if not isinstance(values, dict) or set(values) - {"inflight_requests", "queue_depth", "requests_total", "errors_total"}:
                raise ValueError()
            if any(type(v) is not int or not 0 <= v <= 2**53 for v in values.values()):
                raise ValueError()
            self.sequence += 1
            payload = {"monitor_version": "1.0", "instance_id": self.instance_id,
                       "boot_id": self.boot_id, "sequence": self.sequence, "status": "ready",
                       "uptime_seconds": time.monotonic()-self.started,
                       "metrics": {"cpu_percent": min(100, self.process.cpu_percent()/max(1, os.cpu_count() or 1)),
                                   "rss_bytes": self.process.memory_info().rss, **values}}
            with httpx.Client(transport=self.transport, timeout=2, trust_env=False, follow_redirects=False) as client:
                with client.stream("POST", self.url, headers={"Authorization": "Bearer "+self.token}, json=payload) as response:
                    if response.is_success:
                        self.sent += 1
                    else:
                        self.failed += 1
        except Exception:
            self.failed += 1
        finally:
            self.lock.release()

    def start(self):
        if self.thread or self.stopped.is_set():
            return

        def loop():
            while not self.stopped.is_set():
                self.report()
                self.stopped.wait(self.interval)

        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=loop, daemon=True, name="duoflow-telemetry")
        self.thread.start()

    def stop(self):
        self.stopped.set()
        if self.thread:
            self.thread.join(timeout=3)
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/tests/test_ai_service.py

```python
"""Deterministic SDK transport tests. No real model or production identity used."""
import asyncio
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path

import httpx
import pytest


spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("portable_ai_service", Path(__file__).parents[1] / "ai_service.py")
sdk = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(sdk)
SERVICES = [dict(id="sample.ai", title="合成分析", description="SDK 回归", capability_ids=["sample.analyze"], supported_sources=["platform", "module"])]
PROFILE = dict(name="Synthetic provider", base_url="https://model.example/v1", model="synthetic-model",
               api_key="SYNTHETIC_PRIVATE_API_KEY_12345", enabled=True, temperature=0.2, max_tokens=32, timeout_seconds=2)
MESSAGES = [{"role": "user", "content": "合成输入"}]
SCOPE = dict(ticket="synthetic-access-ticket-value", request_id="a" * 32, operation="invoke", permission="sample.analyze", facts={"project_id": "P"})


def provider_result(content="synthetic answer"):
    return dict(model="synthetic-model", choices=[dict(message=dict(content=content), finish_reason="stop")], usage={"total_tokens": 8})


def configured(directory, handler=None, **kwargs):
    manager = sdk.AIServiceManager(services=SERVICES, data_dir=directory,
        transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler) if handler else None, **kwargs)
    manager.put_config("sample.ai", expected_revision=0, profile=PROFILE)
    return manager


def test_encrypted_restart_cas_clear_and_no_plaintext(tmp_path):
    manager = configured(tmp_path)
    config = manager.get_config("sample.ai")
    assert config["profile"]["api_key_configured"] is True
    assert PROFILE["api_key"] not in json.dumps(config)
    assert PROFILE["api_key"].encode() not in (tmp_path / "module-ai.config.enc").read_bytes()
    assert sdk.AIServiceManager(services=SERVICES, data_dir=tmp_path).get_config("sample.ai") == config
    with pytest.raises(sdk.AIServiceError, match="ai_config_revision_changed"):
        manager.put_config("sample.ai", expected_revision=0, profile=PROFILE)
    with pytest.raises(sdk.AIServiceError, match="ai_destination_changed"):
        manager.put_config("sample.ai", expected_revision=1, profile={"base_url": "https://other.example/v1"})
    with pytest.raises(sdk.AIServiceError, match="ai_configuration"):
        manager.put_config("sample.ai", expected_revision=1, profile={"name": PROFILE["api_key"]})
    assert manager.put_config("sample.ai", expected_revision=1, profile={"clear_api_key": True})["profile"]["api_key_configured"] is False


def test_standalone_own_api_redacts_outbound_and_inbound_key(tmp_path):
    calls = []
    def handler(request):
        calls.append(request)
        assert str(request.url) == PROFILE["base_url"] + "/chat/completions"
        assert request.headers["Authorization"] == "Bearer " + PROFILE["api_key"]
        assert PROFILE["api_key"].encode() not in request.content
        return httpx.Response(200, json=provider_result("answer " + PROFILE["api_key"]))
    manager = configured(tmp_path, handler)
    receipt = asyncio.run(manager.chat_with_receipt(service_id="sample.ai", messages=[{"role": "user", "content": PROFILE["api_key"]}]))
    assert receipt["result"]["content"] == "answer 【密钥已隐藏】"
    assert receipt["route"]["binding_revision"] is None
    asyncio.run(manager.validate(service_id="sample.ai", route=receipt["route"]))
    manager.put_config("sample.ai", expected_revision=1, profile={"model": "new-model"})
    with pytest.raises(sdk.AIServiceError, match="ai_config_revision_changed"):
        asyncio.run(manager.validate(service_id="sample.ai", route=receipt["route"]))
    assert len(calls) == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize("source", ["platform", "module"])
def test_connected_source_is_explicit_and_validated(tmp_path, source):
    calls = []
    route = dict(service_id="sample.ai", source=source, binding_revision=7, module_config_revision=1 if source == "module" else None,
                 platform_config_digest="f" * 64 if source == "platform" else None)
    def handler(request):
        calls.append(request.url.path)
        if request.url.host == "model.example":
            return httpx.Response(200, json=provider_result())
        assert request.headers["X-Platform-Access"] == SCOPE["ticket"]
        body = json.loads(request.content)
        assert body["facts"] == SCOPE["facts"]
        assert body["permission"] == SCOPE["permission"]
        answer = dict(route)
        if request.url.path.endswith("/chat"):
            answer["result"] = dict(content="platform answer", model="platform-model", tool_calls=[])
        return httpx.Response(200, json=answer)
    manager = configured(tmp_path, handler, platform_base_url="http://127.0.0.1:19000")
    receipt = asyncio.run(manager.chat_with_receipt(service_id="sample.ai", messages=MESSAGES, scope=SCOPE))
    assert receipt["route"] == route
    assert receipt["result"]["content"] == ("platform answer" if source == "platform" else "synthetic answer")
    assert calls == (["/api/v1/ai/resolve", "/api/v1/ai/chat", "/api/v1/ai/validate"] if source == "platform" else
                     ["/api/v1/ai/resolve", "/v1/chat/completions", "/api/v1/ai/validate"])
    asyncio.run(manager.validate(service_id="sample.ai", route=route, scope=SCOPE))
    assert calls[-1] == "/api/v1/ai/validate"


def test_missing_access_or_unavailable_platform_never_falls_back(tmp_path):
    calls = []
    def handler(request):
        calls.append(request)
        raise httpx.ConnectError(PROFILE["api_key"])
    manager = configured(tmp_path, handler, platform_base_url="http://localhost:19000")
    with pytest.raises(sdk.AIServiceError, match="ai_access_scope"):
        asyncio.run(manager.chat(service_id="sample.ai", messages=MESSAGES))
    assert not calls
    with pytest.raises(sdk.AIServiceError, match="ai_platform_unavailable"):
        asyncio.run(manager.chat(service_id="sample.ai", messages=MESSAGES, scope=SCOPE))
    assert len(calls) == 1


def test_in_flight_configuration_change_discards_answer(tmp_path):
    def handler(request):
        manager.put_config("sample.ai", expected_revision=1, profile={"model": "new-model"})
        return httpx.Response(200, json=provider_result())
    manager = configured(tmp_path, handler)
    with pytest.raises(sdk.AIServiceError, match="ai_config_revision_changed"):
        asyncio.run(manager.chat(service_id="sample.ai", messages=MESSAGES))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("rejection", ["binding", "grant"])
def test_post_call_revision_or_authorization_change_denies_result(tmp_path, rejection):
    route = dict(service_id="sample.ai", source="module", binding_revision=3, module_config_revision=1, platform_config_digest=None)
    def handler(request):
        if request.url.path.endswith("/resolve"):
            return httpx.Response(200, json=route)
        if request.url.path.endswith("/validate"):
            return httpx.Response(403, json={"error": {"message": PROFILE["api_key"]}}) if rejection == "grant" else httpx.Response(200, json={**route, "binding_revision": 4})
        return httpx.Response(200, json=provider_result())
    manager = configured(tmp_path, handler, platform_base_url="http://localhost:19000")
    with pytest.raises(sdk.AIServiceError, match="ai_platform_rejected" if rejection == "grant" else "ai_binding_revision_changed") as error:
        asyncio.run(manager.chat(service_id="sample.ai", messages=MESSAGES, scope=SCOPE))
    assert PROFILE["api_key"] not in str(error.value)


def test_route_pins_module_revision_before_network(tmp_path):
    calls = []
    def handler(request):
        calls.append(request)
        return httpx.Response(200, json=dict(service_id="sample.ai", source="module", binding_revision=1, module_config_revision=2, platform_config_digest=None))
    manager = configured(tmp_path, handler, platform_base_url="http://localhost:19000")
    with pytest.raises(sdk.AIServiceError, match="ai_config_revision_changed"):
        asyncio.run(manager.chat(service_id="sample.ai", messages=MESSAGES, scope=SCOPE))
    assert len(calls) == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize("status", [302, 401, 403, 429, 500])
def test_provider_error_is_safe_and_never_retried(tmp_path, status):
    calls = []
    def handler(request):
        calls.append(request)
        return httpx.Response(status, json={"error": {"message": PROFILE["api_key"]}})
    manager = configured(tmp_path, handler)
    with pytest.raises(sdk.AIServiceError) as error:
        asyncio.run(manager.chat(service_id="sample.ai", messages=MESSAGES))
    assert PROFILE["api_key"] not in str(error.value)
    assert len(calls) == 1


def test_control_requires_config_revision_for_real_provider_test(tmp_path):
    manager = configured(tmp_path, lambda _: httpx.Response(200, json=provider_result()))
    assert asyncio.run(manager.control("sample.ai", "POST", "test", {"expected_revision": 1}))["ok"] is True
    with pytest.raises(sdk.AIServiceError, match="ai_configuration"):
        asyncio.run(manager.control("sample.ai", "POST", "test", {}))
    with pytest.raises(sdk.AIServiceError, match="ai_control_not_found"):
        asyncio.run(manager.control("sample.ai", "POST", "config", {}))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("change", ["digest", "grant"])
def test_final_commit_guard_rechecks_platform_digest_and_authorization(tmp_path, change):
    changed, committed = False, False
    route = dict(service_id="sample.ai", source="platform", binding_revision=1, module_config_revision=None, platform_config_digest="a" * 64)
    def handler(request):
        if request.url.path.endswith("/chat"):
            assert json.loads(request.content)["platform_config_digest"] == route["platform_config_digest"]
            return httpx.Response(200, json={**route, "result": dict(content="answer", model="platform-model", tool_calls=[])})
        if changed and change == "grant":
            return httpx.Response(403, json={})
        return httpx.Response(200, json={**route, "platform_config_digest": "b" * 64} if changed else route)
    manager = configured(tmp_path, handler, platform_base_url="http://localhost:19000")
    receipt = asyncio.run(manager.chat_with_receipt(service_id="sample.ai", messages=MESSAGES, scope=SCOPE))
    changed = True
    async def commit():
        nonlocal committed
        await manager.validate(service_id="sample.ai", scope=SCOPE, route=receipt["route"])
        committed = True
    with pytest.raises(sdk.AIServiceError, match="ai_platform_rejected" if change == "grant" else "ai_binding_revision_changed"):
        asyncio.run(commit())
    assert committed is False


def test_text_bounds_reject_before_outbound_request(tmp_path):
    calls = []
    def handler(request):
        calls.append(request)
        return httpx.Response(200, json=provider_result())
    manager = configured(tmp_path, handler)
    for messages in [MESSAGES * 65, [{"role": "user", "content": "x" * 16001}], [{"role": "user", "content": "x", "tools": []}]]:
        with pytest.raises(sdk.AIServiceError, match="ai_request"):
            asyncio.run(manager.chat(service_id="sample.ai", messages=messages))
    assert not calls


def test_ai_registration_requires_feature_and_real_capability_binding(tmp_path):
    registration_spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("ai_test_registration", Path(__file__).parents[1] / "registration.py")
    registration = importlib.util.module_from_spec(registration_spec)
    registration_spec.loader.exec_module(registration)
    schema = {"type": "object"}
    manifest = dict(protocol_version="1.2", module=dict(id="sample", version="1.0.0"), capabilities=[dict(id="sample.analyze", input_schema=schema, output_schema=schema)])
    document = dict(registration_version="1.0", module_id="sample", module_version="1.0.0", workflow_operations=[], permissions=dict(mode="platform_access"), ai_services=SERVICES)
    manager = configured(tmp_path, platform_base_url="http://localhost:19000")
    registrar = registration.Registration(manifest, document, {"sample.analyze": lambda: None}, ai_manager=manager)
    offer = dict(registration_version="1.0", nonce="a" * 32, contract_digest="b" * 64, manifest=manifest, registration=document,
                 platform_features=["invoke-1.1", "shared-hosts-1.0", "registration-1.0", "access-1.0"])
    with pytest.raises(registration.RegistrationError):
        registrar.confirm(offer)
    offer["platform_features"].append("ai-services-1.0")
    assert registrar.confirm(offer)["accepted"] is True
    with pytest.raises(registration.RegistrationError):
        registration.Registration(manifest, {**document, "ai_services": [{**SERVICES[0], "capability_ids": ["sample.missing"]}]}, {"sample.analyze": lambda: None})
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/tests/test_graph_testing.py

```python
"""Contract negatives for observation-based assertions, separate from business tests."""

from pathlib import Path
from runpy import run_path

import pytest
from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator

HELPERS = run_path(str(Path(__file__).parents[1] / "graph_testing.py"))


def test_transition_rejects_wrong_object_and_undeclared_result():
    graph = {
        "state_machines": [
            {
                "id": "record",
                "transitions": [{"from": "draft", "event": "submit", "to": "submitted"}],
            }
        ]
    }
    before = {"object_ref": {"object_id": "one"}, "state": "draft"}
    after = {**before, "state": "submitted"}
    check = HELPERS["assert_transition"]
    check(graph, "record", before, "submit", after)
    for value in ({**after, "state": "approved"}, {**after, "object_ref": {"object_id": "two"}}):
        with pytest.raises(AssertionError):
            check(graph, "record", before, "submit", value)


def test_atom_result_checks_real_values_in_both_directions():
    graph = {
        "atoms": [
            {
                "id": "read",
                "kind": "frontend",
                "input_schema": {"type": "string"},
                "output_schema": {"type": "integer"},
            }
        ]
    }
    validate = lambda schema, value: Draft202012Validator(schema).validate(value)
    check = HELPERS["assert_atom_result"]
    check(graph, "read", "key", 2, validate)
    from jsonschema import ValidationError

    for inputs, output in ((2, 2), ("key", "wrong")):
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
            check(graph, "read", inputs, output, validate)


def test_backend_atom_resolves_the_matching_manifest_version():
    graph = {
        "atoms": [{"id": "read", "kind": "backend", "capability": {"id": "read", "version": "1"}}]
    }
    manifest = {
        "capabilities": [
            {
                "id": "read",
                "version": "1",
                "input_schema": {"type": "string"},
                "output_schema": {"type": "integer"},
            }
        ]
    }
    check = HELPERS["assert_atom_result"]
    validate = lambda schema, value: Draft202012Validator(schema).validate(value)
    check(graph, "read", "key", 2, validate, manifest=manifest)
    manifest["capabilities"][0]["version"] = "2"
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError):
        check(graph, "read", "key", 2, validate, manifest=manifest)
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/tests/test_remote_access.py

```python
"""Portable client boundaries; no platform installation or application imports."""

import asyncio
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path

import httpx
import pytest


SOURCE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "remote_access.py"
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("kit_portable_remote_access", SOURCE)
sdk = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(sdk)
RemoteAccessClient = sdk.RemoteAccessClient
service_bearer_matches = sdk.service_bearer_matches
ENDPOINT = "http://127.0.0.1/api/v1/access/remote-decisions"
SUCCESS = {"allowed": True, "reason": "allowed", "policy_revision": 2}
REQUEST = dict(
    ticket="synthetic-ticket",
    request_id="req:one",
    operation="objects.read",
    permission="sample.item.read",
    facts={"project_id": "P", "owner_id": "alice"},
)


def run(client, **changes):
    return asyncio.run(client.decide(**{**REQUEST, **changes}))


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "url",
    [
        "https://example.test/wrong",
        "ftp://example.test/api/v1/access/remote-decisions",
        "http:///api/v1/access/remote-decisions",
        "https://user:secret@example.test/api/v1/access/remote-decisions",
        "https://@example.test/api/v1/access/remote-decisions",
        ENDPOINT + "?q=1",
        ENDPOINT + "?",
        ENDPOINT + "#fragment",
        ENDPOINT + "#",
        ENDPOINT + "/",
        ENDPOINT + "\n",
        "http://127.0.0.1:0/api/v1/access/remote-decisions",
        "http://127.0.0.1:99999/api/v1/access/remote-decisions",
        "http://127.0.0.1/api/v1/access/%72emote-decisions",
        None,
    ],
)
def test_callback_rejects_nonfixed_or_credential_bearing_urls(url):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Invalid configured"):
        RemoteAccessClient(url)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("timeout", [0, -1, 10.1, True, None, "4", float("nan"), float("inf")])
def test_timeout_is_finite_bounded_and_not_boolean(timeout):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="timeout"):
        RemoteAccessClient(ENDPOINT, timeout_seconds=timeout)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "header,secret,expected",
    [
        ("Bearer synthetic-secret", "synthetic-secret", True),
        ("bEaReR synthetic-secret", "synthetic-secret", True),
        (None, "synthetic-secret", False),
        ("Bearer ", "", False),
        ("Bearer wrong", "synthetic-secret", False),
        ("Basic synthetic-secret", "synthetic-secret", False),
        ("Bearer  synthetic-secret", "synthetic-secret", False),
        ("Bearer synthetic-secret\n", "synthetic-secret", False),
        ("Bearer 合成", "合成", False),
        ("Bearer synthetic-secret", None, False),
    ],
)
def test_service_bearer_missing_or_malformed_never_defaults_open(header, secret, expected):
    assert service_bearer_matches(header, secret) is expected


def test_service_bearer_uses_constant_time_comparison(monkeypatch):
    calls = []
    original = sdk.hmac.compare_digest
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        sdk.hmac, "compare_digest", lambda a, b: (calls.append((a, b)), original(a, b))[1]
    )
    assert service_bearer_matches("Bearer synthetic-secret", "synthetic-secret")
    assert calls == [(b"synthetic-secret", b"synthetic-secret")]


def test_exact_body_ticket_header_and_environment_isolation(monkeypatch):
    observations = []
    client_options = []
    real_client = sdk.httpx.AsyncClient

    def construct(**kwargs):
        client_options.append(kwargs)
        return real_client(**kwargs)

    def callback(request):
        observations.append(request)
        return httpx.Response(200, json=SUCCESS)

    monkeypatch.setattr(sdk.httpx, "AsyncClient", construct)
    client = RemoteAccessClient(ENDPOINT, transport=httpx.MockTransport(callback))
    assert run(client) == SUCCESS
    request = observations[0]
    assert str(request.url) == ENDPOINT and request.method == "POST"
    assert request.headers["X-Platform-Access"] == REQUEST["ticket"]
    assert "authorization" not in request.headers
    assert json.loads(request.content) == {k: v for k, v in REQUEST.items() if k != "ticket"}
    assert REQUEST["ticket"].encode() not in request.content
    assert client_options[0]["trust_env"] is False
    assert client_options[0]["follow_redirects"] is False
    assert client_options[0]["timeout"] == 4.0
    assert not any("ticket" in name or "facts" in name for name in vars(client))


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "changes,reason",
    [
        ({"ticket": None}, "access_ticket_invalid"),
        ({"ticket": ""}, "access_ticket_invalid"),
        ({"ticket": "x" * (12 * 1024 + 1)}, "access_ticket_invalid"),
        ({"ticket": "x\r\nInjected: value"}, "access_ticket_invalid"),
        ({"ticket": "非ASCII"}, "access_ticket_invalid"),
        ({"request_id": "bad/id"}, "access_decision_contract"),
        ({"operation": "invoke\n"}, "access_decision_contract"),
        ({"permission": "a" * 161}, "access_decision_contract"),
        ({"facts": {"owner_id": None}}, "access_decision_contract"),
        ({"facts": {"owner_id": True}}, "access_decision_contract"),
        ({"facts": {"Owner": "alice"}}, "access_decision_contract"),
        ({"facts": {"teams": ["a", "a"]}}, "access_decision_contract"),
        ({"facts": {"teams": []}}, "access_decision_contract"),
        ({"facts": {"teams": [str(i) for i in range(21)]}}, "access_decision_contract"),
        ({"facts": {f"fact_{i}": "value" for i in range(33)}}, "access_decision_contract"),
        (
            {"facts": {f"fact_{i}": [str(j) + "合" * 150 for j in range(20)] for i in range(3)}},
            "access_decision_size",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_invalid_input_is_denied_before_transport(changes, reason):
    def callback(_request):
        pytest.fail("Invalid input must not reach the callback")

    result = run(RemoteAccessClient(ENDPOINT, transport=httpx.MockTransport(callback)), **changes)
    assert result == {"allowed": False, "reason": reason, "policy_revision": 0}


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "body",
    [
        {},
        [],
        {**SUCCESS, "actor": {"id": "private"}},
        {**SUCCESS, "allowed": 1},
        {**SUCCESS, "reason": "grant_scope_mismatch"},
        {**SUCCESS, "policy_revision": True},
        {**SUCCESS, "policy_revision": 0},
        {**SUCCESS, "policy_revision": -1},
        {**SUCCESS, "policy_revision": 1.5},
        {**SUCCESS, "reason": None},
        {"allowed": False, "reason": "private-provider-message", "policy_revision": 0},
        {"allowed": False, "reason": "allowed", "policy_revision": 1},
    ],
)
def test_invalid_response_fields_fail_closed(body):
    client = RemoteAccessClient(
        ENDPOINT, transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda _r: httpx.Response(200, json=body))
    )
    assert run(client) == {
        "allowed": False,
        "reason": "access_decision_invalid",
        "policy_revision": 0,
    }


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "raw",
    [
        b"not JSON private content",
        b"\xff",
        b'{"allowed":true,"reason":"allowed","policy_revision":NaN}',
        b'{"allowed":false,"allowed":true,"reason":"allowed","policy_revision":2}',
    ],
)
def test_bad_json_duplicate_keys_and_nonfinite_never_escape(raw):
    result = run(
        RemoteAccessClient(
            ENDPOINT, transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda _r: httpx.Response(200, content=raw))
        )
    )
    assert result == {"allowed": False, "reason": "access_decision_invalid", "policy_revision": 0}


def test_normal_denial_is_preserved():
    denial = {"allowed": False, "reason": "grant_scope_mismatch", "policy_revision": 3}
    client = RemoteAccessClient(
        ENDPOINT, transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda _r: httpx.Response(200, json=denial))
    )
    assert run(client) == denial


@pytest.mark.parametrize("status", [201, 302, 307, 401, 403, 429, 500])
def test_http_failure_or_redirect_never_follows_destination(status):
    calls = []

    def callback(request):
        calls.append(str(request.url))
        return httpx.Response(
            status,
            headers={"Location": "https://must-not-follow.invalid/secret"},
            content=b"private provider text",
        )

    result = run(RemoteAccessClient(ENDPOINT, transport=httpx.MockTransport(callback)))
    assert result == {
        "allowed": False,
        "reason": "access_decision_unavailable",
        "policy_revision": 0,
    }
    assert calls == [ENDPOINT]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "error", [httpx.ConnectError("private address"), httpx.ReadTimeout("private timeout")]
)
def test_network_failure_does_not_expose_exception(error):
    def callback(_request):
        raise error

    result = run(RemoteAccessClient(ENDPOINT, transport=httpx.MockTransport(callback)))
    assert result == {
        "allowed": False,
        "reason": "access_decision_unavailable",
        "policy_revision": 0,
    }


def test_total_deadline_also_applies_to_custom_transport():
    async def callback(_request):
        await asyncio.sleep(1)
        return httpx.Response(200, json=SUCCESS)

    result = run(
        RemoteAccessClient(ENDPOINT, timeout_seconds=0.01, transport=httpx.MockTransport(callback))
    )
    assert result["reason"] == "access_decision_unavailable" and result["allowed"] is False


class Chunks(httpx.AsyncByteStream):
    def __init__(self, chunks):
        self.chunks, self.read, self.closed = chunks, 0, False

    async def __aiter__(self):
        for chunk in self.chunks:
            self.read += 1
            yield chunk

    async def aclose(self):
        self.closed = True


def test_stream_stops_at_byte_limit_and_closes_response():
    chunks = Chunks([b" " * 2048, b" " * 2048, b" ", b"private remainder"])
    client = RemoteAccessClient(
        ENDPOINT, transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda _r: httpx.Response(200, stream=chunks))
    )
    assert run(client)["reason"] == "access_decision_response_size"
    assert chunks.read == 3 and chunks.closed


def test_exact_response_limit_is_accepted():
    body = json.dumps(SUCCESS).encode()
    chunks = Chunks([body, b" " * (4096 - len(body))])
    client = RemoteAccessClient(
        ENDPOINT, transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda _r: httpx.Response(200, stream=chunks))
    )
    assert run(client) == SUCCESS and chunks.closed


def test_announced_oversized_response_is_not_read():
    chunks = Chunks([b"private body"])
    client = RemoteAccessClient(
        ENDPOINT,
        transport=httpx.MockTransport(
            lambda _r: httpx.Response(200, headers={"Content-Length": "4097"}, stream=chunks)
        ),
    )
    assert run(client)["reason"] == "access_decision_response_size"
    assert chunks.read == 0 and chunks.closed


def test_compressed_response_is_rejected_without_reading():
    chunks = Chunks([b"private compressed bytes"])
    client = RemoteAccessClient(
        ENDPOINT,
        transport=httpx.MockTransport(
            lambda _r: httpx.Response(200, headers={"Content-Encoding": "gzip"}, stream=chunks)
        ),
    )
    assert run(client)["reason"] == "access_decision_invalid"
    assert chunks.read == 0 and chunks.closed
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/tests/test_source_read_client.py

```python
import asyncio
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path

import httpx
import pytest


spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("portable_source_reads", Path(__file__).parents[1] / "source_reads.py")
sdk = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(sdk)
SOURCE = {"module_id": "example.source", "object_ref": {"object_type": "example.source.snapshot", "object_id": "S1"}, "revision": 7}


def run(handler, **changes):
    client = sdk.SourceReadClient("http://127.0.0.1:8031/api/v1/access/source-reads", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
    return asyncio.run(client.read(ticket="synthetic-ticket", request_id="r1", operation="invoke",
        permission="consumer.use", facts={"project_id": "P"}, source=SOURCE, **changes))


def response(**changes):
    return {"source_read_version": "1.0", "source": SOURCE, "projection": {
        "object_ref": SOURCE["object_ref"], "revision": 7, "state": "published", "data": {"selected": "actual"}, "allowed_actions": []}, **changes}


def test_portable_reader_uses_fixed_callback_header_and_opaque_source():
    seen = []
    def handler(request):
        seen.append(request)
        return httpx.Response(200, json=response())
    result = run(handler, read_input={"fields": ["selected"]})
    assert result["projection"]["data"] == {"selected": "actual"}
    assert seen[0].headers["X-Platform-Access"] == "synthetic-ticket"
    assert "ticket" not in json.loads(seen[0].content)
    assert json.loads(seen[0].content)["read_input"] == {"fields": ["selected"]}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("url", ["https://host/wrong", "https://user:pass@host/api/v1/access/source-reads", "https://host/api/v1/access/source-reads?url=other"])
def test_portable_reader_rejects_credential_or_callback_override(url):
    with pytest.raises(sdk.SourceReadError, match="source_read_configuration"):
        sdk.SourceReadClient(url)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("payload,code", [
    (response(source={**SOURCE, "revision": 8}), "source_read_response"),
    (response(projection={"data": {"leak": "synthetic-ticket"}}), "source_read_credential"),
    ({"error": {"code": "source_revision_changed"}}, "source_revision_changed"),
])
def test_portable_reader_denies_mismatch_echo_and_public_error(payload, code):
    status = 409 if "error" in payload else 200
    with pytest.raises(sdk.SourceReadError, match=code):
        run(lambda request: httpx.Response(status, json=payload))


def test_portable_reader_bounds_response_and_refuses_redirect():
    with pytest.raises(sdk.SourceReadError, match="source_read_response_size"):
        run(lambda request: httpx.Response(200, content=b" " * 1100001))
    with pytest.raises(sdk.SourceReadError):
        run(lambda request: httpx.Response(302, headers={"location": "https://other.invalid"}))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("status", [403, 409, 422, 503])
def test_source_failure_preserves_safe_http_status_without_private_message(status):
    with pytest.raises(sdk.SourceReadError) as failure:
        run(lambda request: httpx.Response(status, json={"error": {
            "code": "source_fixture_error", "message": "private fixture detail"}}))
    assert failure.value.code == "source_fixture_error"
    assert failure.value.status == status
    assert str(failure.value) == "source_fixture_error"


def test_consumer_can_require_optional_feature_before_registration_acceptance():
    registration_spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("portable_registration", Path(__file__).parents[1] / "registration.py")
    registration_sdk = importlib.util.module_from_spec(registration_spec)
    registration_spec.loader.exec_module(registration_sdk)
    manifest = {"protocol_version": "1.2", "module": {"id": "example.consumer", "version": "1.0.0"}, "capabilities": []}
    document = {"registration_version": "1.0", "module_id": "example.consumer", "module_version": "1.0.0", "workflow_operations": [], "permissions": {"mode": "capability_roles"}}
    features = ["source-reads-1.0"]
    registrar = registration_sdk.Registration(manifest, document, {}, required_platform_features=features)
    features.clear()
    offer = dict(registration_version="1.0", nonce="a" * 32, contract_digest="b" * 64,
                 manifest=manifest, registration=document,
                 platform_features=["invoke-1.1", "shared-hosts-1.0", "registration-1.0"])
    with pytest.raises(registration_sdk.RegistrationError):
        registrar.confirm(offer)
    offer["platform_features"].append("source-reads-1.0")
    assert registrar.confirm(offer)["accepted"]
```

### templates/module-kit/sdk/python/tests/test_telemetry.py

```python
import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path

import httpx
import pytest

spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('portable_telemetry',Path(__file__).parents[1]/'telemetry.py')
module=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(module)
CONFIG={'url':'http://127.0.0.1:8032/api/v1/monitor/reports/demo-service','token':'synthetic-telemetry-token-123456789','instance_id':'one'}


def test_real_process_sample_and_no_business_payload_or_secret_in_body():
    requests=[]
    transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda request:(requests.append(request) or httpx.Response(200,json={})))
    reporter=module.TelemetryReporter(**CONFIG,metrics=lambda:{'requests_total':2,'inflight_requests':0},transport=transport)
    reporter.report();reporter.stop()
    import json
    body=json.loads(requests[0].content)
    assert body['metrics']['rss_bytes']>0 and 0<=body['metrics']['cpu_percent']<=100
    assert body['metrics']['inflight_requests']==0 and 'queue_depth' not in body['metrics']
    assert CONFIG['token'].encode() not in requests[0].content and reporter.sent==1


def test_error_isolation_no_automatic_retry_and_counter_whitelist():
    calls=[]
    def fail(request):
        calls.append(request);raise httpx.ReadTimeout('PRIVATE')
    reporter=module.TelemetryReporter(**CONFIG,transport=httpx.MockTransport(fail))
    reporter.report();assert reporter.failed==1 and len(calls)==1
    reporter.metrics=lambda:{'private':'SECRET'};reporter.report();reporter.stop()
    assert reporter.failed==2 and len(calls)==1


@pytest.mark.parametrize('change',[{'interval_seconds':True},{'interval_seconds':float('nan')},
    {'token':'short'},{'url':CONFIG['url']+'?key=SECRET'},{'url':'http://example.org/api/v1/monitor/reports/demo-service'}])
def test_invalid_config(change):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):module.TelemetryReporter(**{**CONFIG,**change})
```



## 通用架构、状态机与开发调试台

业务事实以模块返回的 projection.state 为准，个人流程 cursor 和任务 Run.status 分开处理。workflow 可以声明 state_machine；完整 Schema 随契约附录导出。它描述业务状态、转移及 step_id/action_id 引用，校验器检查重复、起点、端点与动作关系。guard_description 仅供说明，权限和状态守卫仍由模块后端执行；不得以浏览器改状态或模型文字代替业务事务。无状态机的历史流程显示未声明，不用导航步骤冒充状态。

模块开发完成后使用 /#devtools 检查 capability / interaction / workflow 的定义、引用版本、前端安装情况与可用性。JSON 校验不安装、不执行源码、不访问网络引用；在线交互 state_schema 正则不执行，含该类规则时到离线受控校验。调试台仅平台管理员可用，且只观察本人流程和当前有权业务对象；每2秒轮询、暂停/手动刷新、图与正式 FlowHost 并排。观察历史最多100条，仅当前对象的状态元数据，不含正文与草稿，也不是完整业务事件审计。源码已安装与浏览器构建已加载分别核实。

浏览器 presence 心跳只核对当前身份、本人实例、中央授权指纹、组件定义和个人修订，不读取远程业务对象或列表，不采用 Run 回执、不推进个人步骤。主动调试读取继续重新授权并获取正式投影；presence 新鲜不能作为业务状态或源权限有效的证据。

组合运行图的入口 activity 来自组合步骤状态，入口完成应标为步骤已完成；原子节点的调用状态来自实际调用/观察证据，两者不能共用“调用成功”的含义。步骤可以通过读取已完成业务对象而完成，图中仍须独立显示缺少调用记录，不能用蓝色入口补造运行见证。

优先用 framework-lab 中立模块演练：创建→提交→退回→修改→重提→完成，页面/对话共用步骤组件和对象。它是本人调试样例，不是允许自审的真实审批规则。Node workflow-node 模板也从共享flow状态声明执行转移；其他模块可用自有业务状态引擎，仍返回统一投影。

架构与迁移入口见 docs/通用框架架构与代码地图.md、docs/模块开发与调试说明书.md、docs/框架迁移与AI交接指南.md。scripts/export_framework.py 从正向源码清单导出全新通用包，仅安装 framework-lab，重建提示词与说明书，不复制业务数据/凭据/远程连接/行业演示账号/依赖/构建物/Git历史。新包仍需安装锁定依赖、构建并实际验收；导出摘要不是运行验收。
# 模块接入验收同步

远程能力宿主使用 registration 1.0 双向验收。组件和流程本身仍由受控安装与生产构建加载；模块应在 host_requirements 声明实际需要的编号和版本，公开对象接口与真实分派表一致。主框架逐项校验声明、身份拒绝、健康、无效请求拒绝、模块主动确认和平台安装依赖，全部通过才能发布。权限附件先注册，实际用户授权另外管理。Schema 与两侧架构见同批 contracts/registration/v1、docs/主框架说明.md 和 docs/模块框架说明.md；业务组合、通用字段授权和业务正确性不由接入报告证明。
# 跨模块来源读取补充

共享组件只选择稳定来源引用与版本，不收集或保存源授权票据。服务端可以使用 `source-reads-1.0`，由平台在当前目标票据下重新检查目标和已登记源对象的权限。源读取失败、版本变化或撤权时保留明确失败，界面位置与聊天分支不替代正式来源授权。准确协议见随包 `contracts/access/v1/source-reads.md`。
